# V1.2 第六批代码交付：操作生命周期与提交资格

编写日期：2026-10-28。状态：本批手动实施代码与分层验证方案已集中交付；业务、配置和测试尚未由本次修改或执行，不能视为实现完成或生产验收通过。

本文仅提供手动实施代码。本次只写入 Markdown，不创建下文业务、配置和测试文件，不执行安装、pytest、Redis、Provider、容器或最终验证命令。第五批同样只是当前可见交付文档，不假设你已实施。前端及 Live2D 功能优化继续排除。

## 1. 依赖与保证

先实施并验收第五批，包括批准载荷、Bearer 固定主体、私有下载、真实生成失败与 GIF 补充，再实施本批。复用现有 RedisStateStore 的连接和官方 saver，不新建队列或数据库。

本批的原子范围是“一个主体的一份有界执行账本”，Redis 每次只有一个 key，天然同槽；不是整个 Redis、Mem0、对象存储与 Provider 的分布式事务。外部写入先进入不可见暂存区，当前资格检查和可见引用发布在同一账本 CAS 内完成。外部已经收到的请求、已经下载/播放的内容不可撤回。

核心字段：可信主体、规范 session、operation、payload 摘要、root epoch、generation、单调 fence、deadline、Memory 撤销版本、人格/配置/策略摘要。`request_id` 只作传输关联；不替代幂等键。generation/fence/deadline 由服务端签发，客户端不能自行分配或声明允许写记忆；Voice 提交的 `expected_generation` 仅用于比较服务端上次返回的代际，不允许客户端提升代际。

下列数字是保守默认约束，不是性能测量结果：一轮最多 120 秒、24 次已计数的外部调用，幂等结果保留 24 小时，每主体最多 256 个操作/128 个会话/1000 条可见记忆引用，单账本最多 4 MiB。达到上限拒绝，不无限积压。第九批增加有证据的压缩/清理策略。

## 2. 新增 `aiagent/state/execution_ledger.py`

文件功能：把并发准入、幂等、撤销和可见引用放到可复用的原子边界。业务变化只在 Python 纯函数里计算，Redis 用原始 JSON 精确 CAS；CAS 失败有限重读，不在回调里执行 HTTP、写文件或调用模型。

```python
from __future__ import annotations

import copy
import hashlib
import json
import secrets
import threading
import time
from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import UTC, datetime
from typing import Any, Callable, TypeVar

from aiagent.state.shared_state import (
    SharedStateConflictError,
    SharedStateOwnershipError,
    SharedStateUnavailableError,
)

T = TypeVar("T")
TERMINAL = {"completed", "failed", "cancelled", "unknown"}
MAX_STATE_BYTES = 4 * 1024 * 1024
RETENTION_MS = 86_400_000


class ExecutionRejected(SharedStateConflictError):
    pass


class ExecutionExpired(ExecutionRejected):
    pass


class ExecutionBudgetExceeded(ExecutionRejected):
    pass


def canonical(value: Any) -> str:
    return json.dumps(value, ensure_ascii=False, sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":"), allow_nan=False)


def digest(value: Any) -> str:
    return hashlib.sha256(canonical(value).encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()


def checked_name(value: str) -> str:
    if not isinstance(value, str) or not value or len(value) > 256:
        raise ValueError("execution_invalid_identifier")
    if any(ord(char) < 32 for char in value):
        raise ValueError("execution_invalid_identifier")
    return value


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class Capability:
    user_id: str
    session_id: str
    operation_id: str
    epoch: str
    generation: int
    fence: int
    memory_revision: int
    deadline_ms: int


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class Admission:
    created: bool
    capability: Capability
    status: str
    result: dict[str, Any] | None


class LocalAtomic:
    """Single-process development only; never a Redis failure fallback."""

    def __init__(self, clock: Callable[[], int] | None = None) -> None:
        self._clock = clock or (lambda: time.time_ns() // 1_000_000)
        self._lock = threading.RLock()
        self._values: dict[str, str] = {}

    def read(self, key: str) -> tuple[str, int]:
        with self._lock:
            return self._values.get(key, ""), self._clock()

    def cas(self, key: str, expected: str, value: str, deadline_ms: int = 0) -> bool:
        with self._lock:
            if self._values.get(key, "") != expected:
                return False
            if deadline_ms and self._clock() >= deadline_ms:
                raise ExecutionExpired("execution_deadline_exceeded")
            self._values[key] = value
            return True


_READ = """
local now = redis.call('TIME')
return {redis.call('GET', KEYS[1]) or '', now[1], now[2]}
"""

_CAS = """
if (redis.call('GET', KEYS[1]) or '') ~= ARGV[1] then return 0 end
local t = redis.call('TIME')
local now = tonumber(t[1]) * 1000 + math.floor(tonumber(t[2]) / 1000)
local deadline = tonumber(ARGV[3])
if deadline > 0 and now >= deadline then return -1 end
redis.call('SET', KEYS[1], ARGV[2])
return 1
"""


class RedisAtomic:
    def __init__(self, redis_client: Any) -> None:
        self.redis = redis_client

    def read(self, key: str) -> tuple[str, int]:
        try:
            value, seconds, micros = self.redis.eval(_READ, 1, key)
            if isinstance(value, bytes):
                value = value.decode("utf-8")
            return str(value), int(seconds) * 1000 + int(micros) // 1000
        except Exception as exc:
            raise SharedStateUnavailableError("execution_backend_unavailable") from exc

    def cas(self, key: str, expected: str, value: str, deadline_ms: int = 0) -> bool:
        try:
            result = int(self.redis.eval(_CAS, 1, key, expected, value, deadline_ms))
        except Exception as exc:
            # A timeout may have happened after SET; never report success or rerun an external call.
            raise SharedStateUnavailableError("execution_commit_outcome_unknown") from exc
        if result == -1:
            raise ExecutionExpired("execution_deadline_exceeded")
        return result == 1


class ExecutionLedger:
    def __init__(self, atomic: LocalAtomic | RedisAtomic, prefix: str = "aiagent:v1") -> None:
        if not prefix or "{" in prefix or "}" in prefix:
            raise ValueError("execution_invalid_prefix")
        self.atomic = atomic
        self.prefix = prefix.rstrip(":")

    def key(self, user_id: str) -> str:
        scope = hashlib.sha256(checked_name(user_id).encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()
        return f"{self.prefix}:v12:execution:{{{scope}}}"

    @staticmethod
    def _decode(raw: str, user_id: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
        try:
            if len(raw.encode("utf-8")) > MAX_STATE_BYTES:
                raise ValueError()
            root = json.loads(raw)
            if (not isinstance(root, dict) or root.get("schema") != 1
                    or root.get("owner") != user_id
                    or not isinstance(root.get("epoch"), str) or not root["epoch"]
                    or type(root.get("fence")) is not int
                    or type(root.get("memory_revision")) is not int
                    or type(root.get("memory_enabled")) is not bool
                    or any(not isinstance(root.get(key), dict)
                           for key in ("sessions", "operations", "memory", "objects", "calls",
                                       "memory_staging", "memory_gc", "memory_preference"))):
                raise ValueError()
            if (root["fence"] < 0 or root["memory_revision"] < 0
                    or len(root["memory"]) > 1000 or len(root["memory_staging"]) > 128
                    or len(root["memory_gc"]) > 2048 or len(root["calls"]) > 64):
                raise ValueError()
            for logical_id, reference in root["memory"].items():
                checked_name(logical_id)
                ExecutionLedger._checked_memory_reference(reference)
            from aiagent.state.voice_execution import validate_calls

            validate_calls(root)
            return root
        except (ValueError, TypeError, KeyError, UnicodeError, RecursionError, ExecutionRejected):
            # Corrupt data is not the same as an absent first-use key.
            raise SharedStateUnavailableError("execution_state_invalid") from None

    @staticmethod
    def _new(user_id: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            "schema": 1, "owner": user_id, "epoch": secrets.token_hex(16),
            "fence": 0, "memory_revision": 0, "memory_enabled": False,
            "sessions": {}, "operations": {}, "memory": {}, "objects": {},
            "memory_staging": {}, "memory_gc": {}, "memory_preference": {}, "calls": {},
        }

    def transaction(
        self,
        user_id: str,
        mutate: Callable[[dict[str, Any], int], T],
        *,
        deadline_ms: int = 0,
    ) -> T:
        key = self.key(user_id)
        for _ in range(32):
            before, now = self.atomic.read(key)
            root = self._decode(before, user_id) if before else self._new(user_id)
            if root.get("schema") != 1 or root.get("owner") != user_id:
                raise SharedStateOwnershipError("execution_owner_or_schema_mismatch")
            result = mutate(root, now)
            after = canonical(root)
            if len(after.encode("utf-8")) > MAX_STATE_BYTES:
                raise ExecutionRejected("execution_state_quota_exceeded")
            if self.atomic.cas(key, before, after, deadline_ms):
                return result
        raise ExecutionRejected("execution_contention_retry_exhausted")

    def snapshot(self, user_id: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
        raw, _ = self.atomic.read(self.key(user_id))
        if not raw:
            return {}
        root = self._decode(raw, user_id)
        if root.get("owner") != user_id or root.get("schema") != 1:
            raise SharedStateOwnershipError("execution_owner_or_schema_mismatch")
        return root

    @staticmethod
    def _cap(root: dict[str, Any], record: dict[str, Any]) -> Capability:
        return Capability(
            root["owner"], record["session_id"], record["operation_id"], root["epoch"],
            record["generation"], record["fence"], record["memory_revision"], record["deadline_ms"],
        )

    @staticmethod
    def _assert(
        root: dict[str, Any], cap: Capability, now: int, *, memory: bool = False,
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        if root["owner"] != cap.user_id or root["epoch"] != cap.epoch:
            raise ExecutionRejected("execution_epoch_or_owner_changed")
        if memory and (
            not root["memory_enabled"] or root["memory_revision"] != cap.memory_revision
        ):
            raise ExecutionRejected("execution_memory_revoked")
        record = root["operations"].get(cap.operation_id)
        session = root["sessions"].get(cap.session_id)
        allowed = {"running", "completed"} if memory else {"running"}
        if (
            record is None or session is None or record["status"] not in allowed
            or record["session_id"] != cap.session_id
            or record["generation"] != cap.generation or session["generation"] != cap.generation
            or record["fence"] != cap.fence or record["deadline_ms"] != cap.deadline_ms
            or record["memory_revision"] != cap.memory_revision
        ):
            raise ExecutionRejected("execution_stale_capability")
        if now >= cap.deadline_ms:
            raise ExecutionExpired("execution_deadline_exceeded")
        from aiagent.state.voice_execution import assert_call_capability

        assert_call_capability(root, record, cap, now)
        return record

    def begin(
        self, *, user_id: str, session_id: str, idempotency_key: str,
        payload_digest: str, kind: str = "user", deadline_seconds: int = 120,
        persona_digest: str = "", config_digest: str = "", policy_version: str = "v12-06",
        voice_call_id: str = "",
        expected_voice_generation: int | None = None,
    ) -> Admission:
        checked_name(session_id)
        checked_name(idempotency_key)
        if kind not in {"user", "presence"} or not 1 <= deadline_seconds <= 120:
            raise ValueError("execution_invalid_admission")
        if len(payload_digest) != 64 or any(c not in "0123456789abcdef" for c in payload_digest):
            raise ValueError("execution_invalid_payload_digest")
        operation_id = digest([session_id, idempotency_key])

        def mutate(root, now):
            from aiagent.state.voice_execution import (
                admit_call_turn, check_call_session, settle_call_operation,
            )

            check_call_session(root, now, user_id, session_id, voice_call_id)
            records = root["operations"]
            old = records.get(operation_id)
            if old is not None:
                if old["payload_digest"] != payload_digest or old["kind"] != kind:
                    raise ExecutionRejected("execution_idempotency_payload_conflict")
                if old.get("voice_call_id", "") != voice_call_id:
                    raise ExecutionRejected("execution_idempotency_payload_conflict")
                if now >= old["retained_until_ms"]:
                    raise ExecutionRejected("execution_idempotency_window_closed")
                if old["status"] in {"accepted", "running"} and now >= old["deadline_ms"]:
                    old["status"] = "unknown"
                    session = root["sessions"].get(session_id)
                    if session and session["active"] == operation_id:
                        session["active"] = ""
                    settle_call_operation(root, old, now, "unknown")
                return Admission(False, self._cap(root, old), old["status"], copy.deepcopy(old.get("result")))
            if len(records) >= 256:
                raise ExecutionRejected("execution_operation_quota_exceeded")
            sessions = root["sessions"]
            if session_id not in sessions:
                if len(sessions) >= 128:
                    raise ExecutionRejected("execution_session_quota_exceeded")
                sessions[session_id] = {
                    "generation": 0, "active": "", "closed": False,
                    "checkpoint": "", "history": [], "output_id": "",
                }
            session = sessions[session_id]
            if session["closed"]:
                raise ExecutionRejected("execution_session_closed")
            active = records.get(session["active"])
            if active and active["status"] in {"accepted", "running"}:
                if now >= active["deadline_ms"]:
                    active["status"] = "unknown"
                    settle_call_operation(root, active, now, "unknown")
                elif kind == "user" and active["kind"] == "presence":
                    active["status"] = "cancelled"
                else:
                    raise ExecutionRejected("execution_session_busy")
            root["fence"] += 1
            session["generation"] += 1
            if root["fence"] >= 2**52 or session["generation"] >= 2**52:
                raise ExecutionRejected("execution_counter_exhausted")
            record = {
                "operation_id": operation_id, "session_id": session_id,
                "payload_digest": payload_digest, "kind": kind, "status": "accepted",
                "generation": session["generation"], "fence": root["fence"],
                "memory_revision": root["memory_revision"], "created_ms": now,
                "deadline_ms": now + deadline_seconds * 1000,
                "retained_until_ms": now + RETENTION_MS,
                "persona_digest": persona_digest, "config_digest": config_digest,
                "policy_version": policy_version, "calls": {}, "total_calls": 0,
            }
            records[operation_id] = record
            session["active"] = operation_id
            if voice_call_id:
                admit_call_turn(root, now, voice_call_id, record, expected_voice_generation)
            return Admission(True, self._cap(root, record), "accepted", None)

        return self.transaction(user_id, mutate)

    def start(self, cap: Capability) -> None:
        def mutate(root, now):
            record = root["operations"].get(cap.operation_id)
            if (
                record is None or root["epoch"] != cap.epoch
                or self._cap(root, record) != cap or record["status"] != "accepted"
                or root["sessions"][cap.session_id]["generation"] != cap.generation
            ):
                raise ExecutionRejected("execution_cannot_start")
            if now >= cap.deadline_ms:
                raise ExecutionExpired("execution_deadline_exceeded")
            record["status"] = "running"
        self.transaction(cap.user_id, mutate, deadline_ms=cap.deadline_ms)

    def check(self, cap: Capability, *, memory: bool = False) -> int:
        raw, now = self.atomic.read(self.key(cap.user_id))
        if not raw:
            raise ExecutionRejected("execution_state_missing")
        self._assert(self._decode(raw, cap.user_id), cap, now, memory=memory)
        return cap.deadline_ms - now

    def reserve(self, cap: Capability, stage: str, *, max_calls: int, total_limit: int = 24) -> int:
        checked_name(stage)
        if max_calls < 1 or total_limit < 1:
            raise ValueError("execution_invalid_budget")

        def mutate(root, now):
            record = self._assert(root, cap, now, memory=stage == "embedding")
            calls = record["calls"]
            if record["total_calls"] >= total_limit or calls.get(stage, 0) >= max_calls:
                raise ExecutionBudgetExceeded("execution_call_budget_exceeded")
            calls[stage] = calls.get(stage, 0) + 1
            record["total_calls"] += 1
            return cap.deadline_ms - now
        return self.transaction(cap.user_id, mutate, deadline_ms=cap.deadline_ms)

    def finish(
        self, cap: Capability, *, result: dict[str, Any], checkpoint: str = "",
        history_pair: tuple[str, str] | None = None, objects: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] | None = None,
        memories: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] | None = None,
        voice_result: dict[str, Any] | None = None,
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        if len(canonical(result).encode("utf-8")) > 128 * 1024:
            raise ExecutionRejected("execution_result_too_large")
        if objects and len(objects) > 32:
            raise ExecutionRejected("execution_too_many_output_objects")

        def mutate(root, now):
            record = self._assert(root, cap, now)
            session = root["sessions"][cap.session_id]
            from aiagent.state.voice_execution import publish_call_result

            committed_result = publish_call_result(
                root, record, now, result, voice_result, objects or {},
            )
            if len(canonical(committed_result).encode("utf-8")) > 128 * 1024:
                raise ExecutionRejected("execution_result_too_large")
            if memories:
                self._assert(root, cap, now, memory=True)
                if len(memories) > 8:
                    raise ExecutionRejected("execution_too_many_staged_memories")
                for logical_id, reference in memories.items():
                    self._publish_new_memory(root, cap, logical_id, reference)
            if objects:
                if len(root["objects"]) + len(objects) > 2048:
                    raise ExecutionRejected("execution_object_quota_exceeded")
                for resource_id, descriptor in objects.items():
                    if resource_id in root["objects"]:
                        raise ExecutionRejected("execution_object_id_conflict")
                    root["objects"][resource_id] = {
                        **copy.deepcopy(descriptor), "operation_id": cap.operation_id,
                        "epoch": cap.epoch, "created_ms": now,
                    }
            record["result"] = copy.deepcopy(committed_result)
            record["status"] = "completed"
            record["finished_ms"] = now
            session["checkpoint"] = checkpoint
            session["output_id"] = str(committed_result.get("output_id", ""))
            if history_pair:
                if any(len(text) > 16_384 for text in history_pair):
                    raise ExecutionRejected("execution_history_item_too_large")
                session["history"] = (session["history"] + [list(history_pair)])[-6:]
            session["active"] = ""
            return copy.deepcopy(committed_result)
        return self.transaction(cap.user_id, mutate, deadline_ms=cap.deadline_ms)

    def terminal(self, cap: Capability, status: str) -> None:
        if status not in {"failed", "unknown"}:
            raise ValueError("execution_invalid_terminal_status")

        def mutate(root, now):
            if root["epoch"] != cap.epoch:
                raise ExecutionRejected("execution_epoch_changed")
            record = root["operations"].get(cap.operation_id)
            if record is None or self._cap(root, record) != cap:
                raise ExecutionRejected("execution_stale_capability")
            if record["status"] in TERMINAL:
                return
            record["status"] = status
            record["finished_ms"] = now
            session = root["sessions"][cap.session_id]
            if session["active"] == cap.operation_id:
                session["active"] = ""
            from aiagent.state.voice_execution import settle_call_operation

            settle_call_operation(root, record, now, status)
        self.transaction(cap.user_id, mutate)

    def cancel_session(self, user_id: str, session_id: str, *, close: bool = False, clear: bool = False) -> None:
        def mutate(root, now):
            session = root["sessions"].get(session_id)
            if session is None:
                return
            record = root["operations"].get(session["active"])
            if record and record["status"] in {"accepted", "running"}:
                record["status"] = "cancelled"
                record["finished_ms"] = now
            session["generation"] += 1
            session["active"] = ""
            session["closed"] = bool(close or session["closed"])
            if clear:
                session["checkpoint"] = ""
                session["history"] = []
                session["output_id"] = ""
                removed_ops = {
                    operation_id for operation_id, item in root["operations"].items()
                    if item["session_id"] == session_id
                }
                for operation_id in removed_ops:
                    root["operations"][operation_id].pop("result", None)
                root["objects"] = {
                    key: item for key, item in root["objects"].items()
                    if item.get("operation_id") not in removed_ops
                }
            from aiagent.state.voice_execution import revoke_call_session

            revoke_call_session(root, session_id, now, close=close, clear=clear)
        self.transaction(user_id, mutate)

    def cancel_operation(self, user_id: str, operation_id: str) -> str:
        def mutate(root, now):
            record = root["operations"].get(operation_id)
            if record is None:
                return "not_found"
            if record["status"] in TERMINAL:
                return record["status"]
            session = root["sessions"][record["session_id"]]
            record["status"] = "cancelled"
            record["finished_ms"] = now
            # A delayed cancel for operation A must not cancel successor B.
            if (session["active"] == operation_id
                    and session["generation"] == record["generation"]):
                session["generation"] += 1
                session["active"] = ""
                from aiagent.state.voice_execution import settle_call_operation

                settle_call_operation(root, record, now, "cancelled")
            return "cancelled"
        return self.transaction(user_id, mutate)

    def visible_object(self, user_id: str, kind: str, resource_id: str) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
        if kind not in {"audio", "output"}:
            raise ValueError("execution_invalid_object_kind")
        raw, now = self.atomic.read(self.key(user_id))
        if not raw:
            return None
        root = self._decode(raw, user_id)
        item = root["objects"].get(f"{kind}:{resource_id}")
        if not isinstance(item, dict):
            return None
        operation = root["operations"].get(item.get("operation_id", ""))
        if (operation is None or operation["status"] != "completed"
                or item.get("epoch") != root["epoch"] or item.get("kind") != kind
                or type(item.get("expires_ms")) is not int or now >= item["expires_ms"]):
            return None
        return copy.deepcopy(item)

    @staticmethod
    def _revoke_memory_readers(root: dict[str, Any], now: int) -> None:
        root["memory_revision"] += 1
        if root["memory_revision"] >= 2**52:
            raise ExecutionRejected("execution_counter_exhausted")
        for session in root["sessions"].values():
            active = root["operations"].get(session["active"])
            if active and active["status"] in {"accepted", "running"}:
                active["status"] = "cancelled"
                active["finished_ms"] = now
                session["generation"] += 1
                session["active"] = ""
            session.update(checkpoint="", history=[], output_id="")
        for operation in root["operations"].values():
            operation.pop("result", None)
        root["objects"] = {}
        from aiagent.state.voice_execution import revoke_all_call_outputs

        revoke_all_call_outputs(root, now)

    @staticmethod
    def _retire_memory(root: dict[str, Any], ref: dict[str, Any], now: int) -> None:
        key = digest([ref["agent_id"], ref["physical_id"], ref["visibility_tag"]])
        if key not in root["memory_gc"] and len(root["memory_gc"]) >= 2048:
            raise ExecutionRejected("execution_memory_gc_quota_exceeded")
        root["memory_gc"][key] = {
            "agent_id": ref["agent_id"], "physical_id": ref["physical_id"],
            "visibility_tag": ref["visibility_tag"], "retired_ms": now,
        }

    def memory_control(
        self, user_id: str, *, enabled: bool | None = None,
        delete_ids: list[str] | None = None, clear_agent: str | None = None,
        agent_id: str = "yzl", preference_source: str = "user",
        expected_versions: dict[str, str] | None = None,
    ) -> list[str]:
        checked_name(agent_id)
        if enabled is not None and type(enabled) is not bool:
            raise ValueError("execution_invalid_memory_preference")
        if preference_source not in {"user", "migration"}:
            raise ValueError("execution_invalid_preference_source")

        def mutate(root, now):
            self._expect_memory_versions(root, agent_id, expected_versions or {})
            if enabled is not None:
                root["memory_enabled"] = enabled
                root["memory_preference"] = {"source": preference_source, "updated_ms": now}
            removed = []
            for logical_id, ref in list(root["memory"].items()):
                if ref["agent_id"] != agent_id:
                    continue
                if ((delete_ids is not None and logical_id in delete_ids)
                        or (clear_agent is not None and ref["agent_id"] == clear_agent)):
                    self._retire_memory(root, ref, now)
                    removed.append(ref["physical_id"])
                    del root["memory"][logical_id]
            self._revoke_memory_readers(root, now)
            return removed
        return self.transaction(user_id, mutate)

    def memory_view(self, user_id: str, agent_id: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
        checked_name(agent_id)
        root = self.snapshot(user_id)
        return {
            "epoch": root.get("epoch", ""), "revision": root.get("memory_revision", 0),
            "enabled": root.get("memory_enabled", False),
            "refs": {key: copy.deepcopy(ref) for key, ref in root.get("memory", {}).items()
                     if ref["agent_id"] == agent_id},
        }

    def delete_memory_layer(self, user_id: str, *, agent_id: str, layer: str) -> list[str]:
        checked_name(agent_id)
        if layer not in {"profile", "preference", "episode", "boundary", "other"}:
            raise ValueError("execution_invalid_memory_layer")

        def mutate(root, now):
            deleted = []
            for logical_id, ref in list(root["memory"].items()):
                if (ref["agent_id"] == agent_id
                        and ref["metadata"].get("memory_layer", "other") == layer):
                    self._retire_memory(root, ref, now)
                    del root["memory"][logical_id]
                    deleted.append(logical_id)
            self._revoke_memory_readers(root, now)
            return deleted
        return self.transaction(user_id, mutate)

    def assert_memory_view(self, user_id: str, agent_id: str, expected: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
        if digest(self.memory_view(user_id, agent_id)) != digest(expected):
            raise ExecutionRejected("execution_memory_view_changed")

    def visible_memory(self, user_id: str, agent_id: str) -> dict[str, dict[str, Any]]:
        view = self.memory_view(user_id, agent_id)
        return view["refs"] if view["enabled"] else {}

    def register_memory_stage(self, cap: Capability, *, agent_id: str, tag: str) -> None:
        checked_name(agent_id)
        if len(tag) != 64 or any(char not in "0123456789abcdef" for char in tag):
            raise ValueError("execution_invalid_visibility_tag")

        def mutate(root, now):
            self._assert(root, cap, now, memory=True)
            if tag in root["memory_staging"] or len(root["memory_staging"]) >= 128:
                raise ExecutionRejected("execution_memory_staging_quota_or_collision")
            root["memory_staging"][tag] = {
                "operation_id": cap.operation_id, "agent_id": agent_id, "created_ms": now,
            }
        self.transaction(cap.user_id, mutate, deadline_ms=cap.deadline_ms)

    @staticmethod
    def _checked_memory_reference(reference: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
        fields = {"physical_id", "agent_id", "visibility_tag", "content_digest", "metadata"}
        if not isinstance(reference, dict) or set(reference) != fields:
            raise ExecutionRejected("execution_invalid_memory_reference")
        for key in ("physical_id", "agent_id"):
            checked_name(reference[key])
        for key in ("visibility_tag", "content_digest"):
            value = reference[key]
            if (not isinstance(value, str) or len(value) != 64
                    or any(char not in "0123456789abcdef" for char in value)):
                raise ExecutionRejected("execution_invalid_memory_digest")
        metadata = reference["metadata"]
        allowed = {
            "approval_version", "approved_digest", "category", "importance", "memory_layer",
            "source", "session_ref", "turn_ref", "created_at", "updated_at", "pinned", "pinned_at",
        }
        if (not isinstance(metadata, dict) or set(metadata) - allowed
                or len(canonical(metadata).encode("utf-8")) > 4096
                or any(type(value) not in {str, bool} for value in metadata.values())):
            raise ExecutionRejected("execution_invalid_memory_metadata")
        if "pinned" in metadata and type(metadata["pinned"]) is not bool:
            raise ExecutionRejected("execution_invalid_memory_metadata")
        return copy.deepcopy(reference)

    def _publish_new_memory(self, root, cap, logical_id, reference) -> None:
        checked_name(logical_id)
        ref = self._checked_memory_reference(reference)
        stage = root["memory_staging"].get(ref["visibility_tag"])
        if (stage is None or stage["operation_id"] != cap.operation_id
                or stage["agent_id"] != ref["agent_id"]):
            raise ExecutionRejected("execution_unregistered_memory_stage")
        if logical_id in root["memory"] or len(root["memory"]) >= 1000:
            raise ExecutionRejected("execution_memory_id_or_quota_conflict")
        root["memory"][logical_id] = ref
        del root["memory_staging"][ref["visibility_tag"]]

    def publish_memory(self, cap: Capability, *, logical_id: str, reference: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
        def mutate(root, now):
            operation = self._assert(root, cap, now, memory=True)
            if operation["status"] != "completed":
                raise ExecutionRejected("execution_reply_not_committed")
            self._publish_new_memory(root, cap, logical_id, reference)
        self.transaction(cap.user_id, mutate, deadline_ms=cap.deadline_ms)

    @staticmethod
    def _expect_memory_versions(root, agent_id, expected: dict[str, str]) -> None:
        for logical_id, version in expected.items():
            ref = root["memory"].get(logical_id)
            if ref is None or ref["agent_id"] != agent_id or digest(ref) != version:
                raise ExecutionRejected("execution_memory_edit_conflict")

    def replace_memories(
        self, cap: Capability, *, agent_id: str, expected_versions: dict[str, str],
        primary_id: str, reference: dict[str, Any],
    ) -> None:
        if not expected_versions or len(expected_versions) > 32 or primary_id not in expected_versions:
            raise ValueError("execution_invalid_memory_replacement")

        def mutate(root, now):
            operation = self._assert(root, cap, now, memory=True)
            if operation["status"] != "running":
                raise ExecutionRejected("execution_memory_edit_already_finished")
            self._expect_memory_versions(root, agent_id, expected_versions)
            ref = self._checked_memory_reference(reference)
            stage = root["memory_staging"].get(ref["visibility_tag"])
            if (ref["agent_id"] != agent_id or stage is None
                    or stage["agent_id"] != agent_id or stage["operation_id"] != cap.operation_id):
                raise ExecutionRejected("execution_unregistered_memory_stage")
            for logical_id in expected_versions:
                self._retire_memory(root, root["memory"][logical_id], now)
                del root["memory"][logical_id]
            root["memory"][primary_id] = ref
            del root["memory_staging"][ref["visibility_tag"]]
            # Result publication, source removal and revocation are one CAS.
            self._revoke_memory_readers(root, now)
            operation.update(status="completed", finished_ms=now,
                             result={"memory_id": primary_id, "status": "updated"})
        self.transaction(cap.user_id, mutate, deadline_ms=cap.deadline_ms)

    def pin_memory(
        self, user_id: str, *, agent_id: str, logical_id: str, pinned: bool, expected_version: str,
    ) -> None:
        if type(pinned) is not bool:
            raise ValueError("execution_invalid_pin")

        def mutate(root, now):
            self._expect_memory_versions(root, agent_id, {logical_id: expected_version})
            ref = root["memory"][logical_id]
            ref["metadata"]["pinned"] = pinned
            ref["metadata"]["pinned_at"] = datetime.fromtimestamp(now / 1000, UTC).isoformat() if pinned else ""
            self._revoke_memory_readers(root, now)
        self.transaction(user_id, mutate)

    def operation(self, user_id: str, operation_id: str) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
        record = self.snapshot(user_id).get("operations", {}).get(operation_id)
        return copy.deepcopy(record) if record else None
```

### 2.1 为什么不是“先检查再写”

`check()` 只用于尽早停止耗费，不提供提交原子性。真正提交必须调用 `finish()` 或 `publish_memory()`，资格比较与发布引用均在同一次 CAS 内。Redis Lua 在写入时再次检查 deadline，避免 Python 算完到 Redis 提交之间过期仍通过。

`start()` 只能执行一次；同键第二次准入仅返回已有记录，不再次 start，不再次调用 Provider。`accepted` 是已占位而非已运行；崩溃后不自动接管 unknown，避免供应商已执行但响应丢失造成重复调用。第九批只允许对可证明未执行或具备幂等合同的任务恢复。

`epoch` 在账本首次创建时生成。Redis 丢失/误删账本后，新 root 不会接受旧资格；同时旧 Memory 物理数据没有可见引用，默认不认领。根账本不设置自动 TTL，不让撤销版本因到期归零；第九批负责操作子记录清理，不能用整 key expire 替代。

`memory_revision` 是主体级保守屏障：删除任意一条、分层删除、禁用/重新启用都会撤销此前所有在途操作及其 Memory 写入，已有其它可见记忆保持。为避免已删除事实通过旧摘要/缓存重返上下文，还清空该主体的会话可见 checkpoint/history、成功重放正文及音频索引；物理对象与孤立 checkpoint 交第九批清理，已经下载/播放的内容无法追回。此处选择较强隐私保证，代价是删除单条也会重置该主体短期上下文及旧音频链接，必须在迁移说明中告知用户。

开关是主体级，而不是假装逐 agent 独立开关；若以后要逐 agent 细分，应升级协议，不在调用者之间解释出两种语义。新账本默认 **禁用** 长期记忆，直到显式迁移既有选择或用户重新启用；不能根据旧 Settings 的默认 true 猜测用户已经同意。旧 JSON 偏好仍须给出迁移代码和共享替代实现，未接好前禁止生产开放。本项是有意的隐私迁移收紧，不是静默吞掉旧用户选择。

幂等记录过保留窗时**显式拒绝复用旧键**，不偷偷当作新调用。清理之后相同键是否允许再次执行由第九批的墓碑/保留策略明确规定。当前 quota 满即拒绝，因此不会无限增长，但需要后续清理才能持续服务。

`snapshot()` 是读取视图；外部依赖读取结束后必须重读/比对可见引用，不能拿旧 snapshot 永久证明仍可见。账本不存未批准 Prompt、Provider 密钥或原始 SDK 错误；成功结果和短期历史本身仍是用户数据，受私有存储和保留期约束。

## 3. 新增 `aiagent/state/execution_context.py`

文件功能：在一个 Python 调用栈及 `asyncio.to_thread` 之间传播当前资格与剩余预算；后台 ThreadPool 必须显式拷贝 context。它不是全局可变的“当前用户”，也不从模型/客户端 metadata 取资格。

```python
from __future__ import annotations

import time
from collections.abc import Callable, Generator
from contextlib import contextmanager
from contextvars import ContextVar
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import Any, TypeVar

from aiagent.state.execution_ledger import (
    Capability,
    ExecutionExpired,
    ExecutionLedger,
    ExecutionRejected,
)
from aiagent.state.shared_state import SharedStateError

T = TypeVar("T")

# 每个槽位是实际一次外部调用的上限，不是允许 SDK 内部再自动重试。
CALL_LIMITS = {
    "state": 1, "planner": 1, "llm": 1, "summary": 1,
    "memory_policy": 1, "vision": 1, "asr": 1, "tts": 8,
    "embedding": 8, "rag_embedding": 2, "rerank": 1, "judge": 2,
}


@dataclass
class ExecutionFrame:
    ledger: ExecutionLedger
    capability: Capability
    monotonic_deadline: float
    checkpoint: str = ""
    history_pair: tuple[str, str] | None = None
    persona_config: dict[str, Any] | None = None
    staged_objects: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = field(default_factory=dict)
    staged_memories: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = field(default_factory=dict)
    voice_result: dict[str, Any] | None = None
    memory_only: bool = False

    def remaining(self, *, memory: bool = False) -> float:
        if self.memory_only and not memory:
            raise ExecutionRejected("execution_background_memory_only")
        shared_ms = self.ledger.check(self.capability, memory=memory)
        seconds = min(shared_ms / 1000, self.monotonic_deadline - time.monotonic())
        if seconds <= 0:
            raise ExecutionExpired("execution_deadline_exceeded")
        return seconds


CURRENT: ContextVar[ExecutionFrame | None] = ContextVar("aiagent_execution", default=None)


def current(*, required: bool = False) -> ExecutionFrame | None:
    frame = CURRENT.get()
    if required and frame is None:
        raise ExecutionRejected("execution_context_required")
    return frame


@contextmanager
def bind(
    ledger: ExecutionLedger, cap: Capability, *, memory_only: bool = False,
) -> Generator[ExecutionFrame, None, None]:
    remaining_ms = ledger.check(cap, memory=memory_only)
    frame = ExecutionFrame(ledger, cap, time.monotonic() + remaining_ms / 1000,
                           memory_only=memory_only)
    token = CURRENT.set(frame)
    try:
        yield frame
    finally:
        CURRENT.reset(token)


def call(stage: str, fn: Callable[[float], T], *, per_call_seconds: float) -> T:
    frame = current(required=True)
    assert frame is not None
    if stage not in CALL_LIMITS or per_call_seconds <= 0:
        raise ValueError("execution_invalid_call_stage_or_timeout")
    memory = stage == "embedding"
    frame.remaining(memory=memory)
    granted_ms = frame.ledger.reserve(
        frame.capability, stage, max_calls=CALL_LIMITS[stage],
    )
    timeout = min(per_call_seconds, granted_ms / 1000, frame.monotonic_deadline - time.monotonic())
    if timeout <= 0:
        raise ExecutionExpired("execution_deadline_exceeded")
    try:
        result = fn(timeout)
    except BaseException:
        # 本层不重试；实际是否已调用外部服务不确定，由上层标记 unknown。
        raise
    frame.remaining(memory=memory)
    return result


def raise_if_execution_error(exc: BaseException) -> None:
    if isinstance(exc, SharedStateError):
        raise exc
```

### 3.1 预算的真实边界

`call()` 每次实际外呼前原子占用额度；异常同样消耗额度，不退回。将上限写进 dict 但不接入真实外呼没有效果，因此后面的接线是必需交付，不是可选增强。隐藏摘要调用、TTS 每段、SDK 重试和嵌入调用都必须计数，不能只包一层 Runtime 就宣称限制了真实调用次数。

共享 deadline 使用 Redis TIME；本进程额外用 monotonic 防止本机时钟回拨延长等待。不同机器的 monotonic 值不存进 Redis，也不互相比较。httpx 的 timeout 多为分阶段/空闲超时，不等于绝对整轮墙钟硬终止；迟到调用仍可能在后台计算，返回后的可见性必须由原子提交守住。

当前 frame 内的暂存 dict 只由一个顺序执行链写入。线程池子任务必须传入不可变 cap 并重新读取剩余时间，不能并发修改父 frame。总 deadline 不允许后台任务无限延长，可能导致下一用户回合到来时旧 Memory 写入被丢弃，这是明确选择。

## 4. 新增 `tests/unit/test_execution_ledger.py`

文件功能：对同一状态机验证正常、冲突、取消、过期、旧 fence、Memory 删除/禁用和 CAS 竞态。全部本地、合成数据，不导入完整 Runtime，不代表整链集成通过。

```python
from __future__ import annotations

import threading
from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor
from dataclasses import replace

import pytest

from aiagent.state.execution_context import bind, call
from aiagent.state.execution_ledger import (
    ExecutionBudgetExceeded,
    ExecutionExpired,
    ExecutionLedger,
    ExecutionRejected,
    LocalAtomic,
    digest,
)


@pytest.fixture
def ledger():
    clock = [1_000_000]
    store = ExecutionLedger(LocalAtomic(lambda: clock[0]))
    # Explicit test consent; the production first-use default remains disabled.
    store.memory_control("u1", enabled=True)
    return store, clock


def admit(store, key="request-1", text="hello", user="u1", session="s1", kind="user"):
    return store.begin(
        user_id=user, session_id=session, idempotency_key=key,
        payload_digest=digest({"text": text}), kind=kind,
    )


def running(store, **kwargs):
    cap = admit(store, **kwargs).capability
    store.start(cap)
    return cap


def reference(physical_id="p1", agent="yzl"):
    return {"physical_id": physical_id, "agent_id": agent,
            "visibility_tag": digest(["tag", physical_id]),
            "content_digest": digest(["content", physical_id]), "metadata": {}}


def registered(store, cap, physical_id="p1", agent="yzl"):
    ref = reference(physical_id, agent)
    store.register_memory_stage(cap, agent_id=agent, tag=ref["visibility_tag"])
    return ref


def test_idempotency_replays_same_result_not_a_new_execution(ledger):
    store, _ = ledger
    cap = running(store)
    store.finish(cap, result={"output_id": "o1", "reply": "answer"})
    duplicate = admit(store)
    assert not duplicate.created
    assert duplicate.status == "completed"
    assert duplicate.result["output_id"] == "o1"
    with pytest.raises(ExecutionRejected):
        store.start(duplicate.capability)
    with pytest.raises(ExecutionRejected, match="payload_conflict"):
        admit(store, text="different")


def test_atomic_concurrent_admission_only_one_created(ledger):
    store, _ = ledger
    barrier = threading.Barrier(8)

    def worker(_):
        barrier.wait()
        return admit(store).created

    with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=8) as pool:
        result = list(pool.map(worker, range(8)))
    assert sum(result) == 1


def test_other_owner_and_session_do_not_reuse_result(ledger):
    store, _ = ledger
    first = running(store)
    other_owner = admit(store, user="u2")
    other_session = admit(store, session="s2")
    assert other_owner.created and other_session.created
    with pytest.raises(ExecutionRejected):
        store.check(replace(first, user_id="u2"))


def test_cancel_rejects_all_old_commits(ledger):
    store, _ = ledger
    cap = running(store)
    store.cancel_session("u1", "s1")
    with pytest.raises(ExecutionRejected):
        store.finish(cap, result={"output_id": "late"})
    with pytest.raises(ExecutionRejected):
        store.publish_memory(cap, logical_id="m1", reference=reference())
    successor = running(store, key="next")
    assert successor.generation > cap.generation
    assert successor.fence > cap.fence
    store.finish(successor, result={"output_id": "current"})
    assert store.snapshot("u1")["sessions"]["s1"]["output_id"] == "current"


def test_expiry_unknown_does_not_automatically_repeat_provider(ledger):
    store, clock = ledger
    cap = running(store)
    clock[0] = cap.deadline_ms
    with pytest.raises(ExecutionExpired):
        store.finish(cap, result={"output_id": "late"})
    duplicate = admit(store)
    assert duplicate.created is False and duplicate.status == "unknown"
    assert running(store, key="new-intent").fence > cap.fence


def test_old_epoch_never_reclaims_lost_state(ledger):
    store, _ = ledger
    old = running(store)
    store.atomic._values.clear()
    new = running(store)
    assert new.epoch != old.epoch
    with pytest.raises(ExecutionRejected):
        store.finish(old, result={"output_id": "old"})


def test_commit_checks_deadline_at_cas_not_only_during_python_work(ledger, monkeypatch):
    store, clock = ledger
    cap = running(store)
    original = store.atomic.cas

    def delayed(*args, **kwargs):
        clock[0] = cap.deadline_ms
        return original(*args, **kwargs)

    monkeypatch.setattr(store.atomic, "cas", delayed)
    with pytest.raises(ExecutionExpired):
        store.finish(cap, result={"output_id": "not-visible"})
    assert store.snapshot("u1")["sessions"]["s1"]["output_id"] == ""


def test_memory_delete_disable_enable_is_a_shared_revocation_barrier(ledger):
    store, _ = ledger
    cap = running(store)
    ref = registered(store, cap)
    store.finish(cap, result={}, memories={"m1": ref})
    removed = store.memory_control("u1", delete_ids=["m1"])
    assert removed == ["p1"]
    assert store.visible_memory("u1", "yzl") == {}
    for control in ({"enabled": False}, {"enabled": True}):
        store.memory_control("u1", **control)
        with pytest.raises(ExecutionRejected, match="memory_revoked"):
            store.publish_memory(cap, logical_id="late", reference=reference("late"))


def test_memory_copy_on_write_compares_entire_reference_version(ledger):
    store, _ = ledger
    cap = running(store)
    old = registered(store, cap, "v1")
    store.finish(cap, result={}, memories={"logical": old})
    editor = running(store, key="editor", session="control")
    replacement = registered(store, editor, "v2")
    store.replace_memories(
        editor, primary_id="logical", reference=replacement, agent_id="yzl",
        expected_versions={"logical": digest(old)},
    )
    successor = running(store, key="stale-editor", session="control")
    late = registered(store, successor, "late-v3")
    with pytest.raises(ExecutionRejected, match="edit_conflict"):
        store.replace_memories(
            successor, primary_id="logical", reference=late, agent_id="yzl",
            expected_versions={"logical": digest(old)},
        )
    assert store.visible_memory("u1", "yzl")["logical"]["physical_id"] == "v2"


def test_presence_cannot_preempt_user_but_user_can_preempt_presence(ledger):
    store, _ = ledger
    presence = running(store, kind="presence")
    user = running(store, key="user-intent", kind="user")
    with pytest.raises(ExecutionRejected):
        store.finish(presence, result={})
    with pytest.raises(ExecutionRejected, match="session_busy"):
        admit(store, key="another-presence", kind="presence")
    store.finish(user, result={"output_id": "user"})


def test_output_and_checkpoint_are_published_together(ledger):
    store, _ = ledger
    cap = running(store)
    store.finish(
        cap, result={"output_id": "out"}, checkpoint="stage-thread",
        history_pair=("question", "answer"), objects={"r1": {"path": "private.wav"}},
    )
    state = store.snapshot("u1")
    assert state["sessions"]["s1"]["checkpoint"] == "stage-thread"
    assert state["sessions"]["s1"]["history"] == [["question", "answer"]]
    assert state["objects"]["r1"]["operation_id"] == cap.operation_id


def test_budget_counts_failed_attempt_and_blocks_second_llm(ledger):
    store, _ = ledger
    cap = running(store)
    invoked = []
    with bind(store, cap):
        assert call("llm", lambda timeout: invoked.append(timeout) or "reply", per_call_seconds=20) == "reply"
        with pytest.raises(ExecutionBudgetExceeded):
            call("llm", lambda timeout: invoked.append(timeout), per_call_seconds=20)
    assert len(invoked) == 1


def test_retained_key_closes_explicitly_instead_of_restarting(ledger):
    store, clock = ledger
    cap = running(store)
    store.finish(cap, result={})
    clock[0] += 86_400_001
    with pytest.raises(ExecutionRejected, match="window_closed"):
        admit(store)


def test_new_subject_does_not_silently_opt_into_long_term_memory():
    store = ExecutionLedger(LocalAtomic())
    cap = running(store)
    assert store.snapshot("u1")["memory_enabled"] is False
    with pytest.raises(ExecutionRejected, match="memory_revoked"):
        store.publish_memory(cap, logical_id="m1", reference=reference())
    store.memory_control("u1", enabled=True)
    replacement = running(store, key="explicitly-enabled")
    ref = registered(store, replacement)
    store.finish(replacement, result={}, memories={"m1": ref})
    assert store.visible_memory("u1", "yzl")["m1"]["physical_id"] == "p1"


def test_corrupt_state_is_not_reinterpreted_as_a_fresh_user():
    from aiagent.state.shared_state import SharedStateUnavailableError

    store = ExecutionLedger(LocalAtomic())
    key = store.key("u1")
    bad = '{"schema":1,"owner":"u1","fence":"invalid"}'
    store.atomic._values[key] = bad
    with pytest.raises(SharedStateUnavailableError, match="state_invalid"):
        store.snapshot("u1")
    with pytest.raises(SharedStateUnavailableError, match="state_invalid"):
        admit(store)
    assert store.atomic._values[key] == bad
```

## 5. 新增 `tests/integration/test_execution_ledger_redis.py`

文件功能：必须显式指向专用测试 Redis，使用随机 prefix，仅清理本测试创建的 key；双进程不是两个内存对象。未配置时明确 skip，不把 skip 算作真实 Redis 验证。

```python
from __future__ import annotations

import multiprocessing
import os
import secrets

import pytest

from aiagent.state.execution_ledger import ExecutionLedger, RedisAtomic, digest
from aiagent.state.shared_state import SharedStateUnavailableError


def _worker(url, prefix, gate, output):
    from redis import Redis

    client = Redis.from_url(url, decode_responses=True, socket_connect_timeout=2, socket_timeout=2)
    store = ExecutionLedger(RedisAtomic(client), prefix)
    try:
        if not gate.wait(20):
            raise RuntimeError("test_gate_timeout")
        admission = store.begin(
            user_id="synthetic-user", session_id="s1", idempotency_key="same",
            payload_digest=digest({"text": "same"}),
        )
        output.put(("ok", admission.created))
    except Exception as exc:
        output.put(("error", type(exc).__name__))
    finally:
        client.close()


@pytest.fixture
def real_redis():
    from redis import Redis

    url = os.environ.get("BATCH06_REDIS_URL")
    if not url:
        pytest.skip("explicit dedicated BATCH06_REDIS_URL is required")
    client = Redis.from_url(url, decode_responses=True, socket_connect_timeout=2, socket_timeout=2)
    client.ping()
    prefix = f"batch06-test-{secrets.token_hex(12)}"
    store = ExecutionLedger(RedisAtomic(client), prefix)
    try:
        yield url, prefix, store, client
    finally:
        # Explicit exact keys; no FLUSHDB and no arbitrary wildcard deletion.
        client.delete(store.key("synthetic-user"))
        client.close()


def test_two_processes_admit_exactly_one_execution(real_redis):
    url, prefix, _, _ = real_redis
    ctx = multiprocessing.get_context("spawn")
    gate, output = ctx.Event(), ctx.Queue()
    processes = [ctx.Process(target=_worker, args=(url, prefix, gate, output)) for _ in range(2)]
    for process in processes:
        process.start()
    try:
        gate.set()
        results = [output.get(timeout=30) for _ in processes]
        assert all(status == "ok" for status, _ in results), results
        assert sum(created for _, created in results) == 1
        for process in processes:
            process.join(10)
            assert process.exitcode == 0
    finally:
        for process in processes:
            if process.is_alive():
                process.terminate()
                process.join(10)
        output.close()
        output.join_thread()


def test_real_redis_cancel_rejects_late_commit(real_redis):
    from aiagent.state.execution_ledger import ExecutionRejected

    _, _, store, _ = real_redis
    admission = store.begin(
        user_id="synthetic-user", session_id="s1", idempotency_key="one",
        payload_digest=digest({"text": "one"}),
    )
    cap = admission.capability
    store.start(cap)
    store.cancel_session("synthetic-user", "s1")
    with pytest.raises(ExecutionRejected):
        store.finish(cap, result={"output_id": "late"})


def test_redis_unavailable_never_switches_to_local():
    class BrokenRedis:
        def eval(self, *args):
            raise ConnectionError("synthetic-outage")

    store = ExecutionLedger(RedisAtomic(BrokenRedis()), "outage-test")
    with pytest.raises(SharedStateUnavailableError):
        store.begin(user_id="u1", session_id="s1", idempotency_key="one", payload_digest=digest("one"))
```

## 6. 新增 `apps/api/execution_route.py`：HTTP 操作准入与结果提交

文件功能：保留现有 endpoint 和 JSON envelope，在五类生成入口的外层统一认证、计算载荷摘要、原子准入、重放已完成结果、绑定执行资格，并在 endpoint 返回后原子发布成功结果。它不是替代用户认证的 middleware，也不缓存未认证响应。

新增文件全文：

```python
from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio
import hashlib
import json
import secrets
from typing import Any

from fastapi import HTTPException, Request
from fastapi.exceptions import RequestValidationError
from fastapi.routing import APIRoute
from starlette.datastructures import UploadFile
from starlette.responses import JSONResponse

from aiagent.state.execution_context import bind
from aiagent.state.execution_ledger import (
    ExecutionRejected,
    canonical,
    digest,
)
from aiagent.state.shared_state import SharedStateError
from apps.api.user_auth import enforce_http_policy
from aiagent.state.voice_execution import VoiceCallNotFound, parse_generation, voice_session
from apps.core.runtime_registry import get_runtime
from apps.api.shared_state_errors import shared_state_error_response
from cloud.config import cloud_settings
from config.settings import settings

MANAGED = {
    ("POST", "/chat"), ("POST", "/chat/multimodal"),
    ("POST", "/vision/analyze"), ("POST", "/vision/chat"),
    ("POST", "/voice/realtime/turn"), ("POST", "/voice/transcribe"),
    ("POST", "/knowledge/search"),
}


def configuration_digest() -> str:
    # Explicit allowlist: never hash/serialize all Settings or include credentials.
    fields = (
        "llm_provider", "llm_model", "llm_timeout_seconds",
        "state_provider", "planner_provider", "vision_provider", "vision_model",
        "vision_timeout_seconds", "vision_max_image_bytes", "tts_provider",
        "tts_timeout_seconds", "memory_enable_graph", "enable_mock_llm",
        "enable_mock_state", "enable_mock_planner", "enable_mock_tts",
    )
    return digest({
        "settings": {name: getattr(settings, name, None) for name in fields},
        "execution_state_mode": cloud_settings.execution_state_mode,
        "budget_policy": "v12-06-fixed-120s-24calls",
    })


def resolved_session(user_id: str, logical: str) -> str:
    return "s12:" + hashlib.sha256(canonical([user_id, logical]).encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()


async def _fields(request: Request) -> tuple[dict[str, Any], dict[str, Any]]:
    kind = request.headers.get("content-type", "").split(";", 1)[0].lower()
    if kind == "application/json":
        fields = await request.json()
        if not isinstance(fields, dict):
            raise ValueError("execution_object_body_required")
        if len(canonical(fields).encode("utf-8")) > 128 * 1024:
            raise ValueError("execution_body_too_large")
        return fields, fields
    form = await request.form()
    fields: dict[str, Any] = {}
    payload: dict[str, Any] = {}
    for name, value in form.multi_items():
        if name in payload:
            raise ValueError("execution_duplicate_form_field")
        if isinstance(value, UploadFile):
            def fingerprint(value=value):
                source = value.file
                position = source.tell()
                size = 0
                hasher = hashlib.sha256()
                try:
                    source.seek(0)
                    while chunk := source.read(1024 * 1024):
                        size += len(chunk)
                        if size > 25 * 1024 * 1024:
                            raise ValueError("execution_upload_too_large")
                        hasher.update(chunk)
                finally:
                    source.seek(position)
                return {"sha256": hasher.hexdigest(), "bytes": size,
                        "filename": value.filename, "mime": value.content_type}
            # Execute immediately; the loop variable is not retained by a background task.
            payload[name] = await asyncio.to_thread(fingerprint)
        else:
            if len(str(value)) > 32_768:
                raise ValueError("execution_form_value_too_large")
            fields[name] = value
            payload[name] = value
    if len(canonical(payload).encode("utf-8")) > 128 * 1024:
        raise ValueError("execution_form_too_large")
    return fields, payload


class ExecutionRoute(APIRoute):
    def get_route_handler(self):
        original = super().get_route_handler()

        async def handler(request: Request):
            route_key = (request.method, self.path)
            if route_key not in MANAGED:
                return await original(request)
            # Endpoint dependencies run later; authorize explicitly before admission.
            await enforce_http_policy(request)
            principal = request.state.principal
            try:
                fields, payload = await _fields(request)
                is_voice_turn = self.path == "/voice/realtime/turn"
                voice_call_id = str(fields.get("call_id") or "") if is_voice_turn else ""
                expected_voice_generation = (
                    parse_generation(fields.get("expected_generation")) if is_voice_turn else None
                )
                if is_voice_turn and not voice_call_id:
                    raise ValueError("voice_call_id_required")
                key_values = request.headers.getlist("idempotency-key")
                if len(key_values) > 1:
                    raise ValueError("execution_duplicate_idempotency_header")
                key = key_values[0] if key_values else secrets.token_urlsafe(24)
                if not key or len(key) > 128 or any(ord(char) < 33 or ord(char) > 126 for char in key):
                    raise ValueError("execution_invalid_idempotency_key")
            except ValueError:
                return JSONResponse(status_code=400, content={"ok": False, "error": "invalid_execution_input"})

            logical = str(fields.get("session_id") or fields.get("call_id") or "")
            if not logical:
                family = "vision" if self.path == "/vision/analyze" else "asr" if self.path == "/voice/transcribe" else "chat"
                logical = f"{family}:{principal.user_id}"
            try:
                runtime = get_runtime()
                ledger = runtime.dispatcher.execution_ledger
                persona_config = (
                    runtime.dispatcher.persona_manager.get_active_persona()
                    .config.model_dump(mode="json")
                )
                admission = ledger.begin(
                    user_id=principal.user_id,
                    session_id=(voice_session(principal.user_id, voice_call_id) if is_voice_turn
                                else resolved_session(principal.user_id, logical)),
                    idempotency_key=key,
                    payload_digest=digest({"route": self.path, "payload": payload}),
                    persona_digest=digest(persona_config),
                    config_digest=configuration_digest(),
                    voice_call_id=voice_call_id,
                    expected_voice_generation=expected_voice_generation,
                )
                cap = admission.capability
                headers = {"X-Operation-ID": cap.operation_id, "X-Execution-State": admission.status}
                if not admission.created:
                    if admission.status == "completed" and admission.result is not None:
                        return JSONResponse(
                            status_code=admission.result["status_code"],
                            content=admission.result["body"], headers=headers,
                        )
                    status = 202 if admission.status in {"accepted", "running"} else 409
                    return JSONResponse(status_code=status, headers=headers, content={
                        "ok": status == 202, "operation_id": cap.operation_id,
                        "execution_state": admission.status, "result_ready": False,
                        "automatic_retry_allowed": False,
                    })
                ledger.start(cap)
                with bind(ledger, cap) as frame:
                    frame.persona_config = persona_config
                    try:
                        response = await original(request)
                        if response.status_code >= 400:
                            ledger.terminal(cap, "unknown" if response.status_code >= 500 else "failed")
                            response.headers.update({
                                **headers,
                                "X-Execution-State": "unknown" if response.status_code >= 500 else "failed",
                            })
                            return response
                        body = json.loads(bytes(response.body))
                        if not isinstance(body, dict) or body.get("ok") is not True:
                            ledger.terminal(cap, "failed")
                            response.headers.update({**headers, "X-Execution-State": "failed"})
                            return response
                        frame.remaining()
                        committed = ledger.finish(
                            cap, result={"status_code": response.status_code, "body": body,
                                         "output_id": str(body.get("output_id", ""))},
                            checkpoint=frame.checkpoint, history_pair=frame.history_pair,
                            objects=frame.staged_objects, memories=frame.staged_memories,
                            voice_result=frame.voice_result,
                        )
                        response.headers.update({**headers, "X-Execution-State": "completed"})
                        if is_voice_turn:
                            return JSONResponse(
                                status_code=committed["status_code"], content=committed["body"],
                                headers={key: value for key, value in response.headers.items()
                                         if key.lower() not in {"content-length", "content-type"}},
                            )
                        return response
                    except (HTTPException, RequestValidationError) as exc:
                        status = getattr(exc, "status_code", 422)
                        try:
                            ledger.terminal(cap, "unknown" if status >= 500 else "failed")
                        except SharedStateError:
                            pass
                        raise
                    except BaseException:
                        # A lost response/timeout does not prove an external operation never ran.
                        try:
                            ledger.terminal(cap, "unknown")
                        except SharedStateError:
                            pass
                        raise
            except VoiceCallNotFound:
                return JSONResponse(status_code=404, content={"ok": False, "error": "call_not_found"})
            except ExecutionRejected as exc:
                return JSONResponse(status_code=409, content={
                    "ok": False, "error": str(exc), "automatic_retry_allowed": False,
                })
            except SharedStateError as exc:
                response = shared_state_error_response(exc)
                if response is not None:
                    return response
                return JSONResponse(status_code=503, content={
                    "ok": False, "error": "execution_backend_unavailable",
                    "automatic_retry_allowed": False,
                })

        return handler
```

`Idempotency-Key` 只能是短 ASCII 字符串，摘要包含路由和实际上传文件的字节 SHA-256；临时随机文件路径与 `request_id` 不进入业务载荷摘要。客户端不提供键时仍兼容旧调用，但这种调用不具备传输重试去重保证。

`202` 表示同一操作仍在运行，不是返回了一个空助手回答。`unknown` 不自动重跑。重复完成操作重放之前结果，但资源下载仍须验证当前 owner/可见性与资源有效期，不能因为历史 JSON 中有 URL 就恢复已删除的音频。

`ExecutionRoute` 只能包装普通 JSON 生成接口，不包装文件下载/流响应。文件哈希前的 multipart 解析仍可能暂存请求体；网关请求体上限是另一个防护，不能以这个类声称解决上传洪泛。

### 6.1 路由与 Dispatcher 装配位置

在 `apps/api/routes/chat.py`、`multimodal_chat.py`、`vision.py`、`voice.py`、`voice_realtime.py`、`knowledge.py` 的 import 区增加 `from apps.api.execution_route import ExecutionRoute`，把各自 `router = APIRouter()` 替换为：

```python
router = APIRouter(route_class=ExecutionRoute)
```

未列入 MANAGED 的 stats/start/end 管理/控制接口仍走原 handler 和全局身份 dependency，不被误缓存。两次 enforce 使用同一固定 Token 映射与已缓存 Form，是有意的前置授权，不得把后一次 dependency 删除成匿名旁路。

`EventDispatcher.__init__()` import 区新增：

```python
from aiagent.state.execution_ledger import ExecutionLedger, LocalAtomic, RedisAtomic
```

构造函数新增 keyword-only 参数 `execution_ledger: ExecutionLedger | None = None`（放在现有可选参数末尾），并在末尾新增：

```python
        self.execution_ledger = execution_ledger or ExecutionLedger(
            RedisAtomic(shared_state_store.redis) if shared_state_store is not None else LocalAtomic(),
            prefix=shared_state_store.prefix if shared_state_store is not None else "aiagent:v1",
        )
```

bootstrap 必须按第 20 节在 Memory 装配前创建唯一 ledger，并通过此参数传入。这里保留 `None` 仅兼容独立 Dispatcher 单元构造；正式 Runtime 不能让 Memory 和 Dispatcher 各自创建不同 LocalAtomic。多 Worker Redis 使用同一个连接配置与 namespace；连接失败不回退 LocalAtomic。本地 local 模式仅单 Runtime/单进程。

## 7. `aiagent/orchestrator/dispatcher.py`：停止把全局进程字段当用户状态真源

文件功能：编排主图与输出准备；成功结果由 HTTP 的 ExecutionRoute 统一发布，直接本地调用则由 Dispatcher 自己建立/提交操作。旧 Redis session lock 不再是这一流程的提交资格；不要在新方法外再套旧锁。

保留构造函数及第 6.1 节的 `self.execution_ledger`；import 区加入：

```python
from aiagent.persona.persona_models import PersonaConfig
from aiagent.persona.persona_runtime import PersonaRuntime
from aiagent.state.execution_context import bind, current
from aiagent.state.execution_ledger import ExecutionRejected, digest
from aiagent.state.private_resources import register_output_resources
```

用下面两段完整替换 `_handle_input_unlocked()` 和 `handle_input()`；构造函数的原依赖字段保持，不删除其它模块仍在使用的管理对象。

```python
    def _handle_input_unlocked(self, event: InputEvent) -> OutputEvent:
        frame = current(required=True)
        assert frame is not None
        cap = frame.capability
        if frame.ledger is not self.execution_ledger or event.user_id != cap.user_id:
            raise ExecutionRejected("execution_dispatcher_scope_mismatch")
        frame.remaining()
        event.session_id = cap.session_id
        event.turn_id = f"{cap.operation_id}:{cap.generation}"
        accepted, reason = self.dialogue_manager.should_accept(event)
        if not accepted:
            raise ValueError(reason)
        if not self.scheduler.should_process_now(event):
            raise ValueError("empty_input_event")
        if frame.persona_config is None:
            raise ExecutionRejected("execution_persona_snapshot_required")
        persona = PersonaRuntime(PersonaConfig.model_validate(frame.persona_config))
        session = frame.ledger.snapshot(event.user_id)["sessions"][cap.session_id]
        history = [f"用户: {question}\n助手: {answer}" for question, answer in session["history"]]
        packet = self.agent_core.main_runner.run(
            event=event, persona_runtime=persona, history=history,
            session_id=cap.session_id, turn_id=event.turn_id,
        )
        frame.remaining()
        packet.metadata.update({
            "operation_id": cap.operation_id, "generation": str(cap.generation),
            "fence": str(cap.fence), "session_id": cap.session_id, "turn_id": event.turn_id,
        })
        output = self.output_broadcaster.broadcast(OutputEvent(packet=packet))
        frame.remaining()
        output = register_output_resources(output, user_id=event.user_id)
        frame.history_pair = (event.text, output.packet.reply_text)
        # No ConversationState.add_output / record_turn / RESPONSE_READY before commit.
        return output

    def handle_input(self, event: InputEvent) -> OutputEvent:
        frame = current()
        if frame is not None:
            return self._handle_input_unlocked(event)
        session_id = self.session_manager.resolve_session_id(event)
        persona_config = self.persona_manager.get_active_persona().config.model_dump(mode="json")
        admission = self.execution_ledger.begin(
            user_id=event.user_id, session_id=session_id, idempotency_key=event.event_id,
            payload_digest=digest(event.model_dump(mode="json")),
            persona_digest=digest(persona_config),
        )
        if not admission.created:
            if admission.status == "completed" and admission.result:
                return OutputEvent.model_validate(admission.result["output"])
            raise ExecutionRejected("execution_not_ready_for_replay")
        cap = admission.capability
        self.execution_ledger.start(cap)
        try:
            with bind(self.execution_ledger, cap) as local_frame:
                local_frame.persona_config = persona_config
                output = self._handle_input_unlocked(event)
                self.execution_ledger.finish(
                    cap, result={"output_id": output.output_id, "output": output.model_dump(mode="json")},
                    checkpoint=local_frame.checkpoint, history_pair=local_frame.history_pair,
                    objects=local_frame.staged_objects, memories=local_frame.staged_memories,
                )
                return output
        except BaseException:
            try:
                self.execution_ledger.terminal(cap, "unknown")
            except Exception:
                # Do not fabricate success or rerun the operation when Redis is unavailable.
                pass
            raise
```

原 `conversation_state.add_input/add_output`、全局 `agent_state.current_*`、`dialogue_manager.record_turn()` 不再用于这条用户流程；它们可以留作其它旧本地工具的管理对象，但不能继续成为主聊天的历史 fallback。历史从账本已发布的 history 读取，新回合没有提交前不成为下一个回合的成功历史。

本批未开发新的事件平台，原 RESPONSE_READY 的广播须移到第七批的已提交输出事件；不能为了兼容旧订阅者，在提交前继续发布成功通知。暂停管理仍是已有管理行为，后续不能把进程内 pause snapshot 宣称为多 Worker 用户状态。

## 8. `aiagent/graphs/llm_graph.py`：隔离隐式 checkpoint

文件功能：保留现有 LLM 图、官方 saver、总结节点及 RAG 抽取式事实分支，仅将每次运行写入单独物理线程；已发布 checkpoint 指针在最终操作提交时推进。

### 8.1 装配与方法改名

增加 imports：

```python
import inspect

from aiagent.state.execution_context import current
from aiagent.state.execution_ledger import ExecutionRejected, digest
```

在 `LLMRunner.__init__()` 的 `self.graph = ...` 前增加 `self.execution_ledger = None`。在 `apps/core/bootstrap.py` 已创建 `EventDispatcher(...)` 后、创建最终 CoreRuntime 之前加：

```python
    llm_runner.execution_ledger = dispatcher.execution_ledger
```

这里 `llm_runner` 和 `dispatcher` 使用当前 bootstrap 中的实际局部变量名；若你调整过名字，只替换变量名，不另建第二个 LLMRunner。当前源码已使用这两个名字，实施前通过同文件赋值位置确认。

把现有 `def run(...)` **只改名**为 `def _run_isolated(...)`，保留全部原签名/主体，包括 grounded_fact 分支和返回构造。删除此方法开头的 `if self.shared_state_store is not None: ... register_thread(...)` 整个块：注册逻辑线程移动到下面新 wrapper，不能每次把物理 stage 线程加入旧 100 条用户索引。

在 `_run_isolated()` 前新增完整 `run()`：

```python
    def run(self, *args, **kwargs) -> LLMGraphResult:
        frame = current(required=True)
        assert frame is not None
        cap = frame.capability
        bound = inspect.signature(self._run_isolated).bind(*args, **kwargs)
        bound.apply_defaults()
        logical_thread = bound.arguments["thread_id"]
        user_id = bound.arguments["user_id"]
        if logical_thread != cap.session_id or user_id != cap.user_id:
            raise ExecutionRejected("execution_checkpoint_scope_mismatch")
        if self.execution_ledger is not frame.ledger:
            raise ExecutionRejected("execution_checkpoint_ledger_mismatch")
        frame.remaining()
        if self.shared_state_store is not None:
            self.shared_state_store.register_thread(ThreadOwnerRecord(
                thread_id=logical_thread, user_id=user_id, session_id=logical_thread,
            ))
        self._thread_users[logical_thread] = user_id
        physical_thread = "op12:" + digest([
            cap.user_id, cap.session_id, cap.epoch, cap.operation_id, cap.generation, cap.fence,
        ])
        session = frame.ledger.snapshot(user_id)["sessions"][logical_thread]
        previous = session.get("checkpoint", "")
        seed = {}
        if previous:
            snapshot = self.graph.get_state({"configurable": {"thread_id": previous}})
            values = getattr(snapshot, "values", {})
            if isinstance(values, dict):
                seed = {key: values[key] for key in ("messages", "context") if key in values}
        if not seed:
            messages = []
            for question, answer in session["history"]:
                messages.extend([HumanMessage(content=question), AIMessage(content=answer)])
            if messages:
                seed = {"messages": messages}
        try:
            if seed:
                self.graph.update_state({"configurable": {"thread_id": physical_thread}}, seed)
            bound.arguments["thread_id"] = physical_thread
            result = self._run_isolated(*bound.args, **bound.kwargs)
            frame.remaining()
            frame.checkpoint = physical_thread
            result.thread_id = logical_thread
            result.metadata.update({
                "checkpoint_visibility": "staged_until_operation_commit",
                "operation_id": cap.operation_id,
            })
            return result
        finally:
            self._persona_runtime_cache.pop(physical_thread, None)
            self._thread_users.pop(physical_thread, None)
```

wrapper 使用标准 `inspect.signature(...).bind()` 映射原参数，不靠手工拆 positional args，也不重写整段现有 Graph 输入字段。物理线程由服务端资格派生，客户端不能指定 stage ID。旧任务仍可能写自己的 stage checkpoint，但不会改新任务的线程，也不能更新当前 session 指针。

### 8.2 历史查询与清理

将现有 `recent_dialogue_lines()` 改名为 `_recent_physical_dialogue_lines()`，主体不变。在它前面增加完整 wrapper：

```python
    def recent_dialogue_lines(self, thread_id: str, limit: int = 8) -> list[str]:
        frame = current()
        if frame is not None:
            physical = "op12:" + digest([
                frame.capability.user_id, frame.capability.session_id,
                frame.capability.epoch, frame.capability.operation_id,
                frame.capability.generation, frame.capability.fence,
            ])
            if thread_id == physical:
                return self._recent_physical_dialogue_lines(physical, limit)
        owner = self._thread_users.get(thread_id, "")
        if self.shared_state_store is not None:
            registration = self.shared_state_store.get_thread_owner(thread_id)
            owner = registration.user_id if registration else ""
        if not owner or self.execution_ledger is None:
            return []
        session = self.execution_ledger.snapshot(owner).get("sessions", {}).get(thread_id)
        if not session:
            return []
        return [f"用户: {question}\n助手: {answer}" for question, answer in session["history"]][-limit:]
```

替换 `clear_thread()` 全方法：

```python
    def clear_thread(self, thread_id: str, user_id: str = "") -> None:
        if self.execution_ledger is None:
            raise RuntimeError("execution_ledger_not_attached")
        owner = self._thread_users.get(thread_id, "")
        if self.shared_state_store is not None:
            registration = self.shared_state_store.get_thread_owner(thread_id)
            owner = registration.user_id if registration else ""
        if not user_id or (owner and owner != user_id):
            raise SharedStateOwnershipError("checkpoint_owner_required")
        session = self.execution_ledger.snapshot(user_id).get("sessions", {}).get(thread_id)
        physical = session.get("checkpoint", "") if session else ""
        self.execution_ledger.cancel_session(user_id, thread_id, clear=True)
        if physical:
            delete_thread = getattr(self.checkpointer, "delete_thread", None)
            if not callable(delete_thread):
                raise RuntimeError("checkpoint_delete_unsupported")
            delete_thread(physical)
```

逻辑撤销在物理 delete 之前完成；物理删除失败不能恢复可见指针。`clear_user_threads()` 已按第二/五批传 `user_id`，保留该调用方式；测试 monkeypatch 必须接受 `**kwargs`。不要在清理后删除 owner 索引：并发新回合可能已经注册它，旧清理不能删掉新注册。过期 stage 的物理清理与索引压缩在第九批处理。

旧本地 `AgentCore._build_history_lines()` 的 ConversationState fallback 不再给用户主链使用；Dispatcher 已从 ledger history 注入。保留旧方法不能当作当前 checkpoint 已发布的证据。摘要节点的预算接入仍须完成，只有线程隔离并没有限制隐藏外呼。

## 9. 私有输出：暂存与已提交可见性

### 9.1 `aiagent/state/private_resources.py`

文件功能：第五批已提供身份资源隔离，但 `register_output_resources()` 当场登记音频，会让“尚未提交的操作”提前可下载。本批将输出与音频索引统一收进执行账本；原 `PrivateResourceStore` 保留用于第五批既有其它内部对象，不再作为聊天音频下载的后备来源。

保留第五批文件的已有 imports、类与 `get_private_resources()`，增加以下 imports 和常量。**用下方完整函数替换第五批的 `register_output_resources()`，不是再添加第二份同名函数。**

```python
from aiagent.state.execution_context import current
from aiagent.state.execution_ledger import ExecutionRejected, RETENTION_MS

TTS_CACHE_ROOT = Path("data/cache/tts_real").resolve()
MAX_PRIVATE_AUDIO_BYTES = 32 * 1024 * 1024
MAX_OUTPUT_AUDIO_BYTES = 64 * 1024 * 1024
MAX_OUTPUT_SEGMENTS = 8
```

```python
def register_output_resources(output, *, user_id: str):
    frame = current(required=True)
    assert frame is not None
    if frame.capability.user_id != user_id:
        raise SharedStateOwnershipError("execution_resource_owner_mismatch")
    frame.remaining()
    packet = output.packet
    sources = list(packet.audio_segments) or ([packet.audio_path] if packet.audio_path else [])
    if len(sources) > MAX_OUTPUT_SEGMENTS:
        raise ExecutionRejected("execution_audio_segment_quota_exceeded")
    if sources and len(packet.audio_segment_texts) != len(sources):
        raise ExecutionRejected("execution_audio_segment_text_mismatch")
    # Keep no unauthenticated provider URL when no local output was produced.
    packet.audio_url = None
    packet.audio_segment_urls = []
    expires_ms = frame.capability.deadline_ms + RETENTION_MS
    staged = {f"output:{output.output_id}": {
        "kind": "output", "path": "", "expires_ms": expires_ms,
    }}
    paths, urls, created = [], [], []
    total_bytes = 0
    if sources:
        PRIVATE_AUDIO_ROOT.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    try:
        for source_value in sources:
            frame.remaining()
            source = Path(source_value).resolve()
            if (not source.is_relative_to(TTS_CACHE_ROOT) or not source.is_file()
                    or source.suffix.lower() not in {".wav", ".mp3", ".ogg"}):
                raise ValueError("tts_audio_path_invalid")
            filename = f"{uuid4().hex}{source.suffix.lower()}"
            target = PRIVATE_AUDIO_ROOT / filename
            created.append(target)
            hasher = hashlib.sha256()
            file_bytes = 0
            with source.open("rb") as reader, target.open("xb") as writer:
                while chunk := reader.read(256 * 1024):
                    frame.remaining()
                    file_bytes += len(chunk)
                    total_bytes += len(chunk)
                    if (file_bytes > MAX_PRIVATE_AUDIO_BYTES
                            or total_bytes > MAX_OUTPUT_AUDIO_BYTES):
                        raise ExecutionRejected("execution_audio_bytes_exceeded")
                    writer.write(chunk)
                    hasher.update(chunk)
            if file_bytes == 0:
                raise ValueError("tts_audio_empty")
            staged[f"audio:{filename}"] = {
                "kind": "audio", "path": str(target.resolve()),
                "expires_ms": expires_ms, "bytes": file_bytes,
                "sha256": hasher.hexdigest(),
            }
            paths.append(str(target.resolve()))
            urls.append(f"/audio/{filename}")
        frame.remaining()
        if set(staged) & set(frame.staged_objects):
            raise ExecutionRejected("execution_duplicate_staged_resource")
        if len(staged) + len(frame.staged_objects) > 32:
            raise ExecutionRejected("execution_too_many_output_objects")
        frame.staged_objects.update(staged)
    except BaseException:
```
