```python
        # finish() has not been called by this function, so these new copies
        # cannot already be committed. Unknown final CAS outcomes are different.
        for target in created:
            target.unlink(missing_ok=True)
        raise
    packet.audio_segments = paths
    packet.audio_path = paths[0] if paths else None
    packet.audio_url = urls[0] if urls else None
    packet.audio_segment_urls = urls
    packet.metadata["resource_visibility_policy"] = "completed_operation_only"
    return output
```

解释：

- 本函数不再调用 `get_private_resources().register()`；只更新当前 frame 的暂存描述符。真正发布由第 6/7 节同一 `ledger.finish()` 完成，不增加第二次跨 key 提交。
- 每段最多 32 MiB、整轮音频最多 64 MiB、最多八段；这是保护上限，不是音频质量基线。复制时按块检查，不用 `read_bytes()` 一次读入。
- 第五批 TTS 文件名已经随机；私有副本再次随机命名。路径必须属于已知 TTS 缓存，不能拿模型给出的任意系统路径登记。
- 物理文件可能已生成，但没有 `completed` 索引就不可通过本服务下载。最终 CAS 超时时，不能直接删除这些文件：Redis 可能已经提交，只是响应丢失。第九批按可见引用与宽限期清孤儿。
- 有效期是操作 deadline 加 24 小时的固定上界，最多比操作开始后 24 小时多 120 秒；不是滑动续期，不因重试延长。后续生命周期批次可以统一缩短，不能绕过当前已到期检查。
- 内部缓存与私有目录必须由服务控制；本方案不宣称抵御宿主机管理员篡改文件。多 API Worker 必须共享相同私有目录挂载，或者在后续按对象存储合同迁移；Redis 有索引不代表每台主机都有文件。

### 9.2 完整替换 `apps/api/routes/audio.py`

文件功能：保持 `/audio/{filename}`，但只查询当前 Runtime 的账本，不向第五批旧索引或磁盘目录回退。其它主体、尚未提交、撤销清理、过期及丢失文件统一 404；Redis 故障为 503。

```python
from __future__ import annotations

from pathlib import Path

from fastapi import APIRouter, HTTPException, Request
from fastapi.responses import FileResponse

from aiagent.state.private_resources import PRIVATE_AUDIO_ROOT
from apps.api.shared_state_errors import shared_state_error_response
from apps.core.runtime_registry import get_runtime

router = APIRouter()


@router.get("/audio/{filename}")
def get_audio(filename: str, request: Request):
    principal = getattr(request.state, "principal", None)
    if principal is None:
        raise HTTPException(401, detail={"code": "authentication_required"})
    if Path(filename).name != filename or "\\" in filename or "%" in filename:
        raise HTTPException(404, detail={"code": "audio_not_found"})
    try:
        ledger = get_runtime().dispatcher.execution_ledger
        resource = ledger.visible_object(principal.user_id, "audio", filename)
    except Exception as exc:
        response = shared_state_error_response(exc)
        if response is not None:
            return response
        raise
    if resource is None:
        raise HTTPException(404, detail={"code": "audio_not_found"})
    path = Path(resource.get("path", "")).resolve()
    if (not path.is_relative_to(PRIVATE_AUDIO_ROOT) or path.name != filename
            or path.suffix.lower() not in {".wav", ".mp3", ".ogg"}
            or not path.is_file()):
        raise HTTPException(404, detail={"code": "audio_not_found"})
    media_type = {".wav": "audio/wav", ".mp3": "audio/mpeg", ".ogg": "audio/ogg"}[path.suffix.lower()]
    return FileResponse(path, media_type=media_type, headers={
        "Cache-Control": "private, no-store", "X-Content-Type-Options": "nosniff",
    })
```

下载授权的线性化点是读取当前账本；已经开始发送的 HTTP 文件无法在撤销后追回。删除/禁用完成后发起的新下载会看到新账本并拒绝；不能把这项保证扩大成“已经在网络中传输的字节都被取消”。不提供 query Token。

## 10. `OutputBroadcaster` 与 TTS：生成不等于播放

### 10.1 `aiagent/expression/output_broadcaster.py`

文件功能：已有 broadcaster 同时负责 TTS、Live2D 命令落地与本机播放。本批在受管操作中只准备输出，不提前发送或播放；保留已有独立管理工具的旧行为，不开发 Live2D。

增加 imports：

```python
from copy import copy

from aiagent.state.execution_context import current, raise_if_execution_error
from aiagent.state.speaking_state import SpeakingState
```

将原 `broadcast()` 改名为 `_broadcast_legacy()`，方法主体原样保留。然后在它之前增加以下完整 `broadcast()`；这是入口分流，不要保留两个同名 `broadcast`：

```python
    def broadcast(self, output_event: OutputEvent) -> OutputEvent:
        frame = current()
        if frame is None:
            return self._broadcast_legacy(output_event)
        frame.remaining()
        output = output_event.model_copy(deep=True)
        packet = output.packet
        # Never share mutable TTS state across concurrent users/operations.
        tts = copy(self.tts_dispatcher)
        tts.speaking_state = SpeakingState()
        if packet.should_speak:
            packet = tts.dispatch(packet)
        else:
            packet.metadata["tts"] = "skipped_should_speak_false"
        frame.remaining()
        # No physical playback, Live2D dispatch, or success EventBus publish.
        packet.live2d_command_path = None
        packet.live2d = {}
        packet.metadata.update({
            "audio_playback": "deferred_client_consumption",
            "live2d": "deferred_not_in_v12_scope",
            "output_generation_policy": "publish_on_operation_commit",
        })
        output.packet = packet
        return output
```

在现有 `_run_tts()`、`_run_local_playback()` 和改名后的 `_broadcast_legacy()` 每个 `except Exception as exc:` 内，**第一行**增加以下语句；其它日志或 fallback 保留，但不再吞共享错误：

```python
            raise_if_execution_error(exc)
```

注意：不同分支原缩进可能不同，放在各自 except 内即可。`_broadcast_legacy()` 不应被 HTTP 主链使用；第六批的 Dispatcher 对没有 frame 的直接调用会先创建 frame，因此用户聊天也不会走 legacy。

浅拷贝只隔离 dispatcher 字段，客户端仍共享其原有不可变连接配置和随机输出路径。这里不修改共享客户端的 `output_dir` 或 provider 参数，不在并发请求里临时设置 timeout。第七批再提供会话级消费状态；本批克隆的 SpeakingState 是暂存生成状态，不写回全局 `is_speaking`。

### 10.2 `aiagent/expression/tts_dispatcher.py`

文件功能：保留现有四个 Provider、分段清理、普通可选 TTS 失败降级，但取消/超时/共享状态错误必须向外传播，否则 broadcaster 会把失效操作当成功文本继续提交。

增加 import：

```python
from aiagent.state.execution_context import raise_if_execution_error
```

在 `dispatch()` 中 `except Exception as exc:` 后、日志前插入：

```python
            raise_if_execution_error(exc)
```

同一 except 中已有两处原始异常文本不再透传。精确替换下列赋值，保留其它统计字段：

```python
            packet.metadata["tts_error"] = "tts_provider_failed"
            self.speaking_state.tts_error = "tts_provider_failed"
```

上面对应原来的 `packet.metadata["tts_error"] = str(exc)` 与 `self.speaking_state.tts_error = str(exc)` 两处；不是将两个赋值集中移动到其它位置。日志也应按现有脱敏规则处理，不能因为返回已脱敏就认为文件日志可以保存 Secret。外部 TTS 请求的逐段预算见后续预算接线，不以本节的 `frame.remaining()` 代替真实外呼计数。

## 11. 操作状态/取消 API 与 CORS

### 11.1 新增 `apps/api/routes/executions.py`

文件功能：允许已认证主体查询、取消**自己的特定操作**，而不是按客户端可猜测的 session ID 全局打断。取消 A 与新操作 B 交错时只改变 A，不撤销 B；已完成结果不会被普通取消回滚。清空历史使用第 8 节独立接口语义。

```python
from __future__ import annotations

import re

from fastapi import APIRouter, HTTPException, Request

from apps.api.response_utils import ok_response
from apps.api.shared_state_errors import shared_state_error_response
from apps.core.runtime_registry import get_runtime

router = APIRouter()
_OPERATION_ID = re.compile(r"[0-9a-f]{64}\Z")


def _scope(request: Request, operation_id: str):
    principal = getattr(request.state, "principal", None)
    if principal is None:
        raise HTTPException(401, detail={"code": "authentication_required"})
    if not _OPERATION_ID.fullmatch(operation_id):
        raise HTTPException(404, detail={"code": "operation_not_found"})
    return principal.user_id, get_runtime().dispatcher.execution_ledger


@router.get("/executions/{operation_id}")
def execution_status(operation_id: str, request: Request):
    try:
        user_id, ledger = _scope(request, operation_id)
        record = ledger.operation(user_id, operation_id)
        if record is None:
            raise HTTPException(404, detail={"code": "operation_not_found"})
        return ok_response(operation={
            key: record[key] for key in (
                "operation_id", "session_id", "status", "generation", "fence",
                "created_ms", "deadline_ms", "retained_until_ms", "calls", "total_calls",
            )
        }, result_ready=record["status"] == "completed" and "result" in record,
            automatic_retry_allowed=False)
    except Exception as exc:
        response = shared_state_error_response(exc)
        if response is not None:
            return response
        raise


@router.post("/executions/{operation_id}/cancel")
def cancel_execution(operation_id: str, request: Request):
    try:
        user_id, ledger = _scope(request, operation_id)
        status = ledger.cancel_operation(user_id, operation_id)
        if status == "not_found":
            raise HTTPException(404, detail={"code": "operation_not_found"})
        return ok_response(operation_id=operation_id, execution_state=status,
                           external_work_may_still_run=True)
    except Exception as exc:
        response = shared_state_error_response(exc)
        if response is not None:
            return response
        raise
```

`status` 的 accepted/running 是最后持久记录；超过 deadline 而尚无恢复扫描时可以仍显示 running，但 `deadline_ms` 已到、`check/finish` 一律拒绝。这不是服务承诺仍在正常运行。第九批恢复扫描会将过期在途状态标成 unknown。本接口不返回缓存正文、文件路径、批准载荷或 Provider 原文。

### 11.2 接线与权限矩阵

在第五批 `apps/api/user_auth.py` 的 `_USER_READS` 集合中新增以下三个条目，保留原条目。取消接口也放在这个“只要求可信主体、不采信 body 自报身份”的集合里，名称不改变它是写操作的事实：

```python
    ("GET", "/executions/{operation_id}"),
    ("POST", "/executions/{operation_id}/cancel"),
    ("POST", "/knowledge/search"),
```

知识检索在本批明确作为“认证用户可读共享公开知识制品”开放，必须只部署经审阅的公开知识，不含某个用户的私有资料。它不是租户隔离知识库；若现有库混有私有内容，保持该入口关闭，先分离并审阅制品，不能仅加入这一行就开放。重建、全局诊断和任务管理仍属 admin。

在 `apps/api/http_server.py` 的 router imports 与 `include_router` 区分别增加：

```python
from apps.api.routes.executions import router as executions_router
```

```python
app.include_router(executions_router)
```

不能给状态/取消路由添加 `ExecutionRoute`：它们控制已有操作，不应自己占一个同会话操作造成 busy。保留第五批全局认证 dependency。

在 `apps/api/http_security.py` 的 `DEFAULT_CORS_HEADERS` 元组末尾新增 `"Idempotency-Key",`；在 `DEFAULT_EXPOSE_HEADERS` 元组末尾新增 `"X-Operation-ID",` 与 `"X-Execution-State",`。保留所有已有头、origin 校验与 credentials 策略；不要用 `*` 或动态反射 Origin 解决预检问题。

## 12. 完整新增测试：资源提交与输出隔离

### 12.1 `tests/unit/test_execution_output_commit.py`

文件功能：不是仅检查账本 dict，而是调用真正的 `register_output_resources()` 与 broadcaster，证明文件生成和可下载索引之间有明确提交边界。合成文件位于测试临时目录，不调用真实 TTS/设备。

```python
from __future__ import annotations

from copy import deepcopy
from types import SimpleNamespace
from unittest.mock import Mock

import pytest

from aiagent.expression.output_broadcaster import OutputBroadcaster
from aiagent.schemas.outputs import OutputEvent, ResponsePacket
from aiagent.state import private_resources as resources
from aiagent.state.execution_context import bind
from aiagent.state.execution_ledger import ExecutionLedger, ExecutionRejected, LocalAtomic, digest
from aiagent.state.shared_state import SharedStateUnavailableError
from aiagent.state.speaking_state import SpeakingState


@pytest.fixture
def scope(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    clock = [1_000_000]
    ledger = ExecutionLedger(LocalAtomic(lambda: clock[0]))
    cache = tmp_path / "tts"
    cache.mkdir()
    monkeypatch.setattr(resources, "TTS_CACHE_ROOT", cache.resolve())
    monkeypatch.setattr(resources, "PRIVATE_AUDIO_ROOT", (tmp_path / "private").resolve())
    source = cache / "synthetic.wav"
    source.write_bytes(b"RIFF-synthetic-not-an-audibility-fixture")
    return SimpleNamespace(ledger=ledger, clock=clock, source=source)


def start(scope, key="one", user="u1"):
    cap = scope.ledger.begin(
        user_id=user, session_id="s1", idempotency_key=key, payload_digest=digest(key),
    ).capability
    scope.ledger.start(cap)
    return cap


def packet(scope):
    return OutputEvent(packet=ResponsePacket(
        reply_text="synthetic", audio_path=str(scope.source),
        audio_segments=[str(scope.source)], audio_segment_texts=["synthetic"],
    ))


def test_real_staging_is_unreadable_until_atomic_finish(scope):
    cap = start(scope)
    with bind(scope.ledger, cap) as frame:
        output = resources.register_output_resources(packet(scope), user_id="u1")
        filename = output.packet.audio_url.rsplit("/", 1)[-1]
        assert scope.ledger.visible_object("u1", "audio", filename) is None
        assert resources.Path(output.packet.audio_path).is_file()
        scope.ledger.finish(cap, result={"output_id": output.output_id}, objects=frame.staged_objects)
    assert scope.ledger.visible_object("u1", "audio", filename)["bytes"] > 0
    assert scope.ledger.visible_object("u2", "audio", filename) is None


@pytest.mark.parametrize("terminal", ["cancel", "unknown", "expired"])
def test_late_output_never_becomes_visible(scope, terminal):
    cap = start(scope)
    with bind(scope.ledger, cap) as frame:
        output = resources.register_output_resources(packet(scope), user_id="u1")
        filename = output.packet.audio_url.rsplit("/", 1)[-1]
        if terminal == "cancel":
            scope.ledger.cancel_operation("u1", cap.operation_id)
        elif terminal == "unknown":
            scope.ledger.terminal(cap, "unknown")
        else:
            scope.clock[0] = cap.deadline_ms
        with pytest.raises(ExecutionRejected):
            scope.ledger.finish(cap, result={"output_id": "late"}, objects=frame.staged_objects)
    assert scope.ledger.visible_object("u1", "audio", filename) is None


def test_cancel_old_operation_does_not_cancel_successor(scope):
    first = start(scope)
    scope.ledger.cancel_operation("u1", first.operation_id)
    successor = start(scope, key="two")
    assert scope.ledger.cancel_operation("u1", first.operation_id) == "cancelled"
    scope.ledger.check(successor)
    scope.ledger.finish(successor, result={"output_id": "new"})


def test_clear_session_revokes_cached_result_and_download(scope):
    cap = start(scope)
    with bind(scope.ledger, cap) as frame:
        output = resources.register_output_resources(packet(scope), user_id="u1")
        filename = output.packet.audio_url.rsplit("/", 1)[-1]
        scope.ledger.finish(cap, result={"output_id": output.output_id}, objects=frame.staged_objects)
    scope.ledger.cancel_session("u1", "s1", clear=True)
    assert "result" not in scope.ledger.operation("u1", cap.operation_id)
    assert scope.ledger.visible_object("u1", "audio", filename) is None


def test_committed_file_survives_lost_final_commit_response(scope, monkeypatch):
    cap = start(scope)
    with bind(scope.ledger, cap) as frame:
        output = resources.register_output_resources(packet(scope), user_id="u1")
        filename = output.packet.audio_url.rsplit("/", 1)[-1]
        original = scope.ledger.atomic.cas

        def lost_ack(*args, **kwargs):
            result = original(*args, **kwargs)
            if result:
                raise SharedStateUnavailableError("synthetic_reply_lost_after_commit")
            return result

        monkeypatch.setattr(scope.ledger.atomic, "cas", lost_ack)
        with pytest.raises(SharedStateUnavailableError):
            scope.ledger.finish(cap, result={"output_id": output.output_id}, objects=frame.staged_objects)
    # The caller saw an error, but the authoritative record did commit.
    assert scope.ledger.operation("u1", cap.operation_id)["status"] == "completed"
    assert scope.ledger.visible_object("u1", "audio", filename) is not None
    assert resources.Path(output.packet.audio_path).is_file()


def test_expired_audio_is_not_revived_by_repeated_read(scope):
    cap = start(scope)
    with bind(scope.ledger, cap) as frame:
        output = resources.register_output_resources(packet(scope), user_id="u1")
        filename = output.packet.audio_url.rsplit("/", 1)[-1]
        scope.ledger.finish(cap, result={"output_id": output.output_id}, objects=frame.staged_objects)
    item = scope.ledger.visible_object("u1", "audio", filename)
    scope.clock[0] = item["expires_ms"]
    assert scope.ledger.visible_object("u1", "audio", filename) is None
    assert scope.ledger.visible_object("u1", "audio", filename) is None


def test_copy_budget_failure_cleans_only_current_copies(scope, monkeypatch):
    cap = start(scope)
    monkeypatch.setattr(resources, "MAX_PRIVATE_AUDIO_BYTES", 1)
    with bind(scope.ledger, cap) as frame:
        with pytest.raises(ExecutionRejected, match="audio_bytes"):
            resources.register_output_resources(packet(scope), user_id="u1")
        assert frame.staged_objects == {}
    assert list(resources.PRIVATE_AUDIO_ROOT.iterdir()) == []
    assert scope.source.is_file()


class RecordingTTS:
    def __init__(self):
        self.speaking_state = SpeakingState()
        self.calls = []

    def dispatch(self, value):
        self.calls.append(self.speaking_state)
        self.speaking_state.pending_text = "private-to-operation"
        value.metadata["tts_status"] = "ok"
        return value


def broadcaster(tts):
    return OutputBroadcaster(
        tts_dispatcher=tts, live2d_dispatcher=Mock(), motion_policy=Mock(),
        audio_playback_dispatcher=Mock(), enable_local_audio_playback=True,
    )


def test_managed_broadcast_has_no_playback_live2d_or_global_state_change(scope):
    tts = RecordingTTS()
    original = deepcopy(tts.speaking_state.model_dump())
    owner = broadcaster(tts)
    input_output = OutputEvent(packet=ResponsePacket(reply_text="hello"))
    cap = start(scope)
    with bind(scope.ledger, cap):
        result = owner.broadcast(input_output)
    assert tts.speaking_state.model_dump() == original
    assert len(tts.calls) == 1 and tts.calls[0] is not tts.speaking_state
    assert input_output.packet.metadata == {}
    assert result.packet.metadata["audio_playback"] == "deferred_client_consumption"
    owner.live2d_dispatcher.dispatch.assert_not_called()
    owner.audio_playback_dispatcher.dispatch.assert_not_called()
    owner.motion_policy.refine.assert_not_called()


@pytest.mark.parametrize("error", [
    ExecutionRejected("cancelled"), SharedStateUnavailableError("unavailable"),
])
def test_managed_broadcast_does_not_swallow_lifecycle_failure(scope, error):
    tts = RecordingTTS()
    tts.dispatch = Mock(side_effect=error)
    owner = broadcaster(tts)
    cap = start(scope)
    with bind(scope.ledger, cap), pytest.raises(type(error)):
        owner.broadcast(OutputEvent(packet=ResponsePacket(reply_text="hello")))
    owner.live2d_dispatcher.dispatch.assert_not_called()
    owner.audio_playback_dispatcher.dispatch.assert_not_called()
```

这个合成 `RIFF` 字节串仅供复制/授权测试，不是可播放 WAV，不要据此宣称音频听感通过。测试逐一断言“文件存在但下载索引不可见”“finish 后可见”“取消后旧 finish 被拒”“不同用户不可读”“普通复制失败不误删源文件”，比只断言某个 Mock 被调用更接近实际提交边界。

### 12.2 `tests/api/test_execution_resources_contract.py`

文件功能：真实 FastAPI 身份 dependency、实际新增状态/取消路由、实际音频路由和真实文件复制。Runtime 使用仅持有同一 ledger 的薄适配器，隔离其它 Provider；它不是完整运行时 smoke。

```python
from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import json
from types import SimpleNamespace

import pytest
from fastapi import Depends, FastAPI
from fastapi.testclient import TestClient

from aiagent.schemas.outputs import OutputEvent, ResponsePacket
from aiagent.state import private_resources as resources
from aiagent.state.execution_context import bind
from aiagent.state.execution_ledger import ExecutionLedger, LocalAtomic, digest
from apps.api import user_auth
from apps.api.routes import audio, executions

TOKENS = {"u1": "A" * 43, "u2": "B" * 43}


def headers(user="u1"):
    return {"Authorization": f"Bearer {TOKENS[user]}"}


@pytest.fixture
def api(monkeypatch, tmp_path):
    monkeypatch.setattr(user_auth.settings, "api_auth_mode", "bearer")
    monkeypatch.setattr(user_auth.settings, "api_auth_token_sha256_map", json.dumps({
        hashlib.sha256(token.encode()).hexdigest(): user for user, token in TOKENS.items()
    }))
    monkeypatch.setattr(user_auth.settings, "enable_local_audio_playback", False)
    monkeypatch.setattr(user_auth.settings, "app_env", "test")
    monkeypatch.setattr(user_auth.cloud_settings, "cloud_mode", False)
    monkeypatch.setattr(user_auth.cloud_settings, "execution_state_mode", "local")
    root = (tmp_path / "private").resolve()
    monkeypatch.setattr(resources, "PRIVATE_AUDIO_ROOT", root)
    monkeypatch.setattr(audio, "PRIVATE_AUDIO_ROOT", root)
    monkeypatch.setattr(resources, "TTS_CACHE_ROOT", tmp_path.resolve())
    ledger = ExecutionLedger(LocalAtomic())
    runtime = SimpleNamespace(dispatcher=SimpleNamespace(execution_ledger=ledger))
    monkeypatch.setattr(audio, "get_runtime", lambda: runtime)
    monkeypatch.setattr(executions, "get_runtime", lambda: runtime)
    cap = ledger.begin(user_id="u1", session_id="s1", idempotency_key="one",
                       payload_digest=digest("one")).capability
    ledger.start(cap)
    source = tmp_path / "source.wav"
    source.write_bytes(b"synthetic-audio-authorization-test")
    with bind(ledger, cap) as frame:
        output = resources.register_output_resources(OutputEvent(packet=ResponsePacket(
            reply_text="hello", audio_segments=[str(source)], audio_segment_texts=["hello"],
        )), user_id="u1")
        objects = dict(frame.staged_objects)
    app = FastAPI(dependencies=[Depends(user_auth.enforce_http_policy)])
    app.include_router(audio.router)
    app.include_router(executions.router)
    with TestClient(app) as client:
        yield SimpleNamespace(client=client, ledger=ledger, cap=cap,
                              output=output, objects=objects)


def test_real_audio_route_requires_committed_owner_reference(api):
    url = api.output.packet.audio_url
    assert api.client.get(url).status_code == 401
    assert api.client.get(url, headers=headers()).status_code == 404
    api.ledger.finish(api.cap, result={"output_id": api.output.output_id}, objects=api.objects)
    response = api.client.get(url, headers=headers())
    assert response.status_code == 200
    assert response.content == b"synthetic-audio-authorization-test"
    assert response.headers["cache-control"] == "private, no-store"
    assert api.client.get(url, headers=headers("u2")).status_code == 404
    api.ledger.cancel_session("u1", "s1", clear=True)
    assert api.client.get(url, headers=headers()).status_code == 404


def test_real_cancel_and_status_are_owner_scoped_and_idempotent(api):
    url = f"/executions/{api.cap.operation_id}"
    assert api.client.get(url).status_code == 401
    assert api.client.get(url, headers=headers("u2")).status_code == 404
    assert api.client.post(url + "/cancel", headers=headers("u2")).status_code == 404
    assert api.ledger.operation("u1", api.cap.operation_id)["status"] == "running"
    before = api.client.get(url, headers=headers()).json()
    assert before["operation"]["status"] == "running"
    assert "result" not in before["operation"]
    for _ in range(2):
        response = api.client.post(url + "/cancel", headers=headers())
        assert response.status_code == 200
        assert response.json()["execution_state"] == "cancelled"
    assert api.client.get(url, headers=headers()).json()["result_ready"] is False
    assert api.client.get(api.output.packet.audio_url, headers=headers()).status_code == 404
```

## 13. HTTP Provider 的实际预算接线

### 13.1 新增 `aiagent/services/budgeted_http.py`

文件功能：在实际发出同步 HTTP POST 的位置占用额度、使用当前剩余时间、限制响应正文，并将迟到返回重新交给资格检查。不修改共享 client/self 上的 timeout，不重试，也不在缺 frame 时偷偷放行。沿用项目的 httpx，不引入第二个 Provider 平台。

```python
from __future__ import annotations

import math
from typing import Any

import httpx

from aiagent.state.execution_context import call
from aiagent.state.execution_ledger import ExecutionRejected

MAX_JSON_RESPONSE_BYTES = 4 * 1024 * 1024
MAX_AUDIO_RESPONSE_BYTES = 32 * 1024 * 1024


def budgeted_post(
    client: httpx.Client,
    stage: str,
    url: str,
    *,
    per_call_seconds: float,
    max_response_bytes: int = MAX_JSON_RESPONSE_BYTES,
    **kwargs: Any,
) -> httpx.Response:
    if (not math.isfinite(per_call_seconds) or per_call_seconds <= 0
            or type(max_response_bytes) is not int or max_response_bytes < 1):
        raise ValueError("execution_invalid_http_limits")
    if "timeout" in kwargs or "follow_redirects" in kwargs:
        raise ValueError("execution_http_policy_override_forbidden")

    def perform(timeout: float) -> httpx.Response:
        chunks = []
        size = 0
        with client.stream(
            "POST", url, timeout=timeout, follow_redirects=False, **kwargs,
        ) as response:
            for chunk in response.iter_bytes(chunk_size=64 * 1024):
                size += len(chunk)
                if size > max_response_bytes:
                    raise ExecutionRejected("execution_provider_response_too_large")
                chunks.append(chunk)
            # iter_bytes() already decoded HTTP content-encoding. Avoid asking
            # the new Response object to decompress it a second time.
            headers = [
                (key, value) for key, value in response.headers.multi_items()
                if key.lower() not in {"content-encoding", "content-length", "transfer-encoding"}
            ]
            return httpx.Response(
                response.status_code, headers=headers, content=b"".join(chunks),
                request=response.request,
            )

    return call(stage, perform, per_call_seconds=per_call_seconds)
```

解释：

- `call()` 在 `client.stream()` 前 reserve，一次发送占一次额度；返回 401/429/503 也消耗额度，不自动重试。httpx 默认 transport 的 retry 为零；不要在装配时自行改成隐藏重试 transport。
- 用 `client.stream()` 是为了有界读取响应，不代表实现 token/TTS 增量交付。只有完整响应验证通过才返回原服务继续解析。
- JSON 最多 4 MiB、音频最多 32 MiB，超限作为不可继续的执行错误，而不是吞掉后仍发布成功。累计复制预算仍由第 9 节限制。
- 不跟随重定向，避免一条配置端点悄悄变成对另一个目的地的第二次外呼；本工具不替代正式的网络出口 allowlist/SSRF 策略。
- 返回新 `Response` 仍保留原 request，现有 `raise_for_status()` 可用；去掉三个传输头是因为正文已解码、完整缓存在内存。
- 这是应用层的响应预算，不是强进程隔离。压缩解码器内部和底层网络栈仍可能短暂分配内存；墙钟到期不会强杀已有线程。

### 13.2 State、Planner、Vision 的准确替换

以下三个文件 import 区都增加：

```python
from aiagent.services.budgeted_http import budgeted_post
```

`aiagent/services/state_llm_service.py` 的 `_infer_with_lmstudio()` 中，用下面完整调用替换原 `response = client.post(...)`，其余 with、payload 构造及解析保留，不另加外层 call：

```python
            response = budgeted_post(
                client, "state", f"{self.settings.lmstudio_base_url}/chat/completions",
                per_call_seconds=self.settings.llm_timeout_seconds,
                json=payload,
                headers={
                    "Content-Type": "application/json",
                    "Authorization": f"Bearer {self.settings.lmstudio_api_key or 'lm-studio'}",
                },
            )
```

对 `_infer_with_openai()`，同样完整替换为下列形状，使用该方法已有的 `api_key, base_url`：

```python
            response = budgeted_post(
                client, "state", f"{base_url}/chat/completions",
                per_call_seconds=self.settings.llm_timeout_seconds,
                json=payload,
                headers={"Content-Type": "application/json", "Authorization": f"Bearer {api_key}"},
            )
```

`aiagent/services/planner_llm_service.py` 的 `_infer_with_lmstudio()` 中完整替换为：

```python
            response = budgeted_post(
                client, "planner", f"{self.settings.lmstudio_base_url}/chat/completions",
                per_call_seconds=self.settings.llm_timeout_seconds,
                json=payload,
                headers={
                    "Content-Type": "application/json",
                    "Authorization": f"Bearer {self.settings.lmstudio_api_key or 'lm-studio'}",
                },
            )
```

同文件 `_infer_with_openai()` 中完整替换为：

```python
            response = budgeted_post(
                client, "planner", f"{base_url}/chat/completions",
                per_call_seconds=self.settings.llm_timeout_seconds,
                json=payload,
                headers={"Content-Type": "application/json", "Authorization": f"Bearer {api_key}"},
            )
```

两个文件的 payload 构造保持各自原样，不把 State 的 model 或 system prompt 复制到 Planner。

两个文件还各自新增 `from aiagent.state.execution_context import raise_if_execution_error`，在 `infer_state()` / `infer_plan()` 的 `except Exception as e:` 内第一行增加：

```python
            raise_if_execution_error(e)
```

保留普通模型格式错误的原有 fallback，但共享断连/预算/取消不得 fallback。解析辅助方法捕获的是解析错误，只有实际覆盖外呼的 broad except 需要这一条；不要机械改成所有数字转换都查询 Redis。

`aiagent/services/vision_service.py` 的 `_openai_compatible_vision()` 中，把唯一的 `response = client.post(url, headers=headers, json=payload)` 完整替换为：

```python
            response = budgeted_post(
                client, "vision", url, per_call_seconds=self.timeout_seconds,
                headers=headers, json=payload,
            )
```

第五批 GIF 的多帧准备逻辑保留，预算发生在解码/组装完成后的实际发送位置。GIF 最多六图仍是一个请求，因此消耗一个 vision 槽位，不是六个。解码失败不消耗外部请求额度；它仍消耗本机 CPU，需要上文的媒体预算和后续部署限制。

### 13.3 API ASR 与三个真实 TTS 客户端

文件功能：所有真实语音 HTTP 请求都在底层发送点计数；尤其 IndexTTS2 每段一个请求，不能只包整个 `synthesize_streaming()`。四个文件都增加：

```python
from aiagent.services.budgeted_http import MAX_AUDIO_RESPONSE_BYTES, budgeted_post
```

`integrations/asr/api_asr_client.py` 的 `transcribe()` 内完整替换 `response = client.post(...)`：

```python
                response = budgeted_post(
                    client, "asr", self._join_url("asr"),
                    per_call_seconds=self.timeout_seconds,
                    headers=headers, data=data, files=files,
                )
```

ASR 返回 JSON，不传音频响应上限；在这个文件只导入 `budgeted_post` 即可，避免上面公共说明的 `MAX_AUDIO_RESPONSE_BYTES` 成为 unused import。把该方法现有 `httpx.Client(timeout=self.timeout_seconds)` 改成 `httpx.Client(timeout=self.timeout_seconds, trust_env=False)`，与项目其它 Provider 一致。删除 `ASR finished. text=%s` 的原文日志，换成只记录稳定完成状态，不记录 transcript；这不是模型效果报告。

`integrations/tts/gpt_sovits_client.py` 的 `synthesize()` 内替换单行请求：

```python
            response = budgeted_post(
                client, "tts", url, per_call_seconds=self.timeout_seconds,
                max_response_bytes=MAX_AUDIO_RESPONSE_BYTES, json=payload,
            )
```

`integrations/tts/indextts2_client.py` 的 `synthesize_segment()` 内替换单行请求，仍放在 ref_file 的 with 内：

```python
                response = budgeted_post(
                    client, "tts", url, per_call_seconds=self.timeout_seconds,
                    max_response_bytes=MAX_AUDIO_RESPONSE_BYTES, data=data, files=files,
                )
```

`integrations/tts/voxcpm_client.py` 的 `synthesize()` 内完整替换请求：

```python
            response = budgeted_post(
                client, "tts", url, per_call_seconds=self.timeout_seconds,
                max_response_bytes=MAX_AUDIO_RESPONSE_BYTES, data={"text": cleaned},
            )
```

保留各客户端原有音频格式校验和随机文件名，不提前注册成功资源。在 IndexTTS2 `synthesize_streaming()` 中，`if not segments: return []` 之后、`results = []` 之前增加以下预检，避免先产生八段成本才发现文本要九段：

```python
        if len(segments) > 8:
            from aiagent.state.execution_ledger import ExecutionRejected

            raise ExecutionRejected("execution_tts_segment_quota_exceeded")
```

预检不能取代 `reserve`：并发/迟到与总次数仍在每次真实 post 原子计数；不把九段悄悄截成八段。Mock TTS 仍是本地合成，不增加真实外呼计数。FasterWhisper 本地推理尚需独立总 deadline 接线，不能因为 API ASR 已改就声称所有 ASR 分支都已覆盖。

### 13.4 完整新增 `tests/unit/test_budgeted_http.py`

文件功能：使用 httpx 的真实 MockTransport 检查请求实际发出次数、timeout 扩展、取消后零调用、迟到返回与响应上限；不请求网络。测试 `401/429/503` 不重试，而不是只断言配置有 `retries=0`。

```python
from __future__ import annotations

import gzip
from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor
from threading import Event

import httpx
import pytest

from aiagent.services.budgeted_http import budgeted_post
from aiagent.state.execution_context import bind
from aiagent.state.execution_ledger import (
    ExecutionBudgetExceeded, ExecutionLedger, ExecutionRejected, LocalAtomic, digest,
)


def running():
    ledger = ExecutionLedger(LocalAtomic())
    cap = ledger.begin(user_id="u1", session_id="s1", idempotency_key="one",
                       payload_digest=digest("one")).capability
    ledger.start(cap)
    return ledger, cap


@pytest.mark.parametrize("status", [200, 401, 429, 503])
def test_real_transport_has_one_attempt_and_clamped_timeout(status):
    ledger, cap = running()
    requests = []

    def handler(request):
        requests.append(request)
        return httpx.Response(status, json={"synthetic": True})

    with bind(ledger, cap), httpx.Client(transport=httpx.MockTransport(handler)) as client:
        response = budgeted_post(client, "vision", "https://provider.invalid/v1",
                                 per_call_seconds=300, json={"input": "synthetic"})
    assert response.status_code == status
    assert len(requests) == 1
    assert 0 < requests[0].extensions["timeout"]["read"] <= 120
    assert ledger.operation("u1", cap.operation_id)["calls"] == {"vision": 1}


def test_second_vision_call_is_rejected_before_transport():
    ledger, cap = running()
    requests = []

    def handler(request):
        requests.append(request)
        return httpx.Response(200, json={})

    with bind(ledger, cap), httpx.Client(transport=httpx.MockTransport(handler)) as client:
        budgeted_post(client, "vision", "https://provider.invalid", per_call_seconds=5)
        with pytest.raises(ExecutionBudgetExceeded):
            budgeted_post(client, "vision", "https://provider.invalid", per_call_seconds=5)
    assert len(requests) == 1


def test_no_frame_is_fail_closed_not_unmetered_network():
    requests = []

    def handler(request):
        requests.append(request)
        return httpx.Response(200)

    with httpx.Client(transport=httpx.MockTransport(handler)) as client:
        with pytest.raises(ExecutionRejected, match="context_required"):
            budgeted_post(client, "llm", "https://provider.invalid", per_call_seconds=5)
    assert requests == []


def test_cancel_before_post_prevents_side_effect():
    ledger, cap = running()
    requests = []

    def handler(request):
        requests.append(request)
        return httpx.Response(200)

    with bind(ledger, cap), httpx.Client(transport=httpx.MockTransport(handler)) as client:
        ledger.cancel_operation("u1", cap.operation_id)
        with pytest.raises(ExecutionRejected):
            budgeted_post(client, "tts", "https://provider.invalid", per_call_seconds=5)
    assert requests == []


def test_cancel_during_network_discards_late_result():
    ledger, cap = running()
    entered, release = Event(), Event()

    def handler(request):
        entered.set()
        assert release.wait(5), "test_release_timeout"
        return httpx.Response(200, json={"late": True})

    def worker():
        with bind(ledger, cap), httpx.Client(transport=httpx.MockTransport(handler)) as client:
            return budgeted_post(client, "tts", "https://provider.invalid", per_call_seconds=5)

    with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=1) as pool:
        future = pool.submit(worker)
        try:
            assert entered.wait(5), "test_enter_timeout"
            ledger.cancel_operation("u1", cap.operation_id)
        finally:
            release.set()
        with pytest.raises(ExecutionRejected):
            future.result(timeout=5)
    assert ledger.operation("u1", cap.operation_id)["calls"] == {"tts": 1}


def test_decoded_response_limit_and_gzip_header_are_correct():
    for limit, should_fail in ((32, False), (2, True)):
        ledger, cap = running()
        transport = httpx.MockTransport(lambda request: httpx.Response(
            200, headers={"Content-Encoding": "gzip"}, content=gzip.compress(b"hello"),
        ))
        with bind(ledger, cap), httpx.Client(transport=transport) as client:
            if should_fail:
                with pytest.raises(ExecutionRejected, match="response_too_large"):
                    budgeted_post(client, "vision", "https://provider.invalid",
                                  per_call_seconds=5, max_response_bytes=limit)
            else:
                response = budgeted_post(client, "vision", "https://provider.invalid",
                                         per_call_seconds=5, max_response_bytes=limit)
                assert response.content == b"hello"
                assert "content-encoding" not in response.headers
```

### 13.5 既有单元/评测入口的迁移约束

新的真实 Provider 调用必须有 frame。第五批 GIF 使用 MockTransport 的真实模型路径测试、State/Planner 的 Provider contract、直接 ASR/TTS 客户端测试应明确创建测试执行上下文，而不是在生产 helper 加“没 frame 就不计数”的分支。

`tests/conftest.py` 的既有 `_force_mock_env()` 字典还需加入 `"MEM0_TELEMETRY": "false"`、`"ANONYMIZED_TELEMETRY": "false"`、`"RAG_ARTIFACT_DIR": ""`、`"RAG_ARTIFACT_SHA256": ""`，确保测试收集阶段早于 SDK import 关闭遥测，且普通离线 smoke 不读取开发者真实知识制品。专用集成测试使用自己的显式连接变量，不把它们放进普通 Mock 默认值。

新增以下通用 fixture 到 `tests/conftest.py`，只供那些显式在测试参数中声明 `provider_execution` 的测试使用，**不要 autouse**，否则会把应该验证“缺资格拒绝”的负例也偷偷授权：

```python
@pytest.fixture
def provider_execution():
    from aiagent.state.execution_context import bind
    from aiagent.state.execution_ledger import ExecutionLedger, LocalAtomic, digest

    ledger = ExecutionLedger(LocalAtomic())
    cap = ledger.begin(user_id="u1", session_id="provider-test", idempotency_key="one",
                       payload_digest=digest("synthetic-provider-test")).capability
    ledger.start(cap)
    with bind(ledger, cap) as frame:
        yield frame
```

第五批 `test_real_service_sends_one_ordered_multiframe_request` 与 `test_provider_rejection_never_retries_as_first_frame` 两个测试参数增加 `provider_execution`。本节其它纯解码/Mock provider 测试不需要它。测试若模拟超过 120 秒，使用显式冻结时钟或预期超时，不把 deadline 调成无限。

独立评测的逐 case/turn 接线见第 28/31 节，管理面维护及旧 Worker 处理见第 32 节。不要给所有评测共用一个无限长 frame，也不要为了大规模 embedding 建库绕过 `rag_embedding` 配额；本批选择有界离线制品和显式关闭旧在线写入口，不把旧任务系统自动重试当作安全恢复。

## 14. 新增 HTTP 幂等准入与交错回归测试

完整新增 `tests/api/test_execution_admission_contract.py`。文件功能：运行真实 `ExecutionRoute`、真实身份 dependency 和 FastAPI 校验，模拟单次有界工作，测试同键重放/不同载荷拒绝、运行中重复请求、取消与迟到返回、unknown 不重跑及不同主体隔离。它不替代真实 MainRunner/Mem0/Voice 整链测试，不能混报。

```python
from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio
import hashlib
import json
from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor
from threading import Event
from types import SimpleNamespace

import pytest
from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, FastAPI
from fastapi.testclient import TestClient
from pydantic import BaseModel
from starlette.responses import JSONResponse

from aiagent.state.execution_context import current
from aiagent.state.execution_ledger import ExecutionLedger, LocalAtomic
from apps.api import execution_route, user_auth
from apps.api.execution_route import ExecutionRoute
from apps.api.routes import executions

TOKENS = {"u1": "C" * 43, "u2": "D" * 43}


class Body(BaseModel):
    user_id: str
    text: str
    session_id: str = "demo"
    mode: str = "normal"


def headers(user="u1", key="one"):
    return {"Authorization": f"Bearer {TOKENS[user]}", "Idempotency-Key": key}


@pytest.fixture
def api(monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.setattr(user_auth.settings, "api_auth_mode", "bearer")
    monkeypatch.setattr(user_auth.settings, "api_auth_token_sha256_map", json.dumps({
        hashlib.sha256(token.encode()).hexdigest(): user for user, token in TOKENS.items()
    }))
    monkeypatch.setattr(user_auth.settings, "enable_local_audio_playback", False)
    monkeypatch.setattr(user_auth.settings, "app_env", "test")
    monkeypatch.setattr(user_auth.cloud_settings, "cloud_mode", False)
    monkeypatch.setattr(user_auth.cloud_settings, "execution_state_mode", "local")
    ledger = ExecutionLedger(LocalAtomic())
    persona_config = SimpleNamespace(model_dump=lambda **kwargs: {"persona_id": "synthetic"})
    manager = SimpleNamespace(get_active_persona=lambda: SimpleNamespace(config=persona_config))
    runtime = SimpleNamespace(dispatcher=SimpleNamespace(execution_ledger=ledger, persona_manager=manager))
    monkeypatch.setattr(execution_route, "get_runtime", lambda: runtime)
    monkeypatch.setattr(executions, "get_runtime", lambda: runtime)
    state = SimpleNamespace(calls=0, entered=Event(), release=Event(), hold=False)
    router = APIRouter(route_class=ExecutionRoute)

    @router.post("/chat")
    async def chat(body: Body):
        frame = current(required=True)

        def work():
            frame.remaining()
            state.calls += 1
            state.entered.set()
            if state.hold:
                assert state.release.wait(5), "test_release_timeout"
            frame.remaining()
            return {"ok": True, "output_id": frame.capability.operation_id, "reply": body.text}

        result = await asyncio.to_thread(work)
        if body.mode == "timeout":
            return JSONResponse(status_code=504, content={"ok": False, "error": "synthetic_timeout"})
        return result

    app = FastAPI(dependencies=[Depends(user_auth.enforce_http_policy)])
    app.include_router(router)
    app.include_router(executions.router)
    with TestClient(app) as client:
        try:
            yield SimpleNamespace(client=client, ledger=ledger, state=state)
        finally:
            state.release.set()


def test_same_key_replays_without_second_work_and_other_payload_conflicts(api):
    body = {"user_id": "u1", "text": "hello"}
    first = api.client.post("/chat", headers=headers(), json=body)
    second_headers = {**headers(), "X-Request-ID": "different-transport-id"}
    second = api.client.post("/chat", headers=second_headers, json=body)
    assert first.status_code == second.status_code == 200
    assert first.json() == second.json()
    assert first.headers["x-operation-id"] == second.headers["x-operation-id"]
    assert second.headers["x-execution-state"] == "completed"
    assert api.state.calls == 1
    conflict = api.client.post("/chat", headers=headers(), json={**body, "text": "changed"})
    assert conflict.status_code == 409
    assert api.state.calls == 1


def test_inflight_duplicate_202_does_not_start_again(api):
    api.state.hold = True
    body = {"user_id": "u1", "text": "hello"}
    with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=1) as pool:
        first = pool.submit(api.client.post, "/chat", headers=headers(), json=body)
        try:
            assert api.state.entered.wait(5)
            duplicate = api.client.post("/chat", headers=headers(), json=body)
            assert duplicate.status_code == 202
            assert duplicate.json()["result_ready"] is False
            assert api.state.calls == 1
        finally:
            api.state.release.set()
        assert first.result(timeout=5).status_code == 200


def test_cancel_during_actual_handler_prevents_late_commit(api):
    api.state.hold = True
    body = {"user_id": "u1", "text": "hello"}
    with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=1) as pool:
        first = pool.submit(api.client.post, "/chat", headers=headers(), json=body)
        try:
            assert api.state.entered.wait(5)
            duplicate = api.client.post("/chat", headers=headers(), json=body)
            operation_id = duplicate.headers["x-operation-id"]
            cancel = api.client.post(f"/executions/{operation_id}/cancel", headers=headers())
            assert cancel.status_code == 200
        finally:
            api.state.release.set()
        assert first.result(timeout=5).status_code == 409
    record = api.ledger.operation("u1", operation_id)
    assert record["status"] == "cancelled" and "result" not in record
    assert api.client.post("/chat", headers=headers(), json=body).status_code == 409
    assert api.state.calls == 1


def test_timeout_marks_unknown_and_never_reexecutes_same_key(api):
    body = {"user_id": "u1", "text": "hello", "mode": "timeout"}
    first = api.client.post("/chat", headers=headers(), json=body)
    assert first.status_code == 504
    operation_id = first.headers["x-operation-id"]
    assert api.ledger.operation("u1", operation_id)["status"] == "unknown"
    retry = api.client.post("/chat", headers=headers(), json=body)
    assert retry.status_code == 409
    assert retry.json()["execution_state"] == "unknown"
    assert api.state.calls == 1


def test_principal_validation_precedes_admission_and_user_results_do_not_mix(api):
    assert api.client.post("/chat", json={"user_id": "u1", "text": "hello"}).status_code == 401
    assert api.client.post("/chat", headers=headers(),
                           json={"user_id": "u2", "text": "hello"}).status_code == 403
    assert api.ledger.snapshot("u1") == {}
    first = api.client.post("/chat", headers=headers(), json={"user_id": "u1", "text": "hello"})
    second = api.client.post("/chat", headers=headers("u2"), json={"user_id": "u2", "text": "hello"})
    assert first.status_code == second.status_code == 200
    assert first.headers["x-operation-id"] != second.headers["x-operation-id"]
    assert api.state.calls == 2


def test_duplicate_idempotency_header_rejected_before_work(api):
    response = api.client.post("/chat", headers=[
        ("Authorization", f"Bearer {TOKENS['u1']}"),
        ("Idempotency-Key", "one"), ("Idempotency-Key", "two"),
    ], json={"user_id": "u1", "text": "hello"})
    assert response.status_code == 400
    assert api.state.calls == 0
```

`mode=timeout` 在这份测试中是**合成 endpoint 行为**，不是 HTTP 生产接口新增的控制参数。它证明原 handler 返回 504 后外层标记 unknown，不证明真实云超时线程已经停止。`hold/release` 则确实让 handler 工作线程停在一个可控点，另一个 HTTP 请求完成取消后才释放，验证迟到检查不会提交。

尚未执行的本段指令：

```powershell
.\.venv\Scripts\python.exe -m pytest tests/unit/test_budgeted_http.py tests/api/test_execution_admission_contract.py -q
```

## 15. LLM、Memory policy 与隐藏摘要外呼

### 15.1 `aiagent/services/llm_service.py`

文件功能：三个 LLM 用途使用同一服务，但预算槽分别为 `llm`、`memory_policy` 与 `summary`。保留第五批真实失败显式抛出的语义，不把生命周期异常包成普通生成失败。

增加 imports，保留第五批的 `GenerationUnavailableError` import：

```python
from aiagent.services.budgeted_http import budgeted_post
from aiagent.state.execution_context import call, raise_if_execution_error
```

在现有 `get_chat_model()` 的 `ChatOpenAI(...)` 构造参数中增加 `max_retries=0,`；其它模型、temperature、max_completion_tokens、端点与 key 的解析保持。该字段是 SDK 客户端配置，不应通过 `.bind(max_retries=0)` 发给 Provider 的 JSON。

完整替换第五批的 `invoke_messages()`：

```python
    def invoke_messages(
        self, messages: Sequence[BaseMessage], fallback_text: str,
        mode: str = "chat", persona_name: str | None = None,
    ) -> str:
        del persona_name
        if self.settings.enable_mock_llm or self.settings.llm_provider == LLMProvider.MOCK:
            return fallback_text
        stage = "memory_policy" if mode == "memory_policy" else "llm"
        try:
            return self.invoke_messages_strict(messages, stage=stage)
        except Exception as exc:
            raise_if_execution_error(exc)
            self.logger.warning("generation_unavailable")
            raise GenerationUnavailableError() from None
```

完整替换 `invoke_messages_strict()`，新增 `_invoke_budgeted_chat_model()`：

```python
    def invoke_messages_strict(
        self, messages: Sequence[BaseMessage], *, stage: str = "llm",
        max_tokens: int | None = None,
    ) -> str:
        if stage not in {"llm", "memory_policy", "summary"}:
            raise ValueError("execution_invalid_llm_purpose")
        if max_tokens is not None and (type(max_tokens) is not int or max_tokens < 1):
            raise ValueError("execution_invalid_max_tokens")
        if self.settings.enable_mock_llm or self.settings.llm_provider == LLMProvider.MOCK:
            raise RuntimeError("strict invocation does not allow mock LLM")
        if self.settings.llm_provider == LLMProvider.LMSTUDIO:
            content = self._invoke_lmstudio(messages, stage=stage, max_tokens=max_tokens)
        else:
            response = call(
                stage,
                lambda timeout: self._invoke_budgeted_chat_model(messages, timeout, max_tokens),
                per_call_seconds=self.settings.llm_timeout_seconds,
            )
            raw = getattr(response, "content", response)
            if not isinstance(raw, (str, list)):
                raise TypeError("llm_unsupported_content_type")
            content = self._normalize_content(raw)
        if not isinstance(content, str) or not content.strip():
            raise RuntimeError("llm_empty_content")
        return content.strip()

    def _invoke_budgeted_chat_model(
        self, messages: Sequence[BaseMessage], timeout: float, max_tokens: int | None,
    ) -> BaseMessage:
        base = self.get_chat_model()
        if not isinstance(base, ChatOpenAI) or base.root_client is None:
            raise RuntimeError("llm_budget_adapter_unsupported")
        # with_options shares the configured transport but not mutable timeout
        # state. model_copy keeps per-call options off the cached base model.
        root = base.root_client.with_options(timeout=timeout, max_retries=0)
        updates: dict[str, Any] = {
            "root_client": root, "client": root.chat.completions,
            "request_timeout": timeout, "max_retries": 0,
        }
        if max_tokens is not None:
            updates["max_tokens"] = min(max_tokens, self.settings.llm_max_tokens)
        model = base.model_copy(update=updates)
        return model.invoke(list(messages))
```

这里沿用 LangChain ChatOpenAI 的消息转换与返回类型。没有把 SDK 完全换成手写 JSON，也没有在并发请求里修改缓存模型字段。`root_client/client/request_timeout/max_tokens` 字段在本机安装的 `langchain_openai/chat_models/base.py` 中可见；SDK 升级后必须跑下面的真实适配器 contract，不能拿本次源码观察证明未来版本兼容。`with_options()` 的 root 共享底层 transport，所以不要在每次调用后 `root.close()` 关掉其它并发请求正在使用的连接。

完整替换 `_invoke_lmstudio()`：

```python
    def _invoke_lmstudio(
        self, messages: Sequence[BaseMessage], *, stage: str = "llm",
        max_tokens: int | None = None,
    ) -> str:
        _, base_url, _ = self._resolve_provider_config()
        payload = {
            "model": self.settings.llm_model,
            "messages": [self._to_openai_message(message) for message in messages],
            "temperature": self.settings.llm_temperature,
        }
        if max_tokens is not None:
            payload["max_tokens"] = min(max_tokens, self.settings.llm_max_tokens)
        with httpx.Client(timeout=self.settings.llm_timeout_seconds, trust_env=False) as client:
            response = budgeted_post(
                client, stage, f"{base_url}/chat/completions",
                per_call_seconds=self.settings.llm_timeout_seconds, json=payload,
                headers={
                    "Content-Type": "application/json",
                    "Authorization": f"Bearer {self.settings.lmstudio_api_key or 'lm-studio'}",
                },
            )
        response.raise_for_status()
        data = response.json()
        choices = data.get("choices", []) if isinstance(data, dict) else []
        if not choices or not isinstance(choices[0], dict):
            raise RuntimeError("llm_no_choices")
        message = choices[0].get("message", {})
        content = message.get("content", "") if isinstance(message, dict) else ""
        normalized = self._normalize_content(content)
        if not normalized:
            raise RuntimeError("llm_empty_content")
        return normalized
```

SDK 路径当前只计数并设置 timeout/retry；JSON 响应字节上限尚不等同于第 13 节的 httpx 原始路径，因为 SDK 自己读取响应。本批不能声称“所有 SDK 响应都限制到 4 MiB”；Provider max_tokens 和网络出口策略仍是不同层的限制。必要的强进程/内存隔离属于部署验收，不用一个类字段冒充完成。

### 15.2 `aiagent/graphs/llm_graph.py` 的摘要模型

文件功能：当前 SummarizationNode 直接拿 `get_chat_model().bind(...)`，会绕过服务层预算。保留官方摘要节点与 token_counter，只替换其模型适配器。

新增 import：

```python
from langchain_core.runnables import RunnableLambda
```

完整替换 `_create_summarize_node()`：

```python
    def _create_summarize_node(self):
        if self.llm_service.settings.enable_mock_llm or self.llm_service.settings.llm_provider == LLMProvider.MOCK:
            return self._mock_summarize_node

        def summarize(messages):
            # SummarizationNode expects an AI message with .content, not a string.
            return AIMessage(content=self.llm_service.invoke_messages_strict(
                messages, stage="summary", max_tokens=128,
            ))

        return SummarizationNode(
            token_counter=count_tokens_approximately,
            model=RunnableLambda(summarize),
            max_tokens=1024,
            max_tokens_before_summary=768,
            max_summary_tokens=256,
        )
```

本机 langmem 摘要实现通过 `model.invoke(...).content` 使用模型，因此返回 AIMessage。Mock 仍使用原有窗口裁剪，不宣称真实摘要质量。摘要超时/预算/撤销错误不做未计数重试；主 LLM 的一个槽位与摘要的一个槽位相互独立，但都计入总 24 次上限。

`MemoryPolicyLLMService.decide_write()` 已传 `mode="memory_policy"`，无需再包一次 call。**不要**在该函数外再消耗 memory_policy 预算，否则同一次外呼会被计算两次。

### 15.3 完整新增 `tests/unit/test_llm_execution_budget.py`

文件功能：运行实际 ChatOpenAI 与 OpenAI SDK，但通过本地 MockTransport 捕获发送；验证 stage 分离、关闭隐藏重试、timeout 和缓存模型不被修改。不是只 Mock `invoke_messages_strict()`。

```python
from __future__ import annotations

from types import SimpleNamespace

import httpx
import pytest
from langchain_core.messages import HumanMessage
from langchain_openai import ChatOpenAI
from openai import InternalServerError

from aiagent.graphs.llm_graph import LLMRunner
from aiagent.services.llm_service import LLMService
from aiagent.state.execution_context import bind
from aiagent.state.execution_ledger import ExecutionLedger, LocalAtomic, digest
from config.providers import LLMProvider


def setup_service(handler):
    transport = httpx.Client(transport=httpx.MockTransport(handler), trust_env=False)
    base = ChatOpenAI(
        model="synthetic-model", api_key="synthetic-key",
        base_url="https://provider.invalid/v1", http_client=transport,
        max_retries=0, timeout=300, max_completion_tokens=200,
    )
    settings = SimpleNamespace(
        enable_mock_llm=False, llm_provider=LLMProvider.OPENAI,
        llm_timeout_seconds=300.0, llm_max_tokens=200,
    )
    service = LLMService(settings)
    service._chat_model = base
    ledger = ExecutionLedger(LocalAtomic())
    cap = ledger.begin(user_id="u1", session_id="s1", idempotency_key="one",
                       payload_digest=digest("one")).capability
    ledger.start(cap)
    return transport, service, base, ledger, cap


def success():
    return httpx.Response(200, json={
        "id": "synthetic-completion", "object": "chat.completion", "created": 1,
        "model": "synthetic-model",
        "choices": [{"index": 0, "message": {"role": "assistant", "content": "合成回复"},
                     "finish_reason": "stop"}],
        "usage": {"prompt_tokens": 1, "completion_tokens": 1, "total_tokens": 2},
    })


def test_real_sdk_stage_budgets_and_cached_model_are_isolated():
    requests = []

    def handler(request):
        requests.append(request)
        return success()

    transport, service, base, ledger, cap = setup_service(handler)
    try:
        with bind(ledger, cap):
            assert service.invoke_messages([HumanMessage(content="hello")], "fallback") == "合成回复"
            assert service.invoke_messages([HumanMessage(content="policy")], "fallback",
                                           mode="memory_policy") == "合成回复"
        record = ledger.operation("u1", cap.operation_id)
        assert record["calls"] == {"llm": 1, "memory_policy": 1}
        assert len(requests) == 2
        assert all(0 < request.extensions["timeout"]["read"] <= 120 for request in requests)
        assert base.request_timeout == 300
        assert base.max_tokens == 200
    finally:
        transport.close()


def test_sdk_503_is_one_actual_request_not_implicit_retry():
    requests = []

    def handler(request):
        requests.append(request)
        return httpx.Response(503, json={"error": {"message": "synthetic", "type": "server_error"}})

    transport, service, _, ledger, cap = setup_service(handler)
    try:
        with bind(ledger, cap), pytest.raises(InternalServerError):
            service.invoke_messages_strict([HumanMessage(content="hello")])
        assert len(requests) == 1
        assert ledger.operation("u1", cap.operation_id)["calls"] == {"llm": 1}
    finally:
        transport.close()


def test_real_summarization_node_uses_summary_not_chat_budget():
    requests = []

    def handler(request):
        requests.append(request)
        return success()

    transport, service, _, ledger, cap = setup_service(handler)
    try:
        node = LLMRunner._create_summarize_node(SimpleNamespace(llm_service=service))
        messages = [HumanMessage(id=f"synthetic-{index}", content="可概括的合成历史。" * 100)
                    for index in range(12)]
        with bind(ledger, cap):
            result = node.invoke({"messages": messages, "context": {}})
        assert len(requests) == 1
        assert ledger.operation("u1", cap.operation_id)["calls"] == {"summary": 1}
        assert result["context"]
    finally:
        transport.close()
```

第三个测试故意提供足够长的合成消息触发真正的官方摘要节点，不调用任何真实模型。安装版本对超长单消息处理不同、未触发外呼时应检查输入规模与摘要触发条件，不应把 `len(requests)==1` 删掉来让测试通过。第一个测试捕获两个真实 SDK 请求并检查缓存模型未改变，第二个断言 503 的真实发送次数；MockTransport 只隔离网络。

未执行验证指令：

```powershell
.\.venv\Scripts\python.exe -m pytest tests/unit/test_llm_execution_budget.py tests/unit/test_budgeted_http.py tests/api/test_execution_admission_contract.py -q
```

独立评测中 `aiagent/evaluation/adapters.py` 与 `knowledge/evaluation/agent_quality.py` 的 strict 调用也会受本契约影响。逐 case 执行资格装配尚未给出前，不宣称现有整套评测已经兼容本批；不在服务层加匿名旁路规避它。

## 16. Mem0 物理适配器：只暂存，不自行决定可见性

### 16.1 `aiagent/memory/mem0_memory.py`

文件仍负责 Mem0 配置和物理读写，第 17 节的 facade 才是 Runtime 唯一公开的长期记忆对象。不要把原 `Mem0LongTermMemory` 留给部分 API 继续访问，否则旧的 `get_all/search/update` 就会绕过账本。第五批的安全正文审批继续保留，不重新实现敏感过滤器。

import 区增加：

```python
from aiagent.state.execution_ledger import checked_name
```

在 `class Mem0LongTermMemory:` 前新增以下 SDK 薄子类，并把构造函数中唯一的 `self.memory = Memory.from_config(config)` 精确替换为 `self.memory = VisibilityScopedMem0.from_config(config)`：

```python
class VisibilityScopedMem0(Memory):
    def _compute_entity_boosts(self, query_entities, filters):
        # The installed SDK strips visibility filters on its separate entity store.
        # Do not let unpublished/retired entities influence ranking or trigger hidden embeds.
        del query_entities, filters
        return {}
```

**当前 SDK 的额外事实：** 本机 Mem0 发行版 2.0.1 的普通 search 除主查询 embedding，还可能对抽取实体逐个 embedding，并访问只按 user/agent 过滤的 entity store；它的 broad catch 会吞预算异常。因此本批显式禁用这个尚未版本隔离的排名加成，保留已有主语义检索与 BM25 组合，不重新写一套搜索算法。`infer=False` 的新记录本来就不进入自动实体抽取写入路径，不能拿旧 entity store 当当前事实。此限制是明确的隐私协议变化，恢复需另行证明实体数据也具有可见版本和真实预算。

将第五批给出的 `add_approved()` 完整替换为以下三个方法，保留第五批已拒绝的 `add_turn()`。旧 CRUD 方法可以留在物理类内兼容离线迁移，但不得再装配到 Runtime 或 MemoryRunner；新 facade 不通过 `__getattr__` 自动暴露它们。

```python
    def add_approved(self, payload: ApprovedMemoryWrite) -> dict[str, Any]:
        del payload
        return {"ok": False, "status": "blocked", "reason": "published_memory_facade_required"}

    def _stage_approved(self, payload: ApprovedMemoryWrite, *, visibility_tag: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
        verify_approved(payload)
        checked_name(visibility_tag)
        if (len(visibility_tag) != 64
                or any(char not in "0123456789abcdef" for char in visibility_tag)):
            raise ValueError("memory_invalid_visibility_tag")
        if self.enable_graph:
            raise RuntimeError("memory_graph_requires_separate_approved_adapter")
        metadata = {**approved_metadata(payload), "visibility_tag": visibility_tag}
        with self._lock:
            raw = self.memory.add(
                messages=[{"role": "user", "content": payload.text}],
                user_id=payload.user_id, agent_id=payload.agent_id,
                metadata=metadata, infer=False,
            )
        items = raw.get("results") if isinstance(raw, dict) else None
        if (not isinstance(items, list) or len(items) != 1
                or raw.get("ok") is False or raw.get("status") in {"failed", "error"}):
            raise RuntimeError("memory_stage_outcome_unknown")
        item = items[0]
        if (not isinstance(item, dict) or item.get("event") != "ADD"
                or not isinstance(item.get("id"), str) or not item["id"]
                or item.get("memory") != payload.text):
            raise RuntimeError("memory_stage_outcome_unknown")
        return {"physical_id": item["id"]}

    def _search_visible_physical(
        self, *, user_id: str, agent_id: str, query: str,
        tags: list[str], limit: int,
    ) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
        if not tags:
            return []
        if len(tags) > 1000 or not 1 <= limit <= 100:
            raise ValueError("memory_search_bounds_invalid")
        if self.enable_graph:
            raise RuntimeError("memory_graph_requires_separate_approved_adapter")
        with self._lock:
            raw = self.memory.search(
                query=query, top_k=limit, threshold=0.0, rerank=False,
                filters={"user_id": user_id, "agent_id": agent_id,
                         "visibility_tag": {"in": tags}},
            )
        items = raw.get("results", []) if isinstance(raw, dict) else raw
        if not isinstance(items, list) or len(items) > limit:
            raise RuntimeError("memory_search_result_invalid")
        return [item for item in items if isinstance(item, dict)]

    def _get_physical(self, physical_id: str) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
        with self._lock:
            raw = self.memory.get(physical_id)
        return raw if isinstance(raw, dict) else None
```

**解释：** `_stage_approved()` 每次生成新物理记录，绝不调用 `memory.update()`；同正文去重仍由审批前的 intake 处理，不能依赖 Mem0 再自由推断。模型只接收第五批准许的正文，metadata 只额外加入服务端随机 tag。`_search_visible_physical()` 在向量数据库执行候选过滤，不是先取 top-k 再在 API 丢弃旧记录；否则大量旧版本或孤儿会占满 top-k，导致有效记忆永远召回不到。

当前安装的 Mem0 `search()` 使用 `top_k`、`filters`、`threshold`、`rerank`；Qdrant 适配器把 `{"in": [...]}` 转为 MatchAny。不要把它改成另一版本示例里的 `limit` 或顶层 `user_id`。第 21 节提供真实适配器调用捕获测试，实际 Qdrant 契约仍要在专用环境验证。

### 16.2 新增 `aiagent/memory/budgeted_embedding.py`

文件功能：替换现有 Mem0 OpenAI-compatible embedding 实例，在**实际一次 SDK 请求**处计数、禁止隐藏重试并校验向量响应。`siliconflow` 在现有构造中已经归一化为 `openai`；本批不假装所有 embedding provider 都已适配。

```python
from __future__ import annotations

import math
from typing import Any

from mem0.embeddings.openai import OpenAIEmbedding

from aiagent.state.execution_context import call


class BudgetedMemoryEmbedding:
    def __init__(self, original: OpenAIEmbedding, *, expected_dims: int) -> None:
        if not isinstance(original, OpenAIEmbedding):
            raise ValueError("memory_embedder_requires_budget_adapter")
        if type(expected_dims) is not int or not 1 <= expected_dims <= 65_536:
            raise ValueError("memory_embedding_dimensions_invalid")
        self.client = original.client
        self.config = original.config
        self.pass_dimensions = original._pass_dimensions_to_api
        self.expected_dims = expected_dims

    def _request(self, texts: list[str]) -> list[list[float]]:
        if not texts or len(texts) > 100 or any(not isinstance(text, str) for text in texts):
            raise ValueError("memory_embedding_input_invalid")
        if sum(len(text.encode("utf-8")) for text in texts) > 64 * 1024:
            raise ValueError("memory_embedding_input_too_large")
        kwargs: dict[str, Any] = {
            "input": [text.replace("\n", " ") for text in texts],
            "model": self.config.model, "encoding_format": "float",
        }
        if self.pass_dimensions:
            kwargs["dimensions"] = self.config.embedding_dims

        def request(timeout):
            client = self.client.with_options(timeout=timeout, max_retries=0)
            return client.embeddings.create(**kwargs)

        response = call("embedding", request, per_call_seconds=20)
        items = sorted(response.data, key=lambda item: item.index)
        if [item.index for item in items] != list(range(len(texts))):
            raise RuntimeError("memory_embedding_indices_invalid")
        vectors = []
        for item in items:
            vector = item.embedding
            if (not isinstance(vector, list) or len(vector) != self.expected_dims
                    or any(type(value) not in {int, float} or not math.isfinite(value) for value in vector)):
                raise RuntimeError("memory_embedding_vector_invalid")
            vectors.append(vector)
        return vectors

    def embed(self, text: str, memory_action=None) -> list[float]:
        if memory_action not in {None, "add", "search", "update"}:
            raise ValueError("memory_embedding_action_invalid")
        return self._request([text])[0]

    def embed_batch(self, texts, memory_action="add") -> list[list[float]]:
        if memory_action not in {None, "add", "search", "update"}:
            raise ValueError("memory_embedding_action_invalid")
        if not isinstance(texts, (list, tuple)) or len(texts) > 100:
            raise ValueError("memory_embedding_batch_invalid")
        return self._request(list(texts)) if texts else []
```

**解释：** 不直接改 SDK 缓存 client，不关闭共享 transport。每个 `_request` 原子占用一次 embedding 预算，失败不退额度，默认 SDK 重试关闭。`memory_action` 只兼容当前 Mem0 调用签名，不成为客户端授权。预期维度显式传既有 `MEMORY_EMBEDDING_DIMS`，不盲信 SDK 在没传 embedding_dims 时自行填的 1536；当前项目仅在向量库配置了维度，SDK factory 并没有自动把它同步给 embedder。`dimensions` 是否发送继续沿用原 SDK 的声明，不能为了校验 1024 维就向不支持 dimensions 参数的兼容 Provider 强行发送。响应缺行、重复 index、NaN/Infinity 或维度错都不能进入向量库。

这是请求点预算，不等于 embedding 全响应的原始字节硬上限；SDK 已读入响应后才校验向量。Qdrant 的连接超时也不是本模块替代的内容。服务端总 deadline 与最终账本 CAS 仍负责拒绝迟到可见发布，不能用它声称强行杀死已经运行的网络线程。

## 17. 新增 `aiagent/memory/published_memory.py`

文件功能：统一所有用户面 Memory 读写及控制，分开 API 的稳定逻辑 ID、Mem0 物理 ID、随机可见 tag。编辑/合并不原地修改；删除只先撤下引用并登记物理清理，不谎称后端数据已经擦除。禁用隐藏现有引用但不删除，重新启用只能恢复此前仍存在的引用，不恢复旧任务资格。

新增全文：

```python
from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import math
import secrets
import time
from typing import Any

from aiagent.memory.approved_write import (
    ApprovalError, ApprovedMemoryWrite, approve_plan, approved_metadata, verify_approved,
)
from aiagent.memory.mem0_memory import MemoryHit
from aiagent.memory.memory_layers import (
    build_memory_snapshot, infer_memory_layer, normalize_memory_record, sort_memory_records,
)
from aiagent.memory.memory_safety import MemorySafetyFilter
from aiagent.schemas.memory import (
    MemoryCategory, MemoryImportance, MemoryLayer, MemoryStorePlan,
)
from aiagent.state.execution_context import bind, current
from aiagent.state.execution_ledger import ExecutionLedger, ExecutionRejected, checked_name, digest


def text_digest(text: str) -> str:
    return hashlib.sha256(text.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()


class MemoryInputError(ValueError):
    pass


class PublishedLongTermMemory:
    def __init__(self, physical, ledger: ExecutionLedger, *, available: bool) -> None:
        self._physical = physical
        self.ledger = ledger
        self.available = available
        self.safety_filter = MemorySafetyFilter()

    def _scope(self, user_id: str, agent_id: str, *, writing: bool = False):
        checked_name(user_id)
        checked_name(agent_id)
        frame = current(required=writing)
        if frame is not None:
            if frame.ledger is not self.ledger or frame.capability.user_id != user_id:
                raise ExecutionRejected("execution_memory_scope_mismatch")
            frame.remaining(memory=True)
        return frame

    @staticmethod
    def _limit(limit: int | None, default: int = 200, maximum: int = 1000) -> int:
        if limit is None:
            return default
        if type(limit) is not int or not 1 <= limit <= maximum:
            raise MemoryInputError("memory_limit_invalid")
        return limit

    def _read_ref(self, logical_id: str, ref: dict[str, Any], user_id: str, agent_id: str):
        raw = self._physical._get_physical(ref["physical_id"])
        if not isinstance(raw, dict):
            raise RuntimeError("memory_visible_record_missing")
        metadata = raw.get("metadata")
        if not isinstance(metadata, dict):
            raise RuntimeError("memory_visible_record_invalid")
        # Top-level/metadata owner disagreement is also invalid, not a fallback.
        for field, expected in (("user_id", user_id), ("agent_id", agent_id)):
            values = [value for value in (raw.get(field), metadata.get(field)) if value is not None]
            if not values or any(value != expected for value in values):
                raise RuntimeError("memory_visible_record_owner_mismatch")
        text = raw.get("memory")
        if (raw.get("id") != ref["physical_id"] or not isinstance(text, str)
                or metadata.get("visibility_tag") != ref["visibility_tag"]
                or text_digest(text) != ref["content_digest"]):
            raise RuntimeError("memory_visible_record_integrity_mismatch")
        # Use only the ledger's approved metadata. Never resurrect old SDK hints/relations.
        approved = dict(ref["metadata"])
        return normalize_memory_record({
            "id": logical_id, "memory": text, "metadata": approved, "relations": [],
            "created_at": approved.get("created_at"),
            "updated_at": approved.get("updated_at") or approved.get("created_at"),
        })

    def _validate_read(self, user_id, agent_id, view) -> None:
        self.ledger.assert_memory_view(user_id, agent_id, view)
        self._scope(user_id, agent_id)

    def list_records(self, user_id: str, agent_id: str = "yzl", limit: int | None = 200):
        count = self._limit(limit)
        view = self.ledger.memory_view(user_id, agent_id)
        if not self.available or not view["enabled"]:
            return []
        self._scope(user_id, agent_id)
        # Sort references before I/O; do not read 1000 bodies for a 20-item page.
        refs = sorted(view["refs"].items(), key=lambda item: (
            bool(item[1]["metadata"].get("pinned")),
            {"high": 3, "medium": 2, "low": 1}.get(item[1]["metadata"].get("importance"), 0),
            item[1]["metadata"].get("created_at", ""), item[0],
        ), reverse=True)[:count]
        records = [self._read_ref(key, ref, user_id, agent_id) for key, ref in refs]
        self._validate_read(user_id, agent_id, view)
        return sort_memory_records(records)

    def get_all(self, user_id: str, agent_id: str = "yzl", limit: int | None = None):
        return [record.model_dump(mode="json")
                for record in self.list_records(user_id, agent_id, limit)]

    def get_memory_record(self, memory_id: str, user_id: str, agent_id: str = "yzl"):
        view = self.ledger.memory_view(user_id, agent_id)
        ref = view["refs"].get(memory_id)
        if not self.available or not view["enabled"] or ref is None:
            return None
        self._scope(user_id, agent_id)
        record = self._read_ref(memory_id, ref, user_id, agent_id)
        self._validate_read(user_id, agent_id, view)
        return record

    def search(self, user_id: str, query: str, agent_id: str = "yzl", limit: int = 6):
        count = self._limit(limit, maximum=100)
        if not isinstance(query, str) or not query.strip() or len(query) > 16_384:
            raise MemoryInputError("memory_query_invalid")
        view = self.ledger.memory_view(user_id, agent_id)
        if not self.available or not view["enabled"] or not view["refs"]:
            return []
        frame = self._scope(user_id, agent_id, writing=True)
        assert frame is not None
        raw = self._physical._search_visible_physical(
            user_id=user_id, agent_id=agent_id, query=query,
            tags=[ref["visibility_tag"] for ref in view["refs"].values()], limit=count,
        )
        by_physical = {ref["physical_id"]: key for key, ref in view["refs"].items()}
        hits = []
        seen = set()
        for item in raw:
            logical_id = by_physical.get(item.get("id"))
            if logical_id is None or logical_id in seen:
                raise RuntimeError("memory_search_returned_unpublished_or_duplicate_record")
            seen.add(logical_id)
            ref = view["refs"][logical_id]
            # Reload body by exact ID, independent of search response extensions.
            record = self._read_ref(logical_id, ref, user_id, agent_id)
            score = item.get("score")
            if score is not None and (type(score) not in {int, float} or not math.isfinite(score)):
                raise RuntimeError("memory_search_score_invalid")
            hits.append(MemoryHit(
                id=logical_id, memory=record.memory, score=score, metadata=record.metadata,
                relations=[], pinned=record.pinned, pinned_at=record.pinned_at,
                created_at=record.created_at, updated_at=record.updated_at,
            ))
        self._validate_read(user_id, agent_id, view)
        return hits

    def list_layer_records(self, user_id, layer, agent_id="yzl", limit=200):
        target = MemoryLayer(layer)
        view = self.ledger.memory_view(user_id, agent_id)
        if not self.available or not view["enabled"]:
            return []
        self._scope(user_id, agent_id)
        count = self._limit(limit)
        refs = [(key, ref) for key, ref in view["refs"].items()
                if infer_memory_layer(ref["metadata"].get("category"), ref["metadata"]) == target]
        # The subject quota is 1000; sorting this bounded layer is deterministic.
        records = [self._read_ref(key, ref, user_id, agent_id) for key, ref in refs]
        self._validate_read(user_id, agent_id, view)
        return sort_memory_records(records)[:count]

    def list_profile_memories(self, user_id, agent_id="yzl", limit=20):
        return self.list_layer_records(user_id, MemoryLayer.PROFILE, agent_id, limit)

    def list_pinned_records(self, user_id, agent_id="yzl", limit=8):
        count = self._limit(limit, default=8)
        view = self.ledger.memory_view(user_id, agent_id)
        if not self.available or not view["enabled"]:
            return []
        self._scope(user_id, agent_id)
        refs = [(key, ref) for key, ref in view["refs"].items() if ref["metadata"].get("pinned") is True]
        records = [self._read_ref(key, ref, user_id, agent_id) for key, ref in refs]
        self._validate_read(user_id, agent_id, view)
        return sort_memory_records(records)[:count]

    def get_snapshot(self, user_id, agent_id="yzl", limit=200):
        return build_memory_snapshot(user_id=user_id, agent_id=agent_id,
                                     records=self.list_records(user_id, agent_id, limit))

    def _stage(self, payload: ApprovedMemoryWrite) -> dict[str, Any]:
        verify_approved(payload)
        frame = self._scope(payload.user_id, payload.agent_id, writing=True)
        assert frame is not None
        if not self.available:
            raise RuntimeError("memory_backend_unavailable")
        tag = secrets.token_hex(32)
        # Register before external I/O, so even an unknown write has a cleanup tag.
        self.ledger.register_memory_stage(frame.capability, agent_id=payload.agent_id, tag=tag)
        stored = self._physical._stage_approved(payload, visibility_tag=tag)
        physical_id = stored.get("physical_id") if isinstance(stored, dict) else None
        if not isinstance(physical_id, str) or not physical_id:
            raise RuntimeError("memory_stage_result_invalid")
        frame.remaining(memory=True)
        return {
            "physical_id": physical_id, "agent_id": payload.agent_id,
            "visibility_tag": tag, "content_digest": text_digest(payload.text),
            "metadata": {**approved_metadata(payload), "pinned": False, "pinned_at": ""},
        }

    def add_turn(self, *args, **kwargs):
        return {"ok": False, "status": "blocked", "reason": "approved_payload_required"}

    def add_approved(self, payload: ApprovedMemoryWrite):
        verify_approved(payload)
        frame = self._scope(payload.user_id, payload.agent_id, writing=True)
        assert frame is not None
        if not self.available:
            return {"ok": False, "status": "skipped", "reason": "memory_backend_unavailable"}
        if frame.memory_only:
            # No original messages are queued; waiting never extends the parent's deadline.
            while True:
                seconds = frame.remaining(memory=True)
                record = self.ledger.operation(payload.user_id, frame.capability.operation_id)
                if record is not None and record["status"] == "completed":
                    break
                time.sleep(min(0.02, seconds))
        logical_id = "m12:" + digest([frame.capability.operation_id, payload.agent_id, payload.signature])
        existing = self.ledger.visible_memory(payload.user_id, payload.agent_id).get(logical_id)
        if existing is not None:
            if existing["content_digest"] != text_digest(payload.text):
                raise ExecutionRejected("execution_memory_id_conflict")
            return {"ok": True, "status": "stored", "memory_id": logical_id}
        if logical_id in frame.staged_memories:
            return {"ok": True, "status": "staged", "memory_id": logical_id}
        reference = self._stage(payload)
        if frame.memory_only:
            self.ledger.publish_memory(frame.capability, logical_id=logical_id, reference=reference)
            status = "stored"
        else:
            if len(frame.staged_memories) >= 8:
                raise ExecutionRejected("execution_too_many_staged_memories")
            frame.staged_memories[logical_id] = reference
            status = "staged"
        return {"ok": True, "status": status, "memory_id": logical_id}

    def delete_memory(self, memory_id: str, user_id: str, agent_id: str = "yzl"):
        view = self.ledger.memory_view(user_id, agent_id)
        ref = view["refs"].get(memory_id)
        if ref is None:
            return {"ok": False, "status": "not_found", "reason": "memory_not_found"}
        removed = self.ledger.memory_control(
            user_id, delete_ids=[memory_id], agent_id=agent_id,
            expected_versions={memory_id: digest(ref)},
        )
        return {"ok": True, "status": "deleted", "memory_id": memory_id,
                "deleted_count": len(removed), "physical_deleted": False, "cleanup_pending": True}

    def delete_all(self, user_id: str, agent_id: str = "yzl"):
        removed = self.ledger.memory_control(user_id, clear_agent=agent_id, agent_id=agent_id)
        return {"ok": True, "status": "cleared", "user_id": user_id, "agent_id": agent_id,
                "deleted_count": len(removed), "physical_deleted": False, "cleanup_pending": bool(removed)}

    def delete_layer(self, user_id, layer, agent_id="yzl", limit=1000):
        self._limit(limit, default=1000)
        target = MemoryLayer(layer)
        deleted = self.ledger.delete_memory_layer(
            user_id, agent_id=agent_id, layer=target.value,
        )
        return {"ok": True, "status": "cleared", "user_id": user_id, "agent_id": agent_id,
                "layer": target.value, "deleted_count": len(deleted), "deleted_ids": deleted,
                "errors": [], "physical_deleted": False, "cleanup_pending": bool(deleted)}

    def _edit_payload(self, text, category, importance, source, user_id, agent_id):
        guard = self.safety_filter.evaluate(text)
        plan = MemoryStorePlan(
            should_store=guard.allowed, status="ready" if guard.allowed else "blocked",
            memory_text=guard.redacted_text, category=category, importance=importance,
            guard=guard, source=source,
        )
        return approve_plan(plan, user_id=user_id, agent_id=agent_id)

    def _replace(
        self, *, user_id, agent_id, ids, text, category, importance, layer, pinned, source,
    ):
        if current() is not None:
            raise ExecutionRejected("execution_memory_edit_requires_control_entry")
        if not self.available:
            return {"ok": False, "status": "failed", "reason": "memory_backend_unavailable"}
        if not isinstance(text, str) or not 1 <= len(text.strip()) <= 2000:
            raise MemoryInputError("memory_edit_text_invalid")
        if not ids or len(ids) > 32 or len(set(ids)) != len(ids):
            raise MemoryInputError("memory_edit_ids_invalid")
        view = self.ledger.memory_view(user_id, agent_id)
        if not view["enabled"]:
            raise ExecutionRejected("execution_memory_disabled")
        if any(key not in view["refs"] for key in ids):
            return {"ok": False, "status": "not_found", "reason": "memory_not_found"}
        primary = view["refs"][ids[0]]
        previous = primary["metadata"]
        category = MemoryCategory(category or previous.get("category", "other"))
        importance = MemoryImportance(importance or previous.get("importance", "medium"))
        try:
            payload = self._edit_payload(text.strip(), category, importance, source, user_id, agent_id)
        except ApprovalError:
            return {"ok": False, "status": "blocked", "reason": "memory_edit_not_approved"}
        admission = self.ledger.begin(
            user_id=user_id, session_id="memory-control", idempotency_key=secrets.token_hex(16),
            payload_digest=digest([ids, payload.signature]), policy_version="v12-06-memory-edit",
        )
        cap = admission.capability
```
