```python
        self.ledger.start(cap)
        try:
            with bind(self.ledger, cap):
                self.ledger.assert_memory_view(user_id, agent_id, view)
                reference = self._stage(payload)
                should_pin = (any(view["refs"][key]["metadata"].get("pinned") is True for key in ids)
                              if pinned is None else pinned)
                if type(should_pin) is not bool:
                    raise MemoryInputError("memory_pin_invalid")
                next_metadata = reference["metadata"]
                next_metadata.update({
                    "memory_layer": MemoryLayer(layer).value if layer else infer_memory_layer(category).value,
                    "created_at": previous.get("created_at", payload.created_at),
                    "updated_at": payload.created_at, "pinned": should_pin,
                    "pinned_at": payload.created_at if should_pin else "",
                })
                self.ledger.replace_memories(
                    cap, agent_id=agent_id, primary_id=ids[0], reference=reference,
                    expected_versions={key: digest(view["refs"][key]) for key in ids},
                )
            # The successful CAS revoked this control cap as well; do not check it again.
            committed_record = normalize_memory_record({
                "id": ids[0], "memory": payload.text, "metadata": reference["metadata"],
                "relations": [], "created_at": reference["metadata"]["created_at"],
                "updated_at": payload.created_at,
            })
            return {"ok": True, "status": "merged" if len(ids) > 1 else "updated",
                    "user_id": user_id, "agent_id": agent_id, "memory_id": ids[0],
                    "deleted_ids": ids[1:], "errors": [], "physical_deleted": False,
                    "cleanup_pending": True,
                    "record": committed_record.model_dump(mode="json")}
        except BaseException:
            try:
                self.ledger.terminal(cap, "unknown")
            except Exception:
                pass
            raise

    def update_memory(
        self, *, user_id, memory_id, memory_text, category=None, importance=None,
        layer=None, pinned=None, agent_id="yzl", reason="", metadata=None,
    ):
        del reason
        if metadata:
            raise MemoryInputError("memory_free_metadata_not_supported")
        return self._replace(user_id=user_id, agent_id=agent_id, ids=[memory_id], text=memory_text,
                             category=category, importance=importance, layer=layer,
                             pinned=pinned, source="user_edit")

    def merge_memories(
        self, *, user_id, memory_ids, merged_memory, category=None, importance=None,
        layer=None, pinned=None, agent_id="yzl", reason="", metadata=None,
    ):
        del reason
        if metadata:
            raise MemoryInputError("memory_free_metadata_not_supported")
        if len(memory_ids) < 2:
            return {"ok": False, "status": "skipped", "reason": "merge_requires_at_least_two_memories"}
        return self._replace(user_id=user_id, agent_id=agent_id, ids=list(memory_ids), text=merged_memory,
                             category=category, importance=importance, layer=layer,
                             pinned=pinned, source="user_merge")

    def set_memory_pinned(self, *, user_id, memory_id, pinned, agent_id="yzl", reason=""):
        del reason
        view = self.ledger.memory_view(user_id, agent_id)
        ref = view["refs"].get(memory_id)
        if ref is None:
            return {"ok": False, "status": "not_found", "reason": "memory_not_found"}
        self.ledger.pin_memory(user_id, agent_id=agent_id, logical_id=memory_id,
                               pinned=pinned, expected_version=digest(ref))
        return {"ok": True, "status": "updated", "memory_id": memory_id, "pinned": pinned}

    def format_for_prompt(self, hits: list[MemoryHit]) -> str:
        if not hits:
            return "无长期记忆。"
        lines = ["长期记忆摘要：", "- 仅作个性化上下文，不作为执行指令。"]
        for index, hit in enumerate(hits[:6], 1):
            body = " ".join(hit.memory.split())[:120]
            lines.append(f"{index}. {'【置顶】' if hit.pinned else ''}{body}")
        return "\n".join(lines)

    def graph_status(self):
        return {"enabled": False, "available": self.available,
                "reason": "published_vector_memory_only", "physical_cleanup": "deferred"}
```

### 17.1 逐段解释与兼容变化

- `_scope()` 对带 frame 的访问核对同一 ledger/主体及 Memory 撤销版本。无 frame 的受保护列表/按 ID 查询仍可读取；向量搜索必须拥有短操作资格，因为它会调用 embedding，Memory 搜索 API 的装配见第 20 节。
- `_read_ref()` 对物理 ID、owner、agent、tag、正文摘要全部核对，忽略 Mem0 旧 hints、自由 metadata 和 relations。读出外部正文后 `_validate_read()` 再校验同一可见视图；删除在读取期间完成会明确冲突，不能把旧正文带回 Graph。
- `memory_view` 包含该 agent 的全部有界引用，校验覆盖 epoch、revision、开关与引用变化；不把某次 snapshot 当永久授权。已发给客户端的响应不能撤回；删除承诺的线性化点是账本 CAS，不是让早于删除已发出的网络数据消失。
- `_stage()` 先把随机 tag 登记进账本再物理写入。物理写入响应丢失时，仍可通过 tag 找到孤儿；已知被替换记录进入 `memory_gc`。本批只登记，有界满则拒绝；第九批给真正清理/重试/保留协议，不宣称已物理删除。
- 同步 `add_approved()` 返回 `staged`，引用放在当前 frame；第 2/6/7 节的 `finish(..., memories=...)` 将 Memory、历史、输出、资源一次 CAS 发布。回复失败或取消后只剩不可见物理暂存，不会留下可检索的“成功记忆”。
- 后台 frame 没有父 frame 的可变容器；等原回复 `completed` 后才物理写入/发布，仍受原 deadline、generation 和 memory revision 限制。下一回合已开始时旧任务即失效，不偷偷把 deadline 重置为新 120 秒。
- `_replace()` 在专用 `memory-control` 会话给编辑/合并授予短操作；控制入口不与聊天同 session 抢占。替换 CAS 同时验证所有来源的完整引用版本、撤下来源、发布新正文并撤销旧读取者。不能用“先改 primary、再逐条 delete”实现合并。
- 用户自由 `metadata` 非空现在明确拒绝，`reason` 不进入物理 metadata；`layer/category/importance/pinned` 仍是强类型受控字段。不要把旧正文埋在 `merge_reason` 或 `memory_hint` 里。逻辑 ID 编辑后不变；物理 ID 只在服务端引用/清理账本中存在，不作为 API 记忆 ID。
- 删除/禁用/编辑/置顶都会清该主体旧短期上下文、重放正文与音频引用；不清其它用户的全局历史对象。置顶只改账本受控字段，不重新做 embedding。主体开关仍对所有 agent 生效，删除只删除指定 agent 的条目。
- 关闭 Memory 时列表/快照/搜索为空，但允许按逻辑 ID 删除与清空，不要求为了删除先重新启用。编辑/合并需显式启用。物理 Memory 服务不可用时 facade 不透传 Null 后端的“成功删除”。

**结果返回的边界：** 编辑返回的是此次成功提交的受控记录快照，不在提交后再次读取再假定它必然存在；另一删除可紧接着完成，因此不能保证响应发送时该记录仍可见。分层删除在事务回调中选择该层所有当前引用，不受客户端 limit 截断，不漏掉“列表读取后、删除 CAS 前”刚发布的同层记录。外部调用失败/Redis 响应丢失不得自动重跑编辑。

## 18. `MemoryRunner`：后台传不可变资格，同步不误报 stored

修改位置为 `aiagent/graphs/memory_graph.py`，以第五批第 6 节实施结果为基准，不把当前源码中旧的原始问答后台投递恢复回来。

import 区增加：

```python
from aiagent.state.execution_context import bind, current, raise_if_execution_error
from aiagent.state.execution_ledger import Capability, ExecutionRejected
```

第五批 `_write_after_reply()` 中 `elif background:` 的整个分支替换如下。只传批准正文和不可变 cap，不传整个 frame、不使用 `copy_context()` 共享可变字典，也不把原始问答重新放进队列：

```python
                elif background:
                    frame = current(required=True)
                    assert frame is not None
                    if frame.ledger is not self.memory.ledger or frame.capability.user_id != user_id:
                        raise ExecutionRejected("execution_memory_scope_mismatch")
                    frame.remaining(memory=True)
                    result = self.write_dispatcher.submit(
                        self._commit_approved, payload=payload,
                        execution_capability=frame.capability,
                    )
```

完整替换第五批的 `_commit_approved()`，新增 `_commit_in_frame()`：

```python
    def _commit_approved(
        self, *, payload: ApprovedMemoryWrite, execution_capability: Capability | None = None,
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        verify_approved(payload)
        if execution_capability is not None:
            if execution_capability.user_id != payload.user_id:
                raise ExecutionRejected("execution_memory_scope_mismatch")
            # Each worker owns a fresh frame. Completed parents are allowed for Memory only.
            with bind(self.memory.ledger, execution_capability, memory_only=True):
                return self._commit_in_frame(payload)
        frame = current(required=True)
        assert frame is not None
        if frame.ledger is not self.memory.ledger or frame.capability.user_id != payload.user_id:
            raise ExecutionRejected("execution_memory_scope_mismatch")
        return self._commit_in_frame(payload)

    def _commit_in_frame(self, payload: ApprovedMemoryWrite) -> dict[str, Any]:
        try:
            verify_approved(payload)
            frame = current(required=True)
            assert frame is not None
            frame.remaining(memory=True)
            result = self.memory.add_approved(payload)
            if (not isinstance(result, dict)
                    or result.get("status") not in {"stored", "staged", "skipped", "failed"}
                    or (result.get("status") in {"stored", "staged"} and result.get("ok") is not True)):
                result = {"ok": False, "status": "failed", "reason": "memory_backend_result_invalid"}
        except Exception as exc:
            raise_if_execution_error(exc)
            result = {"ok": False, "status": "failed", "reason": "memory_backend_failed"}
        # Staged is not a successful visible write. Do not emit an audit status of stored here.
        self._audit_safe(user_id=payload.user_id, agent_id=payload.agent_id,
                         status=str(result.get("status", "failed")))
        return result
```

第五批 `_audit_safe()` 的 `allowed` 集合增加 `"staged"`；`MemoryWriteAudit.status` 当前是字符串，无需修改公共 schema。队列 `_run()` 仍只把 `stored + ok=True` 记为 done；后台路径不会返回 staged，因此不改成“所有 ok=True 都 done”。`staged` 审计是准备状态，不是已落库成功证据；第九批再给提交事件审计/持久恢复协议。

**检索异常不能被降级吞掉。** 以下三个位置，每个 `except Exception as exc:` 后第一句都加同一行：`retrieve_before_reply()`、`_retrieve_node()` 的向量检索分支、`_retrieve_node()` 的置顶查询分支。第五批其它节点已有的 shared 错误重抛保留；不要另写只检查三个具体子类的较窄版本。

```python
            raise_if_execution_error(exc)
```

置顶查询普通异常的 `pinned_error = str(exc)` 改为固定 `pinned_error = "memory_pinned_unavailable"`。向量查询普通异常仍可降级为无记忆，但 `ExecutionRejected/Expired/BudgetExceeded/SharedStateUnavailable` 必须上抛，使整个旧操作停止，不能用“记忆可选”绕过撤销。

**同步提交失败的真实含义：** 如果 Mem0 物理调用成功但随后 TTS 失败，frame 的 staged 引用不会 finish；物理数据存在但无可见 tag。后台在等待父回复期间若父变成 unknown/cancelled/failed 或被下一回合换代，`frame.remaining(memory=True)` 直接拒绝，甚至不开始新 embedding 请求。已经发送出去的写入若迟到，最后 publish 也会拒绝，stage tag 留给第九批清理。

## 19. 共享偏好与显式迁移

### 19.1 新增 `aiagent/memory/shared_preferences.py`

文件功能：实现既有 preference_store 的 `get/set/is_enabled` 接口，但共享真源为同一个用户账本；旧 JSON 不再在每个 Worker 各自读取/覆盖。默认禁用，不根据 Settings 的默认 true 推定用户同意。

```python
from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import UTC, datetime

from aiagent.schemas.memory import MemoryPreferenceState
from aiagent.state.execution_ledger import ExecutionLedger, checked_name


class SharedMemoryPreferenceStore:
    def __init__(self, ledger: ExecutionLedger) -> None:
        self.ledger = ledger

    def get(self, user_id: str, agent_id: str = "yzl") -> MemoryPreferenceState:
        checked_name(agent_id)
        root = self.ledger.snapshot(user_id)
        preference = root.get("memory_preference", {})
        updated = preference.get("updated_ms")
        return MemoryPreferenceState(
            user_id=user_id, agent_id=agent_id,
            long_term_enabled=root.get("memory_enabled", False),
            source=preference.get("source", "default_disabled"),
            updated_at=(datetime.fromtimestamp(updated / 1000, UTC).isoformat()
                        if type(updated) is int else None),
            reason="subject_scope_shared_preference",
        )

    def set(self, *, user_id, long_term_enabled, agent_id="yzl", reason=""):
        del reason
        self.ledger.memory_control(user_id, enabled=long_term_enabled, agent_id=agent_id)
        return self.get(user_id, agent_id)

    def is_enabled(self, user_id: str, agent_id: str = "yzl") -> bool:
        return self.get(user_id, agent_id).long_term_enabled
```

解释：`set()` 在同一 CAS 中写偏好和撤销屏障，不是先写 JSON 再取消。当前协议是**主体级开关**，接口仍保留 agent_id 以兼容；返回 reason 明示作用域，不返回用户传入的自由 reason。禁用不是清空，启用也不会恢复已撤销的旧 cap。

### 19.2 新增 `aiagent/memory/preference_migration.py`

文件功能：严格解析旧 JSON 显式选择并生成迁移决定；相同主体多个 agent 意见冲突按禁用处理，不替用户猜测。迁移只导入偏好，**不自动认领既有物理记忆**。没有引用和 tag 的旧 Mem0 记录需要另行受控重审、重新批准与暂存，禁止第一次访问者认领。

```python
from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import json

from aiagent.state.execution_ledger import ExecutionLedger, checked_name

MAX_LEGACY_BYTES = 4 * 1024 * 1024


def _pairs(pairs):
    result = {}
    for key, value in pairs:
        if key in result:
            raise ValueError("memory_preference_duplicate_json_key")
        result[key] = value
    return result


def parse_legacy_preferences(content: bytes) -> tuple[str, dict[str, bool]]:
    if not content or len(content) > MAX_LEGACY_BYTES:
        raise ValueError("memory_preference_file_size_invalid")
    try:
        rows = json.loads(content.decode("utf-8-sig"), object_pairs_hook=_pairs,
                          parse_constant=lambda value: (_ for _ in ()).throw(ValueError(value)))
    except (UnicodeError, ValueError, RecursionError):
        raise ValueError("memory_preference_file_invalid") from None
    if not isinstance(rows, dict) or len(rows) > 10_000:
        raise ValueError("memory_preference_rows_invalid")
    choices: dict[str, list[bool]] = {}
    for key, row in rows.items():
        if not isinstance(row, dict):
            raise ValueError("memory_preference_row_invalid")
        user = checked_name(row.get("user_id"))
        agent = checked_name(row.get("agent_id"))
        enabled = row.get("long_term_enabled")
        if key != f"{agent}:{user}" or type(enabled) is not bool:
            raise ValueError("memory_preference_row_invalid")
        choices.setdefault(user, []).append(enabled)
    return hashlib.sha256(content).hexdigest(), {user: all(values) for user, values in choices.items()}


def import_preference(ledger: ExecutionLedger, user_id: str, enabled: bool, source_digest: str) -> str:
    if type(enabled) is not bool or len(source_digest) != 64:
        raise ValueError("memory_preference_migration_invalid")

    def mutate(root, now):
        existing = root["memory_preference"]
        if existing:
            # Never overwrite a newer user choice; repeated same-source import is a no-op.
            return "already_imported" if existing.get("source_digest") == source_digest else "preserved"
        root["memory_enabled"] = enabled
        root["memory_preference"] = {
            "source": "migration", "updated_ms": now, "source_digest": source_digest,
        }
        ledger._revoke_memory_readers(root, now)
        return "imported"
    return ledger.transaction(user_id, mutate)
```

### 19.3 新增 `scripts/migrate_memory_preferences_v12.py`

文件功能：默认只检查，显式 `--apply` 才写共享 Redis 偏好；无需启动整个 Runtime 或 Provider。只打印合成计数，不打印用户 ID、旧 reason、正文、密钥或 Redis URL。离线 local 模式的 LocalAtomic 无跨进程持久性，故此脚本拒绝 local；开发时使用 API 显式启用，或在同一 Runtime 内调用上面的纯迁移函数。

```python
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import json
from pathlib import Path

from aiagent.memory.preference_migration import MAX_LEGACY_BYTES, import_preference, parse_legacy_preferences
from aiagent.state.execution_ledger import ExecutionLedger, RedisAtomic
from apps.core.bootstrap import build_shared_state_store


def main() -> int:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    parser.add_argument("--source", required=True, type=Path)
    parser.add_argument("--apply", action="store_true")
    args = parser.parse_args()
    with args.source.open("rb") as source:
        content = source.read(MAX_LEGACY_BYTES + 1)
    source_digest, decisions = parse_legacy_preferences(content)
    if not args.apply:
        print(json.dumps({"mode": "dry_run", "subjects": len(decisions),
                          "enabled": sum(decisions.values()),
                          "disabled": len(decisions) - sum(decisions.values())}))
        return 0
    shared = build_shared_state_store()
    if shared is None:
        raise SystemExit("Shared Redis mode required; local state cannot survive this process")
    ledger = ExecutionLedger(RedisAtomic(shared.redis), prefix=shared.prefix)
    counts: dict[str, int] = {}
    for user, enabled in decisions.items():
        status = import_preference(ledger, user, enabled, source_digest)
        counts[status] = counts.get(status, 0) + 1
    print(json.dumps({"mode": "applied", "counts": counts}))
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())
```

未执行的迁移指令，先停止旧 Worker、备份受保护数据与旧偏好文件，在你自己的私有终端配置 Redis，再逐项执行。不要把真实 source 文件加入 Git：

```powershell
.\.venv\Scripts\python.exe -m scripts.migrate_memory_preferences_v12 --source $env:AIAGENT_LEGACY_MEMORY_PREFERENCES
.\.venv\Scripts\python.exe -m scripts.migrate_memory_preferences_v12 --source $env:AIAGENT_LEGACY_MEMORY_PREFERENCES --apply
```

**迁移边界：** 每个主体原子，整个多主体文件不是跨用户事务；中断后可重跑，同源已导入不重复撤销，已有用户选择不覆盖。损坏 JSON、重复 key、缺失归属或非 bool 一律停止，不把它们当空文件后默认启用。批量导入前先人工审阅 dry-run 数量；主体级开关相对旧 agent 级 JSON 是明确协议变化。

## 20. Runtime、搜索资格与 Memory HTTP 错误边界接线

### 20.1 `apps/core/bootstrap.py`：唯一账本，唯一公开 facade

import 区删除不再使用的 `MemoryPreferenceStore`，增加：

```python
from aiagent.memory.budgeted_embedding import BudgetedMemoryEmbedding
from aiagent.memory.published_memory import PublishedLongTermMemory
from aiagent.memory.shared_preferences import SharedMemoryPreferenceStore
from aiagent.state.execution_ledger import ExecutionLedger, LocalAtomic, RedisAtomic
```

在现有 `shared_state_store = build_shared_state_store()` 后、`LLMRunner` 和 Memory 创建前紧接增加：

```python
    if cloud_settings.execution_state_mode == "redis" and shared_state_store is None:
        raise SharedStateUnavailableError("execution_shared_backend_required")
    execution_ledger = ExecutionLedger(
        RedisAtomic(shared_state_store.redis) if shared_state_store is not None else LocalAtomic(),
        prefix=shared_state_store.prefix if shared_state_store is not None else cloud_settings.redis_prefix,
    )
```

这处检查封住既有 `build_shared_state_store()` 中 `fail_closed=False + 空 Redis URL` 返回 None 的路径。本批不再允许已选择 Redis 却静默运行 LocalAtomic。`execution_state_mode=local` 仍是单进程开发模式，和“Agent 部署在本机或云主机”不是同一维度；在本机同样可以配置 Redis 验证多 Worker。

在 Memory 的现有 try 之前增加 `memory_available = False`。在 `Mem0LongTermMemory(...)` 创建完毕、`capabilities.mark_available("memory", ...)` **之前**增加：

```python
        if settings.memory_vector_provider.strip().lower() != "qdrant":
            raise ValueError("published_memory_requires_verified_qdrant_filter")
        long_term_memory.memory.embedding_model = BudgetedMemoryEmbedding(
            long_term_memory.memory.embedding_model, expected_dims=settings.memory_embedding_dims,
        )
        memory_available = True
```

保留第五批的禁图检查和安全降级记录。任一初始化步骤失败时仍降级为 Null，并把 `memory_available` 保持 False；**不要在 except 中恢复旧未预算 embedder**。

整个 try/except 之后，用下面代码完整替换原 `memory_preferences = MemoryPreferenceStore(...)` 创建块：

```python
    long_term_memory = PublishedLongTermMemory(
        long_term_memory, execution_ledger, available=memory_available,
    )
    memory_preferences = SharedMemoryPreferenceStore(execution_ledger)
```

在后面的现有 `EventDispatcher(...)` 构造中增加 `execution_ledger=execution_ledger,`。保留第 8 节的 `llm_runner.execution_ledger = dispatcher.execution_ledger`；这样三个对象引用同一实例，而不是创建三个同名本地状态仓库。`MemoryRunner(memory=...)` 与最终 `CoreRuntime(long_term_memory=...)` 继续传这里的变量，**不能把原物理对象另存一个 public 字段让某条 API 绕过 facade**。

### 20.2 类型与默认依赖，不保留旧 JSON 旁路

`aiagent/graphs/memory_graph.py` 中导入 `MemoryHit` 保留，删除同一行的 `Mem0LongTermMemory`，改为导入 `PublishedLongTermMemory`。删除 `MemoryPreferenceStore` 导入，改导入 `SharedMemoryPreferenceStore`。对应构造签名两个参数精确替换为：

```python
            memory: PublishedLongTermMemory,
            preference_store: SharedMemoryPreferenceStore | None = None,
```

构造赋值 `self.preference_store = ...` 替换为：

```python
        self.preference_store = preference_store or SharedMemoryPreferenceStore(memory.ledger)
```

`apps/core/runtime.py` 的 `Mem0LongTermMemory` import 和 `long_term_memory: Mem0LongTermMemory` 注解改为 `PublishedLongTermMemory` 对应 import/类型。保留既有参数名，调用方不必改名。其它没有访问 facade 的纯 intake/deduper 仍只导入 MemoryHit，不引入全局账本依赖。

### 20.3 `apps/core/runtime.py`：删除不再清全局历史，搜索授予短资格

import 区增加：

```python
import secrets

from aiagent.state.execution_context import bind, current
from aiagent.state.execution_ledger import digest
```

完整替换 `clear_user_memories()`、`delete_user_memory_item()`：

```python
    def clear_user_memories(self, user_id: str) -> dict:
        # Shared reference revocation already clears only this subject's visible contexts.
        result = self.long_term_memory.delete_all(user_id=user_id)
        return {**result, "user_id": user_id, "context_reset_scope": "subject"}

    def delete_user_memory_item(self, user_id: str, memory_id: str) -> dict:
        return self.long_term_memory.delete_memory(memory_id=memory_id, user_id=user_id)
```

不再执行 `self.conversation_state.clear()`，也不再先取前 1000 条列表判断归属后调用无 user 的删除。当前主体各会话的可见 checkpoint/history 已由账本原子撤销；孤立物理 saver 清理由第九批负责，不在本方法同步删除另一用户的进程视图。

现有 `search_memories()` **只改名**为 `_search_memories_in_execution()`，原参数、分层过滤、profile 匹配和结果构造全部保留。再新增以下完整 wrapper，API 路由函数名不变：

```python
    def search_memories(self, user_id: str, query: str, limit: int = 10, layer: str | None = None) -> dict:
        if current() is not None:
            return self._search_memories_in_execution(user_id, query, limit, layer)
        ledger = self.long_term_memory.ledger
        admission = ledger.begin(
            user_id=user_id, session_id="memory-query", idempotency_key=secrets.token_hex(16),
            payload_digest=digest({"query": query, "limit": limit, "layer": layer}),
            deadline_seconds=30, policy_version="v12-06-memory-query",
        )
        cap = admission.capability
        ledger.start(cap)
        try:
            with bind(ledger, cap):
                result = self._search_memories_in_execution(user_id, query, limit, layer)
                # This internal control operation is not an HTTP result cache.
                ledger.finish(cap, result={"status": "searched"})
                return result
        except BaseException:
            try:
                ledger.terminal(cap, "unknown")
            except Exception:
                pass
            raise
```

`get_memory_stats()` 的 `profile.enabled=True` 改为：

```python
                "enabled": self.memory_runner.get_user_preference(user_id=user_id).long_term_enabled,
```

控制/搜索会占用账本 operation 配额，达到 256 条会明确拒绝；第九批清理前需按容量限制试运行，不把它当无限服务。每次搜索随机内部幂等键，不宣称 GET 重试可从缓存复用，也不把查询原文存入日志。

### 20.4 新增 `apps/api/memory_route.py`

文件功能：让未被 ExecutionRoute 包装的 Memory 控制/查询接口同样正确映射共享冲突和断连；不对删除、置顶等无外呼动作创建多余的聊天 frame。用户输入错误与 SDK 普通异常分开，后者不回显路径、Token 或响应原文。

```python
from __future__ import annotations

import json

from fastapi import HTTPException
from fastapi.exceptions import RequestValidationError
from fastapi.routing import APIRoute
from starlette.responses import JSONResponse

from aiagent.memory.published_memory import MemoryInputError
from aiagent.state.shared_state import SharedStateError
from apps.api.shared_state_errors import shared_state_error_response


class MemoryControlRoute(APIRoute):
    def get_route_handler(self):
        original = super().get_route_handler()

        async def handler(request):
            try:
                return await original(request)
            except (HTTPException, RequestValidationError):
                raise
            except SharedStateError as exc:
                response = shared_state_error_response(exc)
                body = (json.loads(bytes(response.body)) if response is not None
                        else {"ok": False, "error": "memory_shared_state_unavailable"})
                body.update(retryable=False, automatic_retry_allowed=False)
                return JSONResponse(
                    status_code=response.status_code if response is not None else 503, content=body,
                )
            except MemoryInputError as exc:
                return JSONResponse(status_code=400, content={"ok": False, "error": str(exc)})
            except Exception:
                return JSONResponse(status_code=503, content={
                    "ok": False, "error": "memory_backend_unavailable", "automatic_retry_allowed": False,
                })

        return handler
```

核对：当前项目共享错误模块实际位于 `apps/api/shared_state_errors.py`；第 6 节已使用同一 helper。不要导入不存在的 `apps.api.errors`。FastAPI 的 `RequestValidationError` 仍交原应用 422 handler，不被最后的 broad catch 改成 503。

在 `apps/api/routes/memory.py` import 区增加 `MemoryControlRoute`，把 `router = APIRouter()` 改成：

```python
from apps.api.memory_route import MemoryControlRoute

router = APIRouter(route_class=MemoryControlRoute)
```

第五批的全局身份 dependency 保持，不能因 facade 会检查 user 就删认证。编辑/合并的受控外呼失败应为 503，不得被 `ok_response(result={ok:false,status:failed})` 包成成功；两条路由在取得 `result` 后、处理 not_found 之前分别增加：

```python
    if result.get("status") == "failed":
        return error_message_response(stage="memory_control", error="memory_backend_unavailable", status_code=503)
```

**语义变化通知：** profile.enabled 从硬编码改为真实共享开关；`deleted/cleared` 代表逻辑撤下，`physical_deleted=False` 与 `cleanup_pending` 必须保留。不要为维持旧测试把它伪改成物理擦除成功。旧 `.env` 的 `MEMORY_LONG_TERM_DEFAULT_ENABLED=true` 和 `MEMORY_PREFERENCES_PATH` 不再是在线真源，后续配置同步会标为迁移兼容参数，而不是悄悄继续生效。

### 20.5 完整替换 `apps/core/runtime_registry.py`

文件功能：只保护单进程 Runtime 的创建，不把它当分布式锁。异步 Voice 首请求会在工作线程初始化，普通聊天/健康接口也可能同时访问；必须防止两份 Runtime 各创建一个 LocalAtomic，或重复绑定同一 Voice 仓储。Redis 多进程一致性仍由执行账本负责。

```python
from __future__ import annotations

from threading import RLock

from apps.core.bootstrap import build_runtime
from apps.core.runtime import CoreRuntime

_runtime: CoreRuntime | None = None
_runtime_error: str | None = None
_runtime_lock = RLock()


def get_runtime() -> CoreRuntime:
    global _runtime, _runtime_error
    with _runtime_lock:
        if _runtime is not None:
            return _runtime
        try:
            candidate = build_runtime()
        except Exception as exc:
            _runtime_error = type(exc).__name__
            raise
        _runtime = candidate
        _runtime_error = None
        return candidate


def get_runtime_error() -> str | None:
    with _runtime_lock:
        return _runtime_error


def reset_runtime() -> None:
    global _runtime, _runtime_error
    with _runtime_lock:
        _runtime = None
        _runtime_error = None
```

解释：只有成功构造的 candidate 才成为真源；失败不切到备用内存实现，错误诊断只保留类型，不回显含凭据/路径的 `repr(exc)`。`reset_runtime()` 仍只供测试/显式运维，不能当作用户取消或自动修复；它不自动关闭资源，调用者须先停任务并释放 write_dispatcher/saver。生产不在有请求时调用 reset。

新增 `tests/unit/test_execution_runtime_registry.py`，完整代码：

```python
from __future__ import annotations

from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor
from threading import Barrier, Lock

from apps.core import runtime_registry as registry


def test_concurrent_first_access_constructs_one_runtime(monkeypatch):
    registry.reset_runtime()
    candidate = object()
    lock = Lock()
    barrier = Barrier(4)
    builds = []

    def build():
        with lock:
            builds.append(1)
        return candidate

    def get():
        barrier.wait(timeout=5)
        return registry.get_runtime()

    monkeypatch.setattr(registry, "build_runtime", build)
    try:
        with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=4) as pool:
            futures = [pool.submit(get) for _ in range(4)]
            assert all(future.result(timeout=5) is candidate for future in futures)
        assert builds == [1]
    finally:
        registry.reset_runtime()
```

## 21. Memory 单元、回归与正反对照测试

以下测试文件均为**待你手动新增**，本次没有写入实际 tests 目录，也没有执行。测试用合成批准偏好和独立内存对象；不访问真实用户记忆、模型或 Qdrant。

### 21.1 新增 `tests/unit/test_published_memory.py`

文件功能：直接测试实际 facade 和实际账本的同步提交、后台资格、编辑/合并、删除/禁用/置顶交错、越权、正文篡改、可见 tag 和 Null 降级。物理桩只替代外部 Mem0，不替代账本规则或 facade 的判断。

```python
from __future__ import annotations

import copy
from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor
from dataclasses import replace
from threading import Event

import pytest

from aiagent.graphs.memory_graph import MemoryRunner
from aiagent.memory.approved_write import ApprovalError, approve_plan, approved_metadata
from aiagent.memory.memory_safety import MemorySafetyFilter
from aiagent.memory.memory_write_dispatcher import MemoryWriteDispatcher
from aiagent.memory.published_memory import MemoryInputError, PublishedLongTermMemory
from aiagent.schemas.memory import MemoryCategory, MemoryImportance, MemoryStorePlan
from aiagent.state.execution_context import bind, call, current
from aiagent.state.execution_ledger import ExecutionLedger, ExecutionRejected, LocalAtomic, digest


class PhysicalMemory:
    def __init__(self):
        self.rows = {}
        self.stages = []
        self.searches = []
        self.after_stage = None
        self.after_get = None

    def _stage_approved(self, payload, *, visibility_tag):
        physical_id = f"physical-{len(self.stages) + 1}"
        self.rows[physical_id] = {
            "id": physical_id, "memory": payload.text, "user_id": payload.user_id,
            "agent_id": payload.agent_id,
            "metadata": {**approved_metadata(payload), "visibility_tag": visibility_tag},
        }
        self.stages.append((payload, visibility_tag, current(required=True)))
        if self.after_stage is not None:
            self.after_stage()
        return {"physical_id": physical_id}

    def _get_physical(self, physical_id):
        result = copy.deepcopy(self.rows.get(physical_id))
        if self.after_get is not None:
            callback, self.after_get = self.after_get, None
            callback()
        return result

    def _search_visible_physical(self, *, user_id, agent_id, query, tags, limit):
        self.searches.append({"user_id": user_id, "agent_id": agent_id,
                              "query": query, "tags": list(tags), "limit": limit})
        return [dict(row, score=0.9) for row in self.rows.values()
                if row["user_id"] == user_id and row["agent_id"] == agent_id
                and row["metadata"]["visibility_tag"] in tags][:limit]


def payload(text="用户喜欢无糖茶", user="u1", agent="yzl"):
    guard = MemorySafetyFilter().evaluate(text)
    return approve_plan(MemoryStorePlan(
        should_store=True, status="ready", category=MemoryCategory.PREFERENCE,
        importance=MemoryImportance.MEDIUM, memory_text=guard.redacted_text,
        guard=guard, source="chat_turn",
    ), user_id=user, agent_id=agent)


def running(ledger, *, user="u1", session="s1", key="k1"):
    cap = ledger.begin(user_id=user, session_id=session, idempotency_key=key,
                       payload_digest=digest([user, session, key])).capability
    ledger.start(cap)
    return cap


@pytest.fixture
def memory():
    ledger = ExecutionLedger(LocalAtomic())
    ledger.memory_control("u1", enabled=True)
    physical = PhysicalMemory()
    return PublishedLongTermMemory(physical, ledger, available=True), physical, ledger


def seed(facade, ledger, text="用户喜欢无糖茶", *, key="seed", agent="yzl"):
    cap = running(ledger, session="seed-session", key=key)
    with bind(ledger, cap) as frame:
        result = facade.add_approved(payload(text, agent=agent))
        assert result["status"] == "staged"
        ledger.finish(cap, result={"output_id": key}, memories=frame.staged_memories)
    return result["memory_id"]


def test_sync_memory_is_invisible_until_same_reply_commit(memory):
    facade, physical, ledger = memory
    cap = running(ledger)
    with bind(ledger, cap) as frame:
        result = facade.add_approved(payload())
        assert result["status"] == "staged"
        assert physical.rows and facade.list_records("u1") == []
        ledger.finish(cap, result={"output_id": "out"}, memories=frame.staged_memories,
                       checkpoint="physical-checkpoint", history_pair=("question", "answer"))
    record = facade.get_memory_record(result["memory_id"], "u1")
    assert record.memory == "用户喜欢无糖茶"
    assert record.id.startswith("m12:") and not record.id.startswith("physical-")
    state = ledger.snapshot("u1")
    assert state["sessions"]["s1"]["checkpoint"] == "physical-checkpoint"
    assert not state["memory_staging"]


@pytest.mark.parametrize("terminal", ["failed", "unknown", "cancelled"])
def test_unsuccessful_reply_never_publishes_staged_memory(memory, terminal):
    facade, physical, ledger = memory
    cap = running(ledger)
    with bind(ledger, cap) as frame:
        facade.add_approved(payload())
        if terminal == "cancelled":
            ledger.cancel_operation("u1", cap.operation_id)
        else:
            ledger.terminal(cap, terminal)
        with pytest.raises(ExecutionRejected):
            ledger.finish(cap, result={}, memories=frame.staged_memories)
    assert facade.list_records("u1") == []
    assert len(physical.rows) == 1
    assert len(ledger.snapshot("u1")["memory_staging"]) == 1


def test_real_memoryrunner_background_uses_new_frame_and_waits_for_commit(memory):
    facade, physical, ledger = memory
    runner = MemoryRunner.__new__(MemoryRunner)
    runner.memory = facade
    audited = []
    runner._audit_safe = lambda **kwargs: audited.append(kwargs["status"])
    queue = MemoryWriteDispatcher(max_workers=1, max_pending=2)
    cap = running(ledger)
    try:
        with bind(ledger, cap) as parent:
            parent.checkpoint = "parent-only"
            queued = queue.submit(runner._commit_approved, payload=payload(), execution_capability=cap)
            assert queued["status"] == "queued"
            assert not ledger.visible_memory("u1", "yzl")
            ledger.finish(cap, result={"output_id": "out"})
        assert queue.drain(timeout=3)
        assert queue.status()["last"]["status"] == "done"
        worker_frame = physical.stages[0][2]
        assert worker_frame is not parent and worker_frame.memory_only
        assert worker_frame.staged_objects is not parent.staged_objects
        assert worker_frame.staged_memories is not parent.staged_memories
        assert worker_frame.checkpoint == "" and parent.checkpoint == "parent-only"
        assert audited == ["stored"] and len(facade.list_records("u1")) == 1
    finally:
        queue.shutdown(wait=True)


@pytest.mark.parametrize("revoke", ["disable", "new_turn", "cancel"])
def test_background_rejection_before_external_write(memory, revoke):
    facade, physical, ledger = memory
    runner = MemoryRunner.__new__(MemoryRunner)
    runner.memory = facade
    runner._audit_safe = lambda **kwargs: None
    cap = running(ledger)
    if revoke != "cancel":
        ledger.finish(cap, result={})
    if revoke == "disable":
        ledger.memory_control("u1", enabled=False)
        ledger.memory_control("u1", enabled=True)
    elif revoke == "new_turn":
        running(ledger, key="successor")
    else:
        ledger.cancel_operation("u1", cap.operation_id)
    with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=1) as pool:
        future = pool.submit(runner._commit_approved, payload=payload(), execution_capability=cap)
        with pytest.raises(ExecutionRejected):
            future.result(timeout=3)
    assert not physical.stages and not facade.list_records("u1")


@pytest.mark.parametrize("control", ["delete", "disable", "clear"])
def test_late_physical_success_cannot_publish_after_control(memory, control):
    facade, physical, ledger = memory
    original = seed(facade, ledger)
    cap = running(ledger, key="late")

    def revoke():
        if control == "delete":
            facade.delete_memory(original, "u1")
        elif control == "clear":
            facade.delete_all("u1")
        else:
            ledger.memory_control("u1", enabled=False)
            ledger.memory_control("u1", enabled=True)

    physical.after_stage = revoke
    with bind(ledger, cap):
        with pytest.raises(ExecutionRejected):
            facade.add_approved(payload("用户习惯晚间散步"))
    records = facade.list_records("u1")
    assert all(record.memory != "用户习惯晚间散步" for record in records)
    assert len(records) == (1 if control == "disable" else 0)
    assert len(physical.rows) == 2  # Logical privacy != physical erasure.


def test_edit_copy_on_write_keeps_id_drops_old_body_and_free_metadata(memory):
    facade, physical, ledger = memory
    logical_id = seed(facade, ledger)
    old_ref = copy.deepcopy(ledger.visible_memory("u1", "yzl")[logical_id])
    physical.rows[old_ref["physical_id"]]["metadata"]["memory_hint"] = "OLD_PRIVATE_CANARY"
    physical.rows[old_ref["physical_id"]]["relations"] = [{"source": "OLD_PRIVATE_CANARY"}]
    result = facade.update_memory(user_id="u1", memory_id=logical_id,
                                  memory_text="用户喜欢茉莉花茶", pinned=True)
    new_ref = ledger.visible_memory("u1", "yzl")[logical_id]
    assert result["ok"] and result["memory_id"] == logical_id
    assert new_ref["physical_id"] != old_ref["physical_id"]
    assert new_ref["visibility_tag"] != old_ref["visibility_tag"]
    assert physical.rows[old_ref["physical_id"]]["memory"] == "用户喜欢无糖茶"
    assert "OLD_PRIVATE_CANARY" not in str(result)
    assert facade.list_pinned_records("u1")[0].memory == "用户喜欢茉莉花茶"
    assert ledger.snapshot("u1")["memory_gc"] and result["physical_deleted"] is False


def test_merge_replaces_all_sources_in_one_cas_and_rejects_stale_editor(memory):
    facade, physical, ledger = memory
    first = seed(facade, ledger, key="first")
    second = seed(facade, ledger, "用户喜欢清淡饮食", key="second")
    stale = running(ledger, session="old-chat", key="old")
    result = facade.merge_memories(user_id="u1", memory_ids=[first, second],
                                    merged_memory="用户喜欢无糖茶和清淡饮食")
    assert result["status"] == "merged" and result["deleted_ids"] == [second]
    assert set(ledger.visible_memory("u1", "yzl")) == {first}
    assert len(ledger.snapshot("u1")["memory_gc"]) == 2
    with pytest.raises(ExecutionRejected):
        ledger.finish(stale, result={"output_id": "late"})


def test_delete_during_read_rejects_stale_snapshot(memory):
    facade, physical, ledger = memory
    logical_id = seed(facade, ledger)
    physical.after_get = lambda: facade.delete_memory(logical_id, "u1")
    with pytest.raises(ExecutionRejected, match="memory_view_changed"):
        facade.get_memory_record(logical_id, "u1")
    assert facade.get_memory_record(logical_id, "u1") is None


def test_search_filter_excludes_orphans_before_top_k_and_pin_is_not_embedding(memory):
    facade, physical, ledger = memory
    logical_id = seed(facade, ledger)
    current_ref = ledger.visible_memory("u1", "yzl")[logical_id]
    physical.rows["orphan"] = {
        "id": "orphan", "user_id": "u1", "agent_id": "yzl", "memory": "ORPHAN_CANARY",
        "metadata": {"visibility_tag": "f" * 64},
    }
    stage_count = len(physical.stages)
    facade.set_memory_pinned(user_id="u1", memory_id=logical_id, pinned=True)
    assert len(physical.stages) == stage_count
    cap = running(ledger, session="query", key="query")
    with bind(ledger, cap):
        hits = facade.search("u1", "茶", limit=1)
    assert [hit.id for hit in hits] == [logical_id] and hits[0].pinned
    assert physical.searches[-1]["tags"] == [current_ref["visibility_tag"]]
    assert "ORPHAN_CANARY" not in facade.format_for_prompt(hits)


@pytest.mark.parametrize("tamper", ["body", "owner", "agent", "tag"])
def test_visible_physical_integrity_is_checked(memory, tamper):
    facade, physical, ledger = memory
    logical_id = seed(facade, ledger)
    ref = ledger.visible_memory("u1", "yzl")[logical_id]
    row = physical.rows[ref["physical_id"]]
    if tamper == "body":
        row["memory"] = "TAMPERED_CANARY"
    elif tamper == "tag":
        row["metadata"]["visibility_tag"] = "f" * 64
    else:
        row["user_id" if tamper == "owner" else "agent_id"] = "other"
    with pytest.raises(RuntimeError, match="memory_visible_record_"):
        facade.get_memory_record(logical_id, "u1")


def test_other_user_and_agent_cannot_read_delete_or_edit(memory):
    facade, physical, ledger = memory
    logical_id = seed(facade, ledger)
    assert facade.get_memory_record(logical_id, "u2") is None
    assert facade.delete_memory(logical_id, "u2")["status"] == "not_found"
    ledger.memory_control("u2", enabled=True)
    assert facade.update_memory(user_id="u2", memory_id=logical_id,
                                memory_text="用户喜欢咖啡")["status"] == "not_found"
    assert facade.get_memory_record(logical_id, "u1", "other-agent") is None
    assert len(physical.stages) == 1


def test_unapproved_body_free_metadata_and_no_frame_are_not_write_paths(memory):
    facade, physical, ledger = memory
    approved = payload()
    with pytest.raises(ApprovalError):
        facade.add_approved(replace(approved, text="TAMPERED_CANARY"))
    with pytest.raises(ExecutionRejected, match="context_required"):
        facade.add_approved(approved)
    with pytest.raises(MemoryInputError, match="free_metadata"):
        facade.update_memory(user_id="u1", memory_id="missing", memory_text="普通偏好",
                              metadata={"memory_hint": "SECRET_CANARY"})
    assert facade.add_turn(user_text="SECRET_CANARY")["status"] == "blocked"
    assert not physical.stages


def test_memory_only_frame_cannot_call_llm_or_modify_parent_state(memory):
    facade, physical, ledger = memory
    cap = running(ledger)
    ledger.finish(cap, result={})
    with bind(ledger, cap, memory_only=True):
        with pytest.raises(ExecutionRejected, match="background_memory_only"):
            call("llm", lambda timeout: "not-called", per_call_seconds=1)


def test_unavailable_physical_backend_never_becomes_successful_storage(memory):
    _, physical, ledger = memory
    facade = PublishedLongTermMemory(physical, ledger, available=False)
    cap = running(ledger)
    with bind(ledger, cap):
        assert facade.add_approved(payload())["status"] == "skipped"
    assert facade.list_records("u1") == [] and not physical.stages


def test_real_thread_interleaving_delete_finishes_before_late_stage(memory):
    facade, physical, ledger = memory
    logical_id = seed(facade, ledger)
    entered, release = Event(), Event()
    cap = running(ledger, key="delayed")

    def hold_stage():
        entered.set()
        if not release.wait(3):
            raise RuntimeError("synthetic_test_release_timeout")

    physical.after_stage = hold_stage

    def delayed_write():
        with bind(ledger, cap):
            return facade.add_approved(payload("用户习惯周末徒步"))

    with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=1) as pool:
        future = pool.submit(delayed_write)
        try:
            assert entered.wait(3)
            assert facade.delete_memory(logical_id, "u1")["status"] == "deleted"
        finally:
            release.set()
        with pytest.raises(ExecutionRejected):
            future.result(timeout=3)
    assert facade.list_records("u1") == []
```

**测试解释：** 同步成功用例与三个非成功终态形成正反对照；MemoryRunner 后台用例调用实际 `_commit_approved()` 和实际 ThreadPool，不把 context 手工写到全局变量。最后一个用 Event 明确约束“物理写入暂存 → 删除成功 → 旧调用返回”的顺序，不用任意 sleep 猜并发。其它用例覆盖逻辑 ID 稳定、旧物理数据不原地修改、metadata 不复活、合并原子性、向量过滤与置顶无需 embedding。它们仍不代替真实 Qdrant/Redis 或完整 MainRunner/Voice 联合回归。

### 21.2 新增 `tests/unit/test_memory_physical_contract.py`

文件功能：捕获实际项目 Mem0 物理适配器发送的参数，并让本机安装的 Mem0/Qdrant 适配器解析过滤条件；用 `__new__` 避免构造外部客户端，不连接数据库。

```python
from __future__ import annotations

from threading import RLock
from types import SimpleNamespace

from mem0.vector_stores.qdrant import Qdrant

from aiagent.memory.approved_write import approve_plan
from aiagent.memory.mem0_memory import Mem0LongTermMemory, VisibilityScopedMem0
from aiagent.memory.memory_safety import MemorySafetyFilter
from aiagent.schemas.memory import MemoryStorePlan


def approved():
    guard = MemorySafetyFilter().evaluate("用户喜欢清淡饮食")
    return approve_plan(MemoryStorePlan(should_store=True, status="ready",
                                        memory_text=guard.redacted_text, guard=guard),
                        user_id="u1", agent_id="yzl")


def test_actual_physical_adapter_sends_only_approved_text_and_server_tag():
    sent = []

    def add(**kwargs):
        sent.append(kwargs)
        return {"results": [{"event": "ADD", "id": "physical-1", "memory": approved_text}]}

    payload = approved()
    approved_text = payload.text
    adapter = Mem0LongTermMemory.__new__(Mem0LongTermMemory)
    adapter._lock, adapter.enable_graph = RLock(), False
    adapter.memory = SimpleNamespace(add=add)
    result = adapter._stage_approved(payload, visibility_tag="a" * 64)
    assert result == {"physical_id": "physical-1"}
    assert sent[0]["infer"] is False
    assert sent[0]["messages"] == [{"role": "user", "content": approved_text}]
    assert sent[0]["metadata"]["visibility_tag"] == "a" * 64
    assert "memory_hint" not in sent[0]["metadata"]
    assert "policy_reason" not in sent[0]["metadata"]
    assert adapter.add_approved(payload)["status"] == "blocked" and len(sent) == 1


def test_search_scope_is_sent_to_backend_not_only_filtered_after_top_k():
    sent = []
    adapter = Mem0LongTermMemory.__new__(Mem0LongTermMemory)
    adapter._lock, adapter.enable_graph = RLock(), False
    adapter.memory = SimpleNamespace(search=lambda **kwargs: sent.append(kwargs) or {"results": []})
    adapter._search_visible_physical(user_id="u1", agent_id="yzl", query="茶",
                                      tags=["a" * 64, "b" * 64], limit=3)
    request = sent[0]
    assert request["top_k"] == 3 and request["rerank"] is False
    assert request["filters"] == {"user_id": "u1", "agent_id": "yzl",
                                   "visibility_tag": {"in": ["a" * 64, "b" * 64]}}
    qdrant = Qdrant.__new__(Qdrant)
    native_filter = qdrant._create_filter(request["filters"])
    serialized = native_filter.model_dump(mode="json")
    assert "visibility_tag" in str(serialized) and "a" * 64 in str(serialized)
    assert "user_id" in str(serialized) and "agent_id" in str(serialized)
    assert any(condition.match is not None and getattr(condition.match, "any", None)
               == ["a" * 64, "b" * 64] for condition in native_filter.must)


def test_installed_mem0_processing_keeps_tags_and_disables_unscoped_entity_boost(monkeypatch):
    import mem0.memory.main as sdk

    monkeypatch.setattr(sdk, "capture_event", lambda *args, **kwargs: None)
    monkeypatch.setattr(sdk, "lemmatize_for_bm25", lambda query: query)
    monkeypatch.setattr(sdk, "extract_entities", lambda query: [("QUOTED", "synthetic-entity")])
    calls = []
    memory = VisibilityScopedMem0.__new__(VisibilityScopedMem0)
    memory.api_version = "v1.1"
    memory._entity_store = None
    memory.embedding_model = SimpleNamespace(embed=lambda text, action: [0.1, 0.2])
    memory.vector_store = SimpleNamespace(
        search=lambda **kwargs: calls.append(kwargs) or [],
        keyword_search=lambda **kwargs: None,
    )
    tags = ["a" * 64]
    result = memory.search(query="synthetic-query", top_k=3, threshold=0.0, rerank=False,
                           filters={"user_id": "u1", "agent_id": "yzl", "visibility_tag": {"in": tags}})
    assert result == {"results": []}
    assert calls[0]["filters"]["visibility_tag"] == {"in": tags}
    assert memory._entity_store is None
    assert memory._compute_entity_boosts([("QUOTED", "secret")], {}) == {}
```

这不是“Qdrant 已上线验证”；它只证明当前安装 SDK 的过滤条件构造合同存在。专用向量库仍需用两个正常 tag 与若干孤儿做真实检索，记录真实召回而非仅审查请求字典。原图分支关闭要求继续保留，不把图数据库的旧关系通过 search relations 回传。

SDK 中 spaCy 词形处理与 fastembed BM25 也可能懒加载模型，甚至下载缺失资源；这些不是 OpenAI 模型调用，不能用 embedding 计数表声称已限制它们。生产依赖应在构建/受控初始化时准备齐全并禁止运行时联网下载；相应配置与离线启动探针仍列为第 23 节的配置/迁移收口要求。上述 contract 用本地确定性 NLP 桩隔离这类依赖，不作为离线生产环境已验证证据。

### 21.3 新增 `tests/unit/test_memory_embedding_budget.py`

文件功能：使用真正 OpenAI SDK 和 MockTransport 检查实际请求次数、timeout 配置、隐藏重试关闭、向量形状与撤销后的结果拒绝；无真实网络和计费。

```python
from __future__ import annotations

from types import SimpleNamespace

import httpx
import pytest
from mem0.embeddings.openai import OpenAIEmbedding
from openai import APIStatusError, OpenAI

from aiagent.memory.budgeted_embedding import BudgetedMemoryEmbedding
from aiagent.state.execution_context import bind
from aiagent.state.execution_ledger import ExecutionLedger, ExecutionRejected, LocalAtomic, digest


def running():
    ledger = ExecutionLedger(LocalAtomic())
    ledger.memory_control("u1", enabled=True)
    cap = ledger.begin(user_id="u1", session_id="embedding", idempotency_key="one",
                       payload_digest=digest("embedding-case")).capability
    ledger.start(cap)
    return ledger, cap


def configured(handler):
    transport = httpx.Client(transport=httpx.MockTransport(handler))
    client = OpenAI(api_key="synthetic-key", base_url="https://embedding.invalid/v1",
                    max_retries=2, http_client=transport)
    original = OpenAIEmbedding.__new__(OpenAIEmbedding)
    original.client = client
    original.config = SimpleNamespace(model="synthetic-embedding", embedding_dims=1536)
    original._pass_dimensions_to_api = False
    return BudgetedMemoryEmbedding(original, expected_dims=2), client


def response(vectors):
    return {"object": "list", "data": [
        {"object": "embedding", "index": index, "embedding": vector}
        for index, vector in enumerate(vectors)
    ], "model": "synthetic-embedding", "usage": {"prompt_tokens": 1, "total_tokens": 1}}


def test_one_actual_sdk_request_per_embedding_and_no_cached_client_mutation():
    requests = []

    def handler(request):
        requests.append(request)
        return httpx.Response(200, json=response([[0.2, 0.4]]))

    adapter, client = configured(handler)
    ledger, cap = running()
    try:
        with bind(ledger, cap):
            assert adapter.embed("普通批准正文", "add") == [0.2, 0.4]
        assert len(requests) == 1
        assert client.max_retries == 2  # Shared base unchanged; actual request uses clone with zero.
        assert b'"dimensions"' not in requests[0].content
        assert 0 < requests[0].extensions["timeout"]["read"] <= 20
        assert ledger.operation("u1", cap.operation_id)["calls"] == {"embedding": 1}
    finally:
        client.close()


def test_real_sdk_503_does_not_retry_or_refund_budget():
    requests = []
    adapter, client = configured(lambda request: requests.append(request) or httpx.Response(
        503, json={"error": {"message": "synthetic_failure", "type": "server_error"}},
    ))
    ledger, cap = running()
    try:
        with bind(ledger, cap), pytest.raises(APIStatusError):
            adapter.embed("普通批准正文", "add")
        assert len(requests) == 1
        assert ledger.operation("u1", cap.operation_id)["calls"] == {"embedding": 1}
    finally:
        client.close()


@pytest.mark.parametrize("vectors", [[[0.1]], [[0.1, 0.2], [0.3, 0.4]]])
def test_bad_dimensions_or_extra_rows_are_rejected(vectors):
    adapter, client = configured(lambda request: httpx.Response(200, json=response(vectors)))
    ledger, cap = running()
    try:
        with bind(ledger, cap), pytest.raises(RuntimeError, match="memory_embedding_"):
            adapter.embed("普通正文", "search")
    finally:
        client.close()


def test_cancel_during_network_request_discards_late_embedding():
    ledger, cap = running()

    def handler(request):
        ledger.cancel_operation("u1", cap.operation_id)
        return httpx.Response(200, json=response([[0.1, 0.2]]))

    adapter, client = configured(handler)
    try:
        with bind(ledger, cap), pytest.raises(ExecutionRejected):
            adapter.embed("普通正文", "add")
        assert ledger.operation("u1", cap.operation_id)["calls"] == {"embedding": 1}
    finally:
        client.close()


def test_no_frame_or_oversized_batch_never_reaches_network():
    requests = []
    adapter, client = configured(lambda request: requests.append(request) or httpx.Response(500))
    try:
        with pytest.raises(ExecutionRejected, match="context_required"):
            adapter.embed("普通正文")
        with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="batch_invalid"):
            adapter.embed_batch(["普通正文"] * 101)
        assert not requests
    finally:
        client.close()
```

### 21.4 新增 `tests/unit/test_shared_memory_preferences.py`

文件功能：验证多个 store 实例确实共用同一账本，禁用/启用撤销旧后台资格，严格迁移旧用户选择且不覆盖新选择。不是单纯测试 JSON 序列化。

```python
from __future__ import annotations

import json

import pytest

from aiagent.memory.preference_migration import import_preference, parse_legacy_preferences
from aiagent.memory.shared_preferences import SharedMemoryPreferenceStore
from aiagent.state.execution_ledger import ExecutionLedger, ExecutionRejected, LocalAtomic, digest


def test_first_use_disabled_and_different_store_instances_share_control():
    ledger = ExecutionLedger(LocalAtomic())
    first, second = SharedMemoryPreferenceStore(ledger), SharedMemoryPreferenceStore(ledger)
    assert first.get("u1").long_term_enabled is False
    first.set(user_id="u1", long_term_enabled=True, reason="DO_NOT_STORE_FREE_REASON")
    assert second.is_enabled("u1") and second.is_enabled("u1", "second-agent")
    cap = ledger.begin(user_id="u1", session_id="s", idempotency_key="k",
                       payload_digest=digest("question")).capability
    ledger.start(cap)
    ledger.finish(cap, result={"reply": "private"})
    second.set(user_id="u1", long_term_enabled=False)
    first.set(user_id="u1", long_term_enabled=True)
    with pytest.raises(ExecutionRejected, match="memory_revoked"):
        ledger.check(cap, memory=True)
    assert "DO_NOT_STORE_FREE_REASON" not in str(ledger.snapshot("u1"))
    assert ledger.operation("u1", cap.operation_id).get("result") is None
    assert not first.is_enabled("u2")


def test_migration_conflict_prefers_disabled_and_is_idempotent():
    rows = {
        "yzl:u1": {"user_id": "u1", "agent_id": "yzl", "long_term_enabled": True},
        "other:u1": {"user_id": "u1", "agent_id": "other", "long_term_enabled": False},
        "yzl:u2": {"user_id": "u2", "agent_id": "yzl", "long_term_enabled": True},
    }
    source, decisions = parse_legacy_preferences(json.dumps(rows).encode())
    assert decisions == {"u1": False, "u2": True}
    ledger = ExecutionLedger(LocalAtomic())
    assert import_preference(ledger, "u1", decisions["u1"], source) == "imported"
    revision = ledger.snapshot("u1")["memory_revision"]
    assert import_preference(ledger, "u1", True, source) == "already_imported"
    assert ledger.snapshot("u1")["memory_revision"] == revision
    assert not SharedMemoryPreferenceStore(ledger).is_enabled("u1")
    assert import_preference(ledger, "u2", True, source) == "imported"
    assert SharedMemoryPreferenceStore(ledger).is_enabled("u2")


def test_migration_never_overwrites_explicit_new_user_choice():
    ledger = ExecutionLedger(LocalAtomic())
    preferences = SharedMemoryPreferenceStore(ledger)
    preferences.set(user_id="u1", long_term_enabled=False)
    revision = ledger.snapshot("u1")["memory_revision"]
    assert import_preference(ledger, "u1", True, "a" * 64) == "preserved"
    assert not preferences.is_enabled("u1")
    assert ledger.snapshot("u1")["memory_revision"] == revision


@pytest.mark.parametrize("content", [
    b'{"x":{},"x":{}}', b'[]', b'{bad', b'NaN',
    b'{"yzl:u1":{"user_id":"u1","agent_id":"yzl","long_term_enabled":"false"}}',
    b'{"yzl:u1":{"agent_id":"yzl","long_term_enabled":true}}',
    b'{"wrong":{"user_id":"u1","agent_id":"yzl","long_term_enabled":true}}',
])
def test_invalid_legacy_file_cannot_silently_enable_or_reset(content):
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        parse_legacy_preferences(content)
```

## 22. 新增 `tests/api/test_execution_memory_contract.py`

文件功能：实际 Memory 路由、第五批真实身份 dependency、实际 CoreRuntime Memory 方法、实际 facade 与 ledger 的 HTTP contract。仅替代物理 Mem0，明确不是完整 Runtime 启动测试；完整主图、Voice 与 saver 联合回归仍在后续待补项。

```python
from __future__ import annotations

import copy
import hashlib
import json
from types import SimpleNamespace

import pytest
from fastapi import Depends, FastAPI
from fastapi.testclient import TestClient

from aiagent.memory.approved_write import approve_plan, approved_metadata
from aiagent.memory.memory_safety import MemorySafetyFilter
from aiagent.memory.published_memory import PublishedLongTermMemory
from aiagent.memory.shared_preferences import SharedMemoryPreferenceStore
from aiagent.schemas.memory import MemoryCategory, MemoryStorePlan
from aiagent.state.execution_context import bind
from aiagent.state.execution_ledger import ExecutionLedger, LocalAtomic, digest
from aiagent.state.shared_state import SharedStateUnavailableError
from apps.api import user_auth
from apps.api.routes import memory as memory_routes
from apps.core.runtime import CoreRuntime

TOKEN = "synthetic_memory_token_" + "x" * 43
HEADERS = {"Authorization": f"Bearer {TOKEN}"}


class Physical:
    def __init__(self):
        self.rows = {}
        self.calls = 0
        self.fail = False

    def _stage_approved(self, payload, *, visibility_tag):
        self.calls += 1
        if self.fail:
            raise RuntimeError("PRIVATE_BACKEND_ERROR_CANARY")
        key = f"physical-{self.calls}"
        self.rows[key] = {"id": key, "memory": payload.text,
                          "user_id": payload.user_id, "agent_id": payload.agent_id,
                          "metadata": {**approved_metadata(payload), "visibility_tag": visibility_tag}}
        return {"physical_id": key}

    def _get_physical(self, key):
        return copy.deepcopy(self.rows.get(key))

    def _search_visible_physical(self, **kwargs):
        return [dict(row, score=0.9) for row in self.rows.values()
                if row["metadata"]["visibility_tag"] in kwargs["tags"]][:kwargs["limit"]]


@pytest.fixture
def secured_memory(monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.setattr(user_auth.settings, "api_auth_mode", "bearer")
    monkeypatch.setattr(user_auth.settings, "api_auth_token_sha256_map", json.dumps({
        hashlib.sha256(TOKEN.encode()).hexdigest(): "u1",
    }))
    monkeypatch.setattr(user_auth.settings, "app_env", "test")
    monkeypatch.setattr(user_auth.settings, "enable_local_audio_playback", False)
    monkeypatch.setattr(user_auth.cloud_settings, "cloud_mode", False)
    monkeypatch.setattr(user_auth.cloud_settings, "execution_state_mode", "local")
    ledger = ExecutionLedger(LocalAtomic())
    preferences = SharedMemoryPreferenceStore(ledger)
    preferences.set(user_id="u1", long_term_enabled=True)
    physical = Physical()
    facade = PublishedLongTermMemory(physical, ledger, available=True)
    runtime = CoreRuntime.__new__(CoreRuntime)
    runtime.long_term_memory = facade
    runtime.memory_runner = SimpleNamespace(
        get_user_preference=lambda **kwargs: preferences.get(**kwargs),
        set_user_preference=lambda **kwargs: preferences.set(**kwargs),
        write_status=lambda: {"pending": 0, "running": False},
    )
    cap = ledger.begin(user_id="u1", session_id="seed", idempotency_key="seed",
                       payload_digest=digest("synthetic-seed")).capability
    ledger.start(cap)
    guard = MemorySafetyFilter().evaluate("用户喜欢无糖茶")
    payload = approve_plan(MemoryStorePlan(should_store=True, status="ready", guard=guard,
                                            memory_text=guard.redacted_text,
                                            category=MemoryCategory.PREFERENCE),
                            user_id="u1", agent_id="yzl")
    with bind(ledger, cap) as frame:
        result = facade.add_approved(payload)
        ledger.finish(cap, result={}, memories=frame.staged_memories)
    monkeypatch.setattr(memory_routes, "get_runtime", lambda: runtime)
    app = FastAPI(dependencies=[Depends(user_auth.enforce_http_policy)])
    app.include_router(memory_routes.router)
    with TestClient(app, raise_server_exceptions=False) as client:
        yield SimpleNamespace(client=client, physical=physical, ledger=ledger,
                               logical_id=result["memory_id"], preferences=preferences, runtime=runtime)


def test_auth_contrast_and_no_unauthorized_mutation(secured_memory):
    case = secured_memory
    before = case.ledger.snapshot("u1")
    assert case.client.get("/memory/user/u1/snapshot").status_code == 401
    assert case.client.delete("/memory/user/u2", headers=HEADERS).status_code == 403
    response = case.client.get("/memory/user/u1/snapshot", headers=HEADERS)
    assert response.status_code == 200 and response.json()["snapshot"]["total"] == 1
    assert case.ledger.snapshot("u1") == before and case.physical.calls == 1


def test_edit_pin_search_delete_preserve_logical_contract(secured_memory):
    case = secured_memory
    path = f"/memory/user/u1/memories/{case.logical_id}"
    edited = case.client.patch(path, headers=HEADERS, json={"memory": "用户喜欢茉莉花茶"})
    assert edited.status_code == 200
    assert edited.json()["result"]["memory_id"] == case.logical_id
    pinned = case.client.post(path + "/pin", headers=HEADERS, json={"pinned": True})
    assert pinned.status_code == 200 and case.physical.calls == 2
    searched = case.client.get("/memory/user/u1/search", headers=HEADERS, params={"query": "茶"})
    assert searched.status_code == 200
    assert searched.json()["result"]["long_term_memories"][0]["id"] == case.logical_id
    deleted = case.client.delete(path, headers=HEADERS)
    assert deleted.status_code == 200 and deleted.json()["result"]["physical_deleted"] is False
    assert deleted.json()["result"]["cleanup_pending"] is True
    snapshot = case.client.get("/memory/user/u1/snapshot", headers=HEADERS)
    assert snapshot.json()["snapshot"]["total"] == 0
    assert len(case.physical.rows) == 2


def test_disable_is_shared_not_physical_clear_and_stats_are_truthful(secured_memory):
    case = secured_memory
    response = case.client.put("/memory/user/u1/preferences", headers=HEADERS,
                                json={"long_term_enabled": False})
    assert response.status_code == 200
    assert SharedMemoryPreferenceStore(case.ledger).is_enabled("u1") is False
    assert case.client.get("/memory/user/u1/stats", headers=HEADERS).json()["stats"]["profile"]["enabled"] is False
    assert case.client.get("/memory/user/u1/snapshot", headers=HEADERS).json()["snapshot"]["total"] == 0
    assert len(case.physical.rows) == 1
    enabled = case.client.put("/memory/user/u1/preferences", headers=HEADERS,
                               json={"long_term_enabled": True})
    assert enabled.status_code == 200
    assert case.client.get("/memory/user/u1/snapshot", headers=HEADERS).json()["snapshot"]["total"] == 1


def test_unknown_backend_failure_is_503_without_error_body_or_success(secured_memory):
    case = secured_memory
    case.physical.fail = True
    response = case.client.patch(f"/memory/user/u1/memories/{case.logical_id}", headers=HEADERS,
                                  json={"memory": "用户喜欢清淡饮食"})
    assert response.status_code == 503 and response.json()["ok"] is False
    assert "PRIVATE_BACKEND_ERROR_CANARY" not in response.text
    assert len(case.ledger.visible_memory("u1", "yzl")) == 1


def test_free_metadata_400_validation_422_and_redis_503_are_distinct(secured_memory, monkeypatch):
    case = secured_memory
    path = f"/memory/user/u1/memories/{case.logical_id}"
    response = case.client.patch(path, headers=HEADERS,
                                  json={"memory": "普通偏好", "metadata": {"memory_hint": "CANARY"}})
    assert response.status_code == 400 and "CANARY" not in response.text
    assert case.client.patch(path, headers=HEADERS, json={"memory": ""}).status_code == 422
    assert case.physical.calls == 1

    def disconnected(*args, **kwargs):
        raise SharedStateUnavailableError("PRIVATE_REDIS_CANARY")

    monkeypatch.setattr(case.ledger.atomic, "read", disconnected)
    response = case.client.get("/memory/user/u1/snapshot", headers=HEADERS)
    assert response.status_code == 503 and "PRIVATE_REDIS_CANARY" not in response.text
```

### 22.2 新增 `tests/integration/test_memory_visibility_redis.py`

文件功能：在两个真正独立的进程之间，用实际 Redis 账本和实际 facade 验证“旧物理调用被阻塞 → 另一进程撤销完成 → 旧调用返回”的顺序。外部 Mem0 仍是受控桩，不宣称真实 Qdrant 并发测试已通过。测试传的是执行 cap，不跨进程传第五批的进程内 HMAC 批准对象；子进程在自身进程内批准合成事实。

```python
from __future__ import annotations

import multiprocessing
import os
import secrets

import pytest

from aiagent.memory.approved_write import approve_plan
from aiagent.memory.memory_safety import MemorySafetyFilter
from aiagent.memory.published_memory import PublishedLongTermMemory
from aiagent.schemas.memory import MemoryStorePlan
from aiagent.state.execution_context import bind
from aiagent.state.execution_ledger import ExecutionLedger, ExecutionRejected, RedisAtomic, digest


class DelayedPhysical:
    def __init__(self, entered, release):
        self.entered, self.release = entered, release

    def _stage_approved(self, payload, *, visibility_tag):
        self.entered.set()
        if not self.release.wait(20):
            raise RuntimeError("synthetic_test_gate_timeout")
        return {"physical_id": "synthetic-delayed-physical"}


def _delayed_worker(url, prefix, cap, entered, release, result):
    from redis import Redis

    client = Redis.from_url(url, decode_responses=True, socket_connect_timeout=2, socket_timeout=2)
    ledger = ExecutionLedger(RedisAtomic(client), prefix)
    facade = PublishedLongTermMemory(DelayedPhysical(entered, release), ledger, available=True)
    guard = MemorySafetyFilter().evaluate("用户喜欢清淡饮食")
    approved = approve_plan(MemoryStorePlan(should_store=True, status="ready", guard=guard,
                                             memory_text=guard.redacted_text),
                             user_id="synthetic-user", agent_id="yzl")
    try:
        with bind(ledger, cap) as frame:
            facade.add_approved(approved)
            ledger.finish(cap, result={}, memories=frame.staged_memories)
        result.put("unexpected-success")
    except ExecutionRejected:
        result.put("revoked")
    except Exception as exc:
        result.put(type(exc).__name__)
    finally:
        client.close()


@pytest.mark.parametrize("control", ["disable_enable", "clear"])
def test_other_process_control_blocks_late_memory_visibility(control):
    from redis import Redis

    url = os.environ.get("BATCH06_REDIS_URL")
    if not url:
        pytest.skip("explicit dedicated BATCH06_REDIS_URL is required")
    client = Redis.from_url(url, decode_responses=True, socket_connect_timeout=2, socket_timeout=2)
    client.ping()
    prefix = f"batch06-memory-{secrets.token_hex(12)}"
    ledger = ExecutionLedger(RedisAtomic(client), prefix)
    ctx = multiprocessing.get_context("spawn")
    entered, release, result = ctx.Event(), ctx.Event(), ctx.Queue()
    process = None
    try:
        ledger.memory_control("synthetic-user", enabled=True)
        cap = ledger.begin(user_id="synthetic-user", session_id="chat", idempotency_key="late",
                           payload_digest=digest("synthetic-late-memory")).capability
        ledger.start(cap)
        process = ctx.Process(target=_delayed_worker,
                              args=(url, prefix, cap, entered, release, result))
        process.start()
        assert entered.wait(20)
        if control == "disable_enable":
            ledger.memory_control("synthetic-user", enabled=False)
            ledger.memory_control("synthetic-user", enabled=True)
        else:
            ledger.memory_control("synthetic-user", clear_agent="yzl")
        release.set()
        assert result.get(timeout=20) == "revoked"
        process.join(10)
        assert process.exitcode == 0
        assert ledger.visible_memory("synthetic-user", "yzl") == {}
        assert len(ledger.snapshot("synthetic-user")["memory_staging"]) == 1
    finally:
        release.set()
        if process is not None and process.is_alive():
            process.terminate()
            process.join(10)
        result.close()
        result.join_thread()
        client.delete(ledger.key("synthetic-user"))
        client.close()
```

只在专用测试 Redis 上由你执行，未配置环境变量时 skip 不算验证成功。本测试仅删除随机 prefix 下已知的一个精确 key，不使用 FLUSHDB：

```powershell
$env:BATCH06_REDIS_URL = "redis://127.0.0.1:6379/15"
.\.venv\Scripts\python.exe -m pytest tests/integration/test_execution_ledger_redis.py tests/integration/test_memory_visibility_redis.py -q
Remove-Item Env:BATCH06_REDIS_URL
```

Redis Cluster 不支持 `/15` 数字数据库；集群部署请使用专用测试集群 URL。本测试账本每次只操作同一主体的单 key，不跨槽。没有运行以上指令，不能写入真实 Redis 已验证证据。
