## 25. Voice 实际生命周期：与执行账本共用一次 CAS

本节不是另起一个 Voice 状态系统。第 2 节已同步加入 `calls` 字典及提交钩子，第 3 节已增加 `frame.voice_result`；请按本文最终版本一次实施，不把早期草稿和本节拼成两个同名类。以下模块负责纯状态变换，网络、ASR 和 TTS 不进入 CAS 回调。

### 25.1 新增 `aiagent/state/voice_execution.py`，完整代码

文件功能：通话创建幂等、主体域隔离、回合原子准入、带资格的阶段推进、最终结果共同发布、带预期代际的打断/结束。通话时间和 TTL 使用账本后端时间；状态查询不续期。最多保留 64 个通话记录，达到配额拒绝；本批不自动淘汰仍在幂等窗口中的记录。

```python
from __future__ import annotations

import copy
import re
from datetime import UTC, datetime
from typing import Any

from aiagent.perception.voice_state_machine import can_start_turn
from aiagent.schemas.voice import VoiceRealtimeCall
from aiagent.state.execution_ledger import (
    Capability, ExecutionLedger, ExecutionRejected, checked_name, digest,
)


class VoiceCallNotFound(ExecutionRejected):
    pass


def voice_session(user_id: str, call_id: str) -> str:
    return "v12voice:" + digest([user_id, call_id])


def parse_generation(value: Any) -> int:
    if isinstance(value, str) and re.fullmatch(r"0|[1-9][0-9]{0,15}", value):
        value = int(value)
    if type(value) is not int or not 0 <= value < 2**52:
        raise ValueError("voice_expected_generation_required")
    return value


def _iso(now: int) -> str:
    return datetime.fromtimestamp(now / 1000, tz=UTC).isoformat(timespec="milliseconds")


def _phase(record: dict[str, Any], phase: str, now: int) -> None:
    call = record["call"]
    call.update(phase=phase, phase_changed_at=_iso(now), last_seen_at=_iso(now))
    call["metadata"].update(voice_realtime_phase=phase,
                            voice_realtime_status=call["status"])


def _sync(root: dict[str, Any], record: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
    session = root["sessions"][record["session_id"]]
    record["call"]["metadata"].update(
        execution_generation=session["generation"],
        operation_id=record["operation_id"],
        voice_call_expires_ms=record["expires_ms"],
    )


def _clear_output(record: dict[str, Any], *, transcript: bool = False) -> None:
    call = record["call"]
    call.update(last_output_id="", last_audio_url="", last_audio_path="")
    call["metadata"].update(voice_output_has_audio=False, voice_audio_ready=False)
    if transcript:
        call["last_transcript"] = ""


def _lookup(root: dict[str, Any], now: int, call_id: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
    record = root["calls"].get(call_id)
    if record is None or now >= record["expires_ms"]:
        raise VoiceCallNotFound("call_not_found")
    return record


def validate_calls(root: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
    for call_id, record in root["calls"].items():
        checked_name(call_id)
        if not isinstance(record, dict):
            raise ValueError("voice_state_invalid")
        call = VoiceRealtimeCall.model_validate(record["call"])
        if (call.call_id != call_id or call.user_id != root["owner"]
                or record["session_id"] != voice_session(root["owner"], call_id)
                or record["session_id"] not in root["sessions"]
                or not isinstance(record["operation_id"], str)
                or type(record["expires_ms"]) is not int
                or type(record["ttl_ms"]) is not int
                or type(record["ended_ttl_ms"]) is not int
                or not 60_000 <= record["ttl_ms"] <= 86_400_000
                or not 60_000 <= record["ended_ttl_ms"] <= 86_400_000
                or not isinstance(record["start_key_digest"], str)
                or not isinstance(record["start_payload_digest"], str)
                or call.last_audio_path):
            raise ValueError("voice_state_invalid")


def check_call_session(root, now, user_id, session_id, call_id):
    if not call_id:
        if session_id.startswith("v12voice:"):
            raise ExecutionRejected("voice_session_requires_call_admission")
        return
    record = _lookup(root, now, call_id)
    if (record["session_id"] != session_id or record["call"]["user_id"] != user_id
            or record["call"]["status"] != "active"):
        raise VoiceCallNotFound("call_not_found")


def admit_call_turn(root, now, call_id, operation, expected_generation):
    record = _lookup(root, now, call_id)
    session = root["sessions"][record["session_id"]]
    # begin() has just incremented generation, in this same uncommitted CAS candidate.
    if parse_generation(expected_generation) != session["generation"] - 1:
        raise ExecutionRejected("voice_generation_conflict")
    decision = can_start_turn(VoiceRealtimeCall.model_validate(record["call"]))
    if not decision.allowed:
        raise ExecutionRejected("voice_turn_not_admissible")
    record["operation_id"] = operation["operation_id"]
    record["expires_ms"] = now + record["ttl_ms"]
    operation["voice_call_id"] = call_id
    operation["deadline_ms"] = min(operation["deadline_ms"], record["expires_ms"])
    call = record["call"]
    call["turn_count"] += 1
    call["last_turn_id"] = f"{call_id}:{call['turn_count']}"
    call.update(last_error="", last_interrupt_reason="", last_transcript="")
    _clear_output(record)
    call["metadata"].update(voice_realtime_call_id=call_id,
                            voice_realtime_turn_id=call["last_turn_id"],
                            voice_realtime_turn_count=call["turn_count"])
    _phase(record, "uploaded", now)
    _sync(root, record)


def assert_call_capability(root, operation, cap, now):
    call_id = operation.get("voice_call_id", "")
    if not call_id:
        return
    record = _lookup(root, now, call_id)
    if (record["operation_id"] != cap.operation_id
            or record["session_id"] != cap.session_id
            or record["call"]["status"] != "active"):
        raise ExecutionRejected("voice_stale_turn")


def publish_call_result(root, operation, now, result, voice_result, objects):
    committed = copy.deepcopy(result)
    call_id = operation.get("voice_call_id", "")
    if not call_id:
        if voice_result is not None:
            raise ExecutionRejected("voice_result_without_call")
        return committed
    if (not isinstance(voice_result, dict)
            or set(voice_result) != {"transcript", "output_id", "audio_url", "has_audio"}
            or type(voice_result["has_audio"]) is not bool
            or any(not isinstance(voice_result[key], str)
                   for key in ("transcript", "output_id", "audio_url"))
            or len(voice_result["transcript"]) > 16_384):
        raise ExecutionRejected("voice_invalid_result")
    if voice_result["output_id"] != str(result.get("output_id", "")):
        raise ExecutionRejected("voice_output_mismatch")
    audio_objects = [item for item in objects.values() if item.get("kind") == "audio"]
    if voice_result["has_audio"] != bool(audio_objects):
        raise ExecutionRejected("voice_audio_publication_mismatch")
    url = voice_result["audio_url"]
    if url and not any(url == f"/audio/{key.removeprefix('audio:')}"
                       for key in objects if key.startswith("audio:")):
        raise ExecutionRejected("voice_unregistered_audio_url")
    record = _lookup(root, now, call_id)
    call = record["call"]
    call.update(last_transcript=voice_result["transcript"],
                last_output_id=voice_result["output_id"], last_audio_url=url,
                last_audio_path="", last_error="")
    call["metadata"].update(voice_output_has_audio=bool(audio_objects),
                            voice_audio_ready=bool(audio_objects))
    # Generating an audio object is not proof of consumption or playback.
    _phase(record, "completed" if voice_result["transcript"] else "empty_turn", now)
    _sync(root, record)
    from aiagent.perception.voice_realtime_observability import voice_response_payload

    committed["body"].update(voice_response_payload(VoiceRealtimeCall.model_validate(call)))
    committed["body"].update(
        execution_generation=root["sessions"][record["session_id"]]["generation"],
        operation_id=operation["operation_id"],
    )
    return committed


def settle_call_operation(root, operation, now, status):
    call_id = operation.get("voice_call_id", "")
    record = root["calls"].get(call_id)
    if record is None or record["operation_id"] != operation["operation_id"]:
        return
    if record["call"]["status"] == "ended":
        return
    _clear_output(record, transcript=True)
    record["call"]["last_error"] = "" if status == "cancelled" else "voice_turn_unavailable"
    _phase(record, "interrupted", now)
    _sync(root, record)


def revoke_call_session(root, session_id, now, *, close=False, clear=False):
    for record in root["calls"].values():
        if record["session_id"] != session_id:
            continue
        _clear_output(record, transcript=True)
        if close:
            record["call"].update(status="ended", ended_at=_iso(now))
            record["expires_ms"] = now + record["ended_ttl_ms"]
        _phase(record, "completed" if close else "interrupted", now)
        _sync(root, record)


def revoke_all_call_outputs(root, now):
    for record in root["calls"].values():
        _clear_output(record, transcript=True)
        if record["call"]["status"] != "ended":
            _phase(record, "interrupted", now)
        _sync(root, record)


def start_call(ledger: ExecutionLedger, *, user_id: str, username: str,
               idempotency_key: str, ttl_seconds=1800, ended_ttl_seconds=300):
    checked_name(user_id)
    checked_name(idempotency_key)
    if (not isinstance(username, str) or len(username) > 64
            or any(ord(char) < 32 for char in username)
            or type(ttl_seconds) is not int or not 60 <= ttl_seconds <= 86_400
            or type(ended_ttl_seconds) is not int or not 60 <= ended_ttl_seconds <= 86_400):
        raise ValueError("voice_invalid_start")
    key_digest = digest(["voice-start", idempotency_key])
    payload_digest = digest([user_id, username])

    def mutate(root, now):
        for record in root["calls"].values():
            if record["start_key_digest"] != key_digest:
                continue
            if record["start_payload_digest"] != payload_digest:
                raise ExecutionRejected("voice_start_payload_conflict")
            if now >= record["expires_ms"]:
                raise VoiceCallNotFound("call_not_found")
            return VoiceRealtimeCall.model_validate(copy.deepcopy(record["call"]))
        if len(root["calls"]) >= 64 or len(root["sessions"]) >= 128:
            raise ExecutionRejected("voice_call_quota_exceeded")
        call_id = "vc12_" + digest([root["epoch"], user_id, key_digest])[:32]
        session_id = voice_session(user_id, call_id)
        root["sessions"][session_id] = {
            "generation": 0, "active": "", "closed": False,
            "checkpoint": "", "history": [], "output_id": "",
        }
        call = VoiceRealtimeCall(
            call_id=call_id, user_id=user_id, username=username,
            started_at=_iso(now), last_seen_at=_iso(now), phase_changed_at=_iso(now),
        )
        record = {
            "call": call.model_dump(mode="json"), "session_id": session_id,
            "operation_id": "", "expires_ms": now + ttl_seconds * 1000,
            "ttl_ms": ttl_seconds * 1000, "ended_ttl_ms": ended_ttl_seconds * 1000,
            "start_key_digest": key_digest, "start_payload_digest": payload_digest,
        }
        root["calls"][call_id] = record
        _sync(root, record)
        return VoiceRealtimeCall.model_validate(copy.deepcopy(record["call"]))
    return ledger.transaction(user_id, mutate)


def get_call(ledger: ExecutionLedger, user_id: str, call_id: str):
    def mutate(root, now):
        record = root["calls"].get(call_id)
        if record is None or now >= record["expires_ms"]:
            return None
        operation = root["operations"].get(record["operation_id"])
        if (operation and operation["status"] in {"accepted", "running"}
                and now >= operation["deadline_ms"]):
            operation.update(status="unknown", finished_ms=now)
            session = root["sessions"][record["session_id"]]
            if session["active"] == operation["operation_id"]:
                session["active"] = ""
            settle_call_operation(root, operation, now, "unknown")
        return VoiceRealtimeCall.model_validate(copy.deepcopy(record["call"]))
    # This may settle an expired operation but never refreshes call expiry/last_seen.
    return ledger.transaction(user_id, mutate)


def mark_call_phase(ledger: ExecutionLedger, cap: Capability, phase: str):
    transitions = {"uploaded": {"transcribing"}, "transcribing": {"thinking"}}

    def mutate(root, now):
        operation = ledger._assert(root, cap, now)
        record = _lookup(root, now, operation.get("voice_call_id", ""))
        previous = record["call"]["phase"]
        if phase != previous and phase not in transitions.get(previous, set()):
            raise ExecutionRejected("voice_invalid_phase_transition")
        _phase(record, phase, now)
        _sync(root, record)
        return VoiceRealtimeCall.model_validate(copy.deepcopy(record["call"]))
    return ledger.transaction(cap.user_id, mutate, deadline_ms=cap.deadline_ms)


def control_call(ledger: ExecutionLedger, *, user_id: str, call_id: str,
                 expected_generation: int, close: bool):
    expected_generation = parse_generation(expected_generation)

    def mutate(root, now):
        record = _lookup(root, now, call_id)
        session_id = record["session_id"]
        session = root["sessions"][session_id]
        if record["call"]["status"] == "ended":
            if close and record.get("end_expected_generation") == expected_generation:
                return VoiceRealtimeCall.model_validate(copy.deepcopy(record["call"]))
            raise VoiceCallNotFound("call_not_found")
        if session["generation"] != expected_generation:
            raise ExecutionRejected("voice_generation_conflict")
        session["generation"] += 1
        if session["generation"] >= 2**52:
            raise ExecutionRejected("execution_counter_exhausted")
        for operation in root["operations"].values():
            if operation["session_id"] == session_id:
                if operation["status"] in {"accepted", "running"}:
                    operation.update(status="cancelled", finished_ms=now)
                operation.pop("result", None)
        root["objects"] = {
            key: item for key, item in root["objects"].items()
            if root["operations"].get(item.get("operation_id", ""), {}).get("session_id") != session_id
        }
        session.update(active="", output_id="", closed=bool(close))
        if close:
            session.update(checkpoint="", history=[])
            record["end_expected_generation"] = expected_generation
        else:
            record["call"]["interrupt_count"] += 1
            record["call"]["last_interrupt_reason"] = "user_interrupt"
        revoke_call_session(root, session_id, now, close=close, clear=True)
        if not close:
            record["expires_ms"] = now + record["ttl_ms"]
            _sync(root, record)
        return VoiceRealtimeCall.model_validate(copy.deepcopy(record["call"]))
    return ledger.transaction(user_id, mutate)
```

**代码解释与边界：**

1. `voice_session()` 与普通聊天的 `s12:` 命名空间分离。即使客户端把 `call_id` 当作普通 chat 的 session，也无法重开已经结束的语音会话。
2. `begin()` 的 CAS 候选内同时增加 generation、登记 operation 和推进 turn_count；并发失败不会提前增加可见计数。同键重放不执行推进函数。
3. `parse_generation()` 接受 JSON 整数或 Form 的规范十进制字符串，不接受布尔、负值、小数、指数格式。turn、interrupt、end 都必须携带最近状态中的 `execution_generation`；不是把客户端 generation 当作服务器真源。
4. `publish_call_result()` 在 `finish()` 的同一 CAS 内更新通话、输出、历史、资源与同步记忆。HTTP body 也由该提交快照产生，不在提交后重读造成另一轮状态混入。
5. 通话记录不存音频绝对路径，只存已登记私有 URL；生成音频只标 `voice_audio_ready`，不标 `speaking`。第七批才引入消费者 ACK，不能将这里的 completed 解读为已经播完。
6. 异常、取消、Memory 撤销、清会话都同步撤销通话可见状态；旧操作的迟到终态只能影响自己的记录，不能将后继回合标为失败。
7. `get_call()` 为收敛超时状态使用同一 CAS，但不续期。不存在的 user/call 查询不会返回别人的记录；轮询不能使通话永久存活。
8. 打断撤销该通话历史输出下载和当前资格，保留已提交对话上下文；结束额外清短期上下文。已下载的音频及远端正在生成的工作不可物理撤回。两者均不删除独立长期记忆。
9. `revoke_all_call_outputs()` 是第 2 节 Memory 隐私屏障的补充：删除/禁用记忆后，不能经 call.last_transcript 或旧音频旁路读回。代价是该主体所有通话输出会失效，已在本批采用这一保守边界。
10. 本模块顶层导入 ledger，ledger 只在函数内部导入本模块，避免模块初始化时的循环引用；不要把这些局部 import 全部提升到 ledger 文件顶部。

### 25.2 完整替换 `aiagent/perception/voice_call_store.py`

文件功能：保留项目既有仓储入口，但只作为共享执行账本的异步薄适配器。删除旧 `_calls`、独立 Redis JSON、锁和 fail-open 分支；不同时保存两份可变通话状态。旧 `save/next_turn/mark_output/mark_transcript/fail` API 故意不保留，遗漏调用会直接暴露，不能为了让旧测试通过保留无资格写入。

```python
from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio
from typing import Any

from aiagent.state.execution_ledger import Capability, ExecutionLedger, RedisAtomic
from aiagent.state.shared_state import SharedStateUnavailableError
from aiagent.state.voice_execution import (
    control_call, get_call, mark_call_phase, start_call,
)
from cloud.config import cloud_settings


# Compatibility names for callers that used these error classes.
from aiagent.state.shared_state import SharedStateConflictError as VoiceCallStoreConflictError
from aiagent.state.shared_state import SharedStateError as VoiceCallStoreError
from aiagent.state.shared_state import SharedStateUnavailableError as VoiceCallStoreUnavailableError

__all__ = [
    "VoiceRealtimeCallStore", "VoiceCallStoreError", "VoiceCallStoreConflictError",
    "VoiceCallStoreUnavailableError", "voice_call_store",
]


class VoiceRealtimeCallStore:
    def __init__(self, *, prefix: str, provider: str = "auto", ttl_seconds: int = 1800,
                 ended_ttl_seconds: int = 300, lock_ttl_seconds: int = 15,
                 lock_wait_seconds: float = 2.0, fail_open: bool = False,
                 ledger: ExecutionLedger | None = None) -> None:
        if provider not in {"auto", "redis", "memory"} or fail_open:
            raise ValueError("voice_store_requires_fail_closed")
        self.prefix = prefix.rstrip(":")
        self.provider = provider
        self.ttl_seconds = ttl_seconds
        self.ended_ttl_seconds = ended_ttl_seconds
        self._ledger: ExecutionLedger | None = None
        # Legacy lock arguments are accepted for config migration, never used as fencing.
        if ledger is not None:
            self.bind_ledger(ledger)

    def bind_ledger(self, ledger: ExecutionLedger) -> None:
        is_redis = isinstance(ledger.atomic, RedisAtomic)
        if (ledger.prefix != self.prefix or self.provider == "redis" and not is_redis
                or self.provider == "memory" and is_redis):
            raise ValueError("voice_execution_backend_mismatch")
        self._ledger = ledger

    def require_ledger(self) -> ExecutionLedger:
        if self._ledger is None:
            raise SharedStateUnavailableError("voice_execution_not_bound")
        return self._ledger

    @property
    def effective_provider(self) -> str:
        if self._ledger is None:
            return "unbound"
        return "redis" if isinstance(self._ledger.atomic, RedisAtomic) else "memory"

    async def start(self, *, user_id: str, username: str, idempotency_key: str):
        return await asyncio.to_thread(
            start_call, self.require_ledger(), user_id=user_id, username=username,
            idempotency_key=idempotency_key, ttl_seconds=self.ttl_seconds,
            ended_ttl_seconds=self.ended_ttl_seconds,
        )

    async def get(self, call_id: str, *, user_id: str, touch: bool = False):
        if touch:
            raise ValueError("voice_polling_must_not_extend_ttl")
        return await asyncio.to_thread(get_call, self.require_ledger(), user_id, call_id)

    async def mark_phase(self, cap: Capability, phase: str):
        return await asyncio.to_thread(mark_call_phase, self.require_ledger(), cap, str(phase))

    async def interrupt(self, call_id: str, *, user_id: str, expected_generation: int):
        return await asyncio.to_thread(
            control_call, self.require_ledger(), user_id=user_id, call_id=call_id,
            expected_generation=expected_generation, close=False,
        )

    async def end(self, call_id: str, *, user_id: str, expected_generation: int):
        return await asyncio.to_thread(
            control_call, self.require_ledger(), user_id=user_id, call_id=call_id,
            expected_generation=expected_generation, close=True,
        )

    async def backend_status(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        backend = self.effective_provider
        ok = False
        if self._ledger is not None:
            try:
                await asyncio.to_thread(
                    self._ledger.atomic.read, self._ledger.key("__voice_health_probe__"),
                )
                ok = True
            except SharedStateUnavailableError:
                pass
        return {
            "ok": ok, "configured_provider": self.provider,
            "effective_provider": backend, "fail_open": False,
            "redis_configured": bool(cloud_settings.redis_url),
            "redis_available": ok and backend == "redis",
            "state_source": "execution_ledger", "last_backend": backend,
            "last_error": "" if ok else "voice_execution_unavailable",
        }


voice_call_store = VoiceRealtimeCallStore(
    prefix=cloud_settings.redis_prefix, provider=cloud_settings.voice_call_store_provider,
    ttl_seconds=cloud_settings.voice_call_ttl_seconds,
    ended_ttl_seconds=cloud_settings.voice_call_ended_ttl_seconds,
    fail_open=cloud_settings.voice_call_store_fail_open,
)
```

解释：`bind_ledger()` 在 Runtime 装配完成前绑定**同一对象**，不从 health/auth 调用里递归 `get_runtime()`。本地开发可以显式 local 单进程账本；在本机启动服务也可以选择 Redis，和部署位置无关。Redis 故障不会切内存；`provider=auto` 跟随已经装配的账本，而不再次根据另一份 URL 推断独立后端。旧锁参数仅迁移期接受，不参与任何保证。

### 25.3 准确接线：Bootstrap、身份和 Readiness

`apps/core/bootstrap.py` 导入区新增：

```python
from aiagent.perception.voice_call_store import voice_call_store
```

在现有 `dispatcher = EventDispatcher(...)` 装配完成之后、`capabilities.mark_available("runtime", ...)` 之前新增：

```python
    voice_call_store.bind_ledger(execution_ledger)
```

这里 `execution_ledger` 是第 20.1 节在 Memory 之前创建的唯一实例，Dispatcher 同样引用它，不允许另外创建 `LocalAtomic()`。初始化失败就保留 Runtime 不可用，不在 catch 中再次绑定本地副本。测试 `reset_runtime()` 后若使用同一模块仓储，下一次 build 必须重新绑定；清理测试只创建显式注入的独立 ledger，不能调用旧 `clear_for_tests()` 清共享数据。

`apps/api/user_auth.py` 的 `_CALL_INPUTS` 分支，**仅替换取通话的单行**，保留后面的统一 404 和主体比较：

```python
        call = await _CALL_STORE.get(call_id, user_id=principal.user_id, touch=False)
```

`cloud/failure_policy.py` 的 `RedisDependency.VOICE_CALL_STORE` 值从 `FailureMode.FALLBACK_MEMORY` 改为 `FailureMode.FAIL_CLOSED`。`cloud/readiness.py` 的 `_voice_call_store_check()` 保留既有字段和摘要，只作以下两处修改：

```python
    required = bool(
        cloud_settings.cloud_mode or redis_expected
        or cloud_settings.execution_state_mode == "redis"
    )
```

以及最后 `ReadinessCheck(...)` 的参数：

```python
        failure_mode=FailureMode.FAIL_CLOSED.value,
```

删除同函数的 `elif ok and effective_provider == "memory_fallback": ...` 分支。同一仓储的 `backend_status()` 已不可能返回该模式，不能保留“故障后成功使用内存”的运维指导。

### 25.4 完整替换 `apps/api/routes/voice_realtime.py`

文件功能：复用原 API 路径和主要聚合输出字段；turn 资格由第 6 节 `ExecutionRoute` 原子授予，handler 不再二次 `next_turn()`。start/control/state 通过薄仓储执行有界状态操作。仅 turn 执行 ASR/聊天；本模块不调用全局播放器或 `runtime.interrupt_speaking()`，打断只撤本用户本通话的资格，不能打断其它用户。

```python
from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio
import secrets
import tempfile
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Annotated

from fastapi import APIRouter, File, Form, HTTPException, Request, UploadFile
from pydantic import BaseModel, ConfigDict, Field
from starlette.responses import JSONResponse

from aiagent.perception.voice_call_store import voice_call_store
from aiagent.perception.voice_realtime_observability import voice_response_payload
from aiagent.state.execution_context import current
from aiagent.state.execution_ledger import ExecutionRejected
from aiagent.state.shared_state import SharedStateError
from aiagent.state.voice_execution import VoiceCallNotFound, mark_call_phase
from apps.api.execution_route import ExecutionRoute
from apps.api.response_utils import ok_response
from apps.api.shared_state_errors import shared_state_error_response
from apps.api.user_auth import authenticate
from apps.core.runtime_registry import get_runtime

_CALL_STORE = voice_call_store
MAX_UPLOAD_BYTES = 25 * 1024 * 1024
AUDIO_SUFFIXES = {".wav", ".mp3", ".ogg", ".m4a", ".webm"}


class VoiceRoute(ExecutionRoute):
    def get_route_handler(self):
        original = super().get_route_handler()

        async def handler(request):
            try:
                # Authenticate before expensive initialization; owner checks follow ledger binding.
                authenticate(request)
                await asyncio.to_thread(get_runtime)
                return await original(request)
            except HTTPException:
                raise
            except VoiceCallNotFound:
                return JSONResponse(status_code=404, content={"ok": False, "error": "call_not_found"})
            except SharedStateError as exc:
                response = shared_state_error_response(exc)
                if response is not None:
                    return response
                return JSONResponse(status_code=503, content={"ok": False, "error": "voice_state_unavailable"})
        return handler


router = APIRouter(route_class=VoiceRoute)


class VoiceRealtimeStartRequest(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")
    user_id: str
    username: str = Field(default="guest", max_length=64)


class VoiceRealtimeEndRequest(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")
    call_id: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=256)
    expected_generation: int = Field(strict=True, ge=0, lt=2**52)


class VoiceRealtimeInterruptRequest(VoiceRealtimeEndRequest):
    # Compatibility input only. Never stored, used for authorization, or logged.
    reason: str = Field(default="user_interrupt", max_length=120)


def _identity(request: Request) -> str:
    return request.state.principal.user_id


def _payload(call):
    return {
        **voice_response_payload(call),
        "execution_generation": call.metadata["execution_generation"],
        "operation_id": call.metadata["operation_id"],
    }


@router.post("/voice/realtime/start")
async def voice_realtime_start(req: VoiceRealtimeStartRequest, request: Request):
    values = request.headers.getlist("idempotency-key")
    key = values[0] if len(values) == 1 else secrets.token_urlsafe(24)
    if len(values) > 1 or not key or len(key) > 128 or any(not 33 <= ord(c) <= 126 for c in key):
        raise HTTPException(400, detail={"code": "invalid_idempotency_key"})
    if any(ord(char) < 32 for char in req.username):
        raise HTTPException(422, detail={"code": "invalid_username"})
    call = await _CALL_STORE.start(user_id=_identity(request), username=req.username,
                                   idempotency_key=key)
    return ok_response(**_payload(call), turn_seconds=2.8)


@router.get("/voice/realtime/state/{call_id}")
async def voice_realtime_state(call_id: str, request: Request):
    call = await _CALL_STORE.get(call_id, user_id=_identity(request))
    if call is None:
        raise VoiceCallNotFound("call_not_found")
    return ok_response(**_payload(call))


@router.post("/voice/realtime/interrupt")
async def voice_realtime_interrupt(req: VoiceRealtimeInterruptRequest, request: Request):
    call = await _CALL_STORE.interrupt(
        req.call_id, user_id=_identity(request), expected_generation=req.expected_generation,
    )
    return ok_response(**_payload(call), result={
        "cancelled": True, "provider_abort_guaranteed": False,
        "scope": "owned_call", "playback_acknowledged": False,
    })


@router.post("/voice/realtime/end")
async def voice_realtime_end(req: VoiceRealtimeEndRequest, request: Request):
    call = await _CALL_STORE.end(
        req.call_id, user_id=_identity(request), expected_generation=req.expected_generation,
    )
    return ok_response(**_payload(call))


async def _read_audio(file: UploadFile) -> tuple[bytes, str]:
    suffix = Path(file.filename or "turn.m4a").suffix.lower()
    if suffix not in AUDIO_SUFFIXES:
        raise HTTPException(400, detail={"code": "unsupported_audio_extension"})
    frame = current(required=True)
    assert frame is not None
    content = bytearray()
    while True:
        frame.remaining()
        chunk = await file.read(256 * 1024)
        if not chunk:
            break
        if len(content) + len(chunk) > MAX_UPLOAD_BYTES:
            raise HTTPException(413, detail={"code": "voice_upload_too_large"})
        content.extend(chunk)
    if not content:
        raise HTTPException(400, detail={"code": "empty_voice_upload"})
    return bytes(content), suffix


def _run_turn(runtime, call_id: str, user_id: str, username: str,
              content: bytes, suffix: str) -> dict:
    frame = current(required=True)
    assert frame is not None
    # The worker owns both the temporary file and cleanup, including after HTTP timeout.
    root = Path("data/cache/voice_private")
    root.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="turn_", dir=root) as directory:
        frame.remaining()
        path = Path(directory) / f"input{suffix}"
        path.write_bytes(content)
        call = mark_call_phase(frame.ledger, frame.capability, "transcribing")
        transcript = (runtime.transcribe_audio_file(str(path)) or "").strip()
        frame.remaining()
        if len(transcript) > 16_384:
            raise ExecutionRejected("voice_transcript_too_large")
        packet = None
        output_id = ""
        if transcript:
            mark_call_phase(frame.ledger, frame.capability, "thinking")
            output = runtime.handle_chat_full(
                text=transcript, user_id=user_id, username=username,
                session_id=call_id, turn_id=call.last_turn_id,
            )
            frame.remaining()
            packet, output_id = output.packet, output.output_id
        # Both values remain private to the frame until ExecutionRoute.finish().
        frame.voice_result = {
            "transcript": transcript, "output_id": output_id,
            "audio_url": (packet.audio_url or "") if packet else "",
            "has_audio": any(item.get("kind") == "audio" for item in frame.staged_objects.values()),
        }
        return {
            "call_id": call_id, "session_id": call_id, "turn_id": call.last_turn_id,
            "transcript": transcript, "output_id": output_id,
            "reply": packet.reply_text if packet else "",
            "base_reply_text": (packet.base_reply_text or packet.reply_text) if packet else "",
            "emotion": str(packet.emotion or "neutral") if packet else "neutral",
            "motion": (packet.motion or "idle") if packet else "idle",
            "expression": (packet.expression or "neutral") if packet else "neutral",
            "audio_path": "", "audio_segments": [],
            "audio_url": (packet.audio_url or "") if packet else "",
            "audio_segment_urls": list(packet.audio_segment_urls) if packet else [],
            "audio_segment_texts": list(packet.audio_segment_texts) if packet else [],
            "live2d_command_path": "", "live2d": packet.live2d if packet else {},
            "metadata": dict(packet.metadata) if packet else {},
        }


@router.post("/voice/realtime/turn")
async def voice_realtime_turn(
    request: Request, call_id: Annotated[str, Form()],
    expected_generation: Annotated[int, Form(ge=0)],
    file: Annotated[UploadFile, File()],
    user_id: Annotated[str, Form()] = "guest",
    username: Annotated[str, Form(max_length=64)] = "guest",
):
    frame = current(required=True)
    assert frame is not None
    content, suffix = await _read_audio(file)
    try:
        payload = await asyncio.wait_for(
            asyncio.to_thread(_run_turn, get_runtime(), call_id, _identity(request),
                              username, content, suffix),
            timeout=frame.remaining(),
        )
        frame.remaining()
        return ok_response(**payload)
    except SharedStateError:
        raise
    except TimeoutError:
        # ExecutionRoute marks unknown; it never resubmits ASR/LLM automatically.
        raise HTTPException(504, detail={"code": "voice_turn_timeout"}) from None
    except HTTPException:
        raise
    except Exception:
        # Do not store/return raw provider exceptions or transcripts in error metadata.
        raise HTTPException(503, detail={"code": "voice_turn_unavailable"}) from None
```

**详细解释：**

- `ExecutionRoute` 已在第 6 节同步：turn 解析 `expected_generation`，使用专用 voice session，`begin(..., voice_call_id=...)`；成功 `finish(..., voice_result=...)` 返回本次提交快照，再以该 body 返回 JSON。非 Voice 入口的提交结果不变。
- `_run_turn()` 使用真实 Runtime 的 ASR 和聊天方法，不新建服务。`asyncio.to_thread` 复制 ContextVar，worker 顺序更新同一个 frame；主协程只在 worker 成功返回后提交，不与 worker 并发发布暂存 dict。
- 临时文件由 worker 的 `TemporaryDirectory` 管理，HTTP 超时不提前删除仍被 ASR 读取的文件。线程即使继续计算，下一检查/提交也会因为 unknown、取消、过期或 generation 变化而拒绝。进程硬退出的遗留临时目录交第九批清理，不宣称这里已能杀死本地推理。
- 上传采用 256 KiB 分块、25 MiB 上限，仍需 multipart/网关全局体积和并发限制。这里只检查音频后缀，不假称完成了音频魔数安全解析或媒体沙箱。
- 空转写正常提交 `empty_turn`，不调用聊天，不制造空助手消息，不运行 TTS。非空结果只返回私有 URL，兼容 `audio_path/audio_segments` 字段但置空，不泄漏宿主机路径。
- 成功 call metadata 由纯状态代码生成，不把 Provider 错误或自由 `reason` 存入仓储。packet metadata 仍走原公共响应规则；第八批继续做来源与用途隔离。
- `reason` 兼容接收但不保存；控制操作固定用 `user_interrupt`。`expected_generation` 缺失为 422/400，旧客户端要先读 state 再控制，不能为兼容恢复“盲打断最新一轮”。
- 没有新增 Live2D 行为，仅透传已有 `packet.live2d` 聚合字段，路径字段置空。后端无消费者 ACK 时不声称已停止客户端播放。

## 26. Voice 单元、回归、HTTP Contract 与 Redis 交错测试

### 26.1 新增 `tests/unit/test_voice_execution.py`

文件功能：不依赖真实 ASR/Redis，直接验证生产使用的纯状态函数和 ledger；此外调用真实 `_run_turn()`，用阻塞 ASR 替身证明打断后的工作线程不能发布，临时文件也不会在仍被使用时提前删除。

```python
from __future__ import annotations

from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor
from pathlib import Path
from threading import Event
from types import SimpleNamespace
from unittest.mock import Mock

import pytest

from aiagent.state.execution_context import bind
from aiagent.state.execution_ledger import ExecutionLedger, ExecutionRejected, LocalAtomic, digest
from aiagent.state.voice_execution import (
    VoiceCallNotFound, control_call, get_call, mark_call_phase, parse_generation,
    start_call, voice_session,
)
from apps.api.routes.voice_realtime import _run_turn


@pytest.fixture
def store():
    clock = [1_800_000_000_000]
    return ExecutionLedger(LocalAtomic(clock=lambda: clock[0])), clock


def start(ledger, user="u1", key="start"):
    return start_call(ledger, user_id=user, username="synthetic",
                      idempotency_key=key, ttl_seconds=60, ended_ttl_seconds=60)


def begin(ledger, call, key="turn"):
    admission = ledger.begin(
        user_id=call.user_id, session_id=voice_session(call.user_id, call.call_id),
        idempotency_key=key, payload_digest=digest(key), voice_call_id=call.call_id,
        expected_voice_generation=call.metadata["execution_generation"],
    )
    if admission.created:
        ledger.start(admission.capability)
    return admission


def finish(ledger, cap, *, audio=False):
    objects = {
        "output:o1": {"kind": "output", "path": "", "expires_ms": cap.deadline_ms + 1000},
    }
    if audio:
        objects["audio:synthetic.wav"] = {
            "kind": "audio", "path": "synthetic-private-path", "expires_ms": cap.deadline_ms + 1000,
        }
    return ledger.finish(
        cap, result={"status_code": 200, "body": {"ok": True, "output_id": "o1"}, "output_id": "o1"},
        objects=objects, history_pair=("question", "answer"), checkpoint="physical-op-1",
        voice_result={"transcript": "synthetic text", "output_id": "o1",
                      "audio_url": "/audio/synthetic.wav" if audio else "", "has_audio": audio},
    )


def test_start_idempotency_and_owner_namespace(store):
    ledger, _ = store
    first, same, other = start(ledger), start(ledger), start(ledger, user="u2")
    assert first.call_id == same.call_id != other.call_id
    assert first.metadata["execution_generation"] == 0
    assert get_call(ledger, "u2", first.call_id) is None
    assert voice_session("u1", first.call_id) != voice_session("u2", first.call_id)
    with pytest.raises(ExecutionRejected, match="payload_conflict"):
        start_call(ledger, user_id="u1", username="changed", idempotency_key="start")


def test_turn_atomic_admission_duplicate_and_busy_contrast(store):
    ledger, _ = store
    call = start(ledger)
    accepted = begin(ledger, call)
    duplicate = begin(ledger, call)
    assert accepted.created and not duplicate.created and duplicate.status == "running"
    assert get_call(ledger, "u1", call.call_id).turn_count == 1
    with pytest.raises(ExecutionRejected, match="busy"):
        begin(ledger, call, "different")
    assert get_call(ledger, "u1", call.call_id).turn_count == 1


def test_finish_atomically_publishes_call_resources_history_and_replay(store):
    ledger, _ = store
    call = start(ledger)
    cap = begin(ledger, call).capability
    assert ledger.visible_object("u1", "audio", "synthetic.wav") is None
    mark_call_phase(ledger, cap, "transcribing")
    mark_call_phase(ledger, cap, "thinking")
    result = finish(ledger, cap, audio=True)
    current = get_call(ledger, "u1", call.call_id)
    assert current.phase == "completed" and current.last_audio_path == ""
    assert current.metadata["voice_audio_ready"] is True
    assert result["body"]["call"] == current.model_dump(mode="json")
    assert ledger.visible_object("u1", "audio", "synthetic.wav") is not None
    session = ledger.snapshot("u1")["sessions"][cap.session_id]
    assert session["history"] == [["question", "answer"]]
    assert session["checkpoint"] == "physical-op-1"
    assert begin(ledger, call).result == result


def test_interrupt_revokes_old_worker_and_stale_control_does_not_touch_next_turn(store):
    ledger, _ = store
    initial = start(ledger)
    a = begin(ledger, initial).capability
    interrupted = control_call(ledger, user_id="u1", call_id=initial.call_id,
                               expected_generation=a.generation, close=False)
    b = begin(ledger, interrupted, "turn-b").capability
    before = ledger.snapshot("u1")
    with pytest.raises(ExecutionRejected, match="generation_conflict"):
        control_call(ledger, user_id="u1", call_id=initial.call_id,
                     expected_generation=a.generation, close=False)
    with pytest.raises(ExecutionRejected):
        finish(ledger, a)
    assert ledger.cancel_operation("u1", a.operation_id) == "cancelled"
    assert ledger.snapshot("u1") == before
    ledger.check(b)
    finish(ledger, b)


def test_end_closes_context_and_removes_old_result_without_deleting_memory(store):
    ledger, _ = store
    initial = start(ledger)
    cap = begin(ledger, initial).capability
    finish(ledger, cap, audio=True)
    ended = control_call(ledger, user_id="u1", call_id=initial.call_id,
                          expected_generation=cap.generation, close=True)
    assert ended.status == "ended" and not ended.last_transcript
    assert ledger.visible_object("u1", "audio", "synthetic.wav") is None
    assert "result" not in ledger.snapshot("u1")["operations"][cap.operation_id]
    assert control_call(ledger, user_id="u1", call_id=initial.call_id,
                        expected_generation=cap.generation, close=True) == ended
    with pytest.raises(VoiceCallNotFound):
        begin(ledger, initial)
    session = ledger.snapshot("u1")["sessions"][cap.session_id]
    assert session["closed"] and session["history"] == [] and session["checkpoint"] == ""


def test_memory_revocation_clears_call_transcript_and_cancels_current_turn(store):
    ledger, _ = store
    ledger.memory_control("u1", enabled=True)
    initial = start(ledger)
    cap = begin(ledger, initial).capability
    ledger.memory_control("u1", enabled=False)
    with pytest.raises(ExecutionRejected):
        finish(ledger, cap)
    call = get_call(ledger, "u1", initial.call_id)
    assert call.phase == "interrupted" and not call.last_transcript and not call.last_audio_url


def test_polling_does_not_refresh_ttl_and_expired_key_never_recreates_call(store):
    ledger, clock = store
    first = start(ledger)
    expiry = first.metadata["voice_call_expires_ms"]
    clock[0] += 10_000
    assert get_call(ledger, "u1", first.call_id).metadata["voice_call_expires_ms"] == expiry
    clock[0] = expiry
    assert get_call(ledger, "u1", first.call_id) is None
    with pytest.raises(VoiceCallNotFound):
        start(ledger)


@pytest.mark.parametrize("value", [True, -1, "01", "1.0", "1e0", "", None])
def test_generation_input_rejects_ambiguous_values(value):
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        parse_generation(value)


def test_audio_ready_requires_registered_resources_and_no_partial_commit(store):
    ledger, _ = store
    cap = begin(ledger, start(ledger)).capability
    before = ledger.snapshot("u1")
    with pytest.raises(ExecutionRejected, match="publication_mismatch"):
        ledger.finish(cap, result={"output_id": "o1", "body": {"ok": True}},
                      voice_result={"transcript": "x", "output_id": "o1",
                                    "audio_url": "/audio/unregistered.wav", "has_audio": True})
    assert ledger.snapshot("u1") == before


def test_real_worker_late_asr_does_not_chat_or_publish_and_owns_cleanup(store, monkeypatch, tmp_path):
    ledger, _ = store
    monkeypatch.chdir(tmp_path)
    initial = start(ledger)
    cap = begin(ledger, initial).capability
    entered, release = Event(), Event()
    seen = []

    def transcribe(path):
        seen.append(Path(path))
        entered.set()
        assert release.wait(5)
        assert Path(path).is_file(), "HTTP cancellation must not delete a worker-owned input"
        return "late transcript"

    runtime = SimpleNamespace(transcribe_audio_file=transcribe, handle_chat_full=Mock())

    def work():
        with bind(ledger, cap):
            return _run_turn(runtime, initial.call_id, "u1", "synthetic", b"synthetic", ".wav")

    with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=1) as pool:
        future = pool.submit(work)
        try:
            assert entered.wait(5)
            control_call(ledger, user_id="u1", call_id=initial.call_id,
                         expected_generation=cap.generation, close=False)
        finally:
            release.set()
        with pytest.raises(ExecutionRejected):
            future.result(timeout=5)
    runtime.handle_chat_full.assert_not_called()
    assert seen and not seen[0].exists()
    assert not ledger.snapshot("u1")["objects"]
```

测试解释：`finish()` 的音频路径是纯账本测试描述符，不冒充存在的音频文件；真实文件授权/下载见第 12 节。最后一个用例才使用真实私有临时输入并控制实际 worker 的交错，证明资格撤销先于后续聊天，而不是只测试一个孤立 bool。

### 26.2 新增 `tests/api/test_execution_voice_contract.py`

文件功能：真实 Voice 路由、真实 `ExecutionRoute`、第五批真实身份校验、真实仓储和账本；只替换 ASR/聊天内容生成及人格配置，以免依赖模型。它不是完整模型端到端测试，也不把合成音频当作真实 ASR 识别质量证据。

```python
from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import json
from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor
from threading import Event
from types import SimpleNamespace

import pytest
from fastapi import Depends, FastAPI
from fastapi.testclient import TestClient

from aiagent.perception.voice_call_store import VoiceRealtimeCallStore
from aiagent.schemas.outputs import ResponsePacket
from aiagent.state.execution_context import call, current
from aiagent.state.execution_ledger import ExecutionLedger, LocalAtomic
from apps.api import execution_route, user_auth
from apps.api.routes import voice_realtime as routes

TOKEN1 = "synthetic_voice_u1_" + "x" * 44
TOKEN2 = "synthetic_voice_u2_" + "y" * 44


def headers(key="turn", token=TOKEN1):
    return {"Authorization": f"Bearer {token}", "Idempotency-Key": key}


@pytest.fixture
def api(monkeypatch, tmp_path):
    monkeypatch.chdir(tmp_path)
    monkeypatch.setattr(user_auth.settings, "api_auth_mode", "bearer")
    monkeypatch.setattr(user_auth.settings, "api_auth_token_sha256_map", json.dumps({
        hashlib.sha256(TOKEN1.encode()).hexdigest(): "u1",
        hashlib.sha256(TOKEN2.encode()).hexdigest(): "u2",
    }))
    monkeypatch.setattr(user_auth.settings, "app_env", "test")
    monkeypatch.setattr(user_auth.settings, "enable_local_audio_playback", False)
    monkeypatch.setattr(user_auth.cloud_settings, "cloud_mode", False)
    monkeypatch.setattr(user_auth.cloud_settings, "execution_state_mode", "local")
    ledger = ExecutionLedger(LocalAtomic())
    store = VoiceRealtimeCallStore(prefix=ledger.prefix, provider="memory", ledger=ledger)
    monkeypatch.setattr(routes, "_CALL_STORE", store)
    state = SimpleNamespace(asr=0, chat=0, empty=False, hold=False, entered=Event(),
                            release=Event(), finished=Event())

    def transcribe(path):
        assert current(required=True) is not None
        state.asr += 1
        state.entered.set()
        try:
            if state.hold:
                assert state.release.wait(5)
            return call("asr", lambda timeout: "" if state.empty else "synthetic question", per_call_seconds=5)
        finally:
            state.finished.set()

    def chat(**kwargs):
        state.chat += 1
        frame = current(required=True)
        reply = call("llm", lambda timeout: "synthetic reply", per_call_seconds=5)
        frame.history_pair = (kwargs["text"], reply)
        return SimpleNamespace(output_id="synthetic-output", packet=ResponsePacket(reply_text=reply))

    runtime = SimpleNamespace(
        transcribe_audio_file=transcribe, handle_chat_full=chat,
        dispatcher=SimpleNamespace(execution_ledger=ledger, persona_manager=SimpleNamespace(
            get_active_persona=lambda: SimpleNamespace(config=SimpleNamespace(model_dump=lambda **kw: {})),
        )),
    )
    monkeypatch.setattr(routes, "get_runtime", lambda: runtime)
    monkeypatch.setattr(execution_route, "get_runtime", lambda: runtime)
    app = FastAPI(dependencies=[Depends(user_auth.enforce_http_policy)])
    app.include_router(routes.router)
    with TestClient(app, raise_server_exceptions=False) as client:
        try:
            yield SimpleNamespace(client=client, ledger=ledger, store=store, state=state)
        finally:
            state.release.set()


def start(api):
    response = api.client.post("/voice/realtime/start", headers=headers("start"),
                               json={"user_id": "u1", "username": "synthetic"})
    assert response.status_code == 200, response.text
    return response.json()


def turn(api, call, key="turn"):
    return api.client.post(
        "/voice/realtime/turn", headers=headers(key),
        data={"call_id": call["call_id"], "user_id": "u1", "username": "synthetic",
              "expected_generation": str(call["execution_generation"])},
        files={"file": ("synthetic.wav", b"synthetic-audio-content", "audio/wav")},
    )


def test_authenticated_first_voice_request_initializes_before_store_access(api, monkeypatch):
    api.store._ledger = None
    initialized = []

    def initialize():
        api.store.bind_ledger(api.ledger)
        initialized.append(True)
        return object()

    monkeypatch.setattr(routes, "get_runtime", initialize)
    initial = start(api)
    assert initial["execution_generation"] == 0 and initialized == [True]


def test_unauthenticated_first_voice_request_does_not_initialize(api, monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.setattr(routes, "get_runtime", lambda: pytest.fail("must authorize before initialization"))
    response = api.client.post("/voice/realtime/start", json={"user_id": "u1"})
    assert response.status_code == 401


def test_missing_voice_generation_rejected_before_asr(api):
    initial = start(api)
    response = api.client.post(
        "/voice/realtime/turn", headers=headers(), data={"call_id": initial["call_id"], "user_id": "u1"},
        files={"file": ("synthetic.wav", b"synthetic", "audio/wav")},
    )
    assert response.status_code == 400
    assert api.state.asr == api.state.chat == 0


def test_success_and_duplicate_contract_no_second_asr_or_chat(api):
    initial = start(api)
    response = turn(api, initial)
    assert response.status_code == 200, response.text
    body = response.json()
    assert body["reply"] == "synthetic reply" and body["phase"] == "completed"
    assert body["execution_generation"] == 1 and body["turn_count"] == 1
    assert body["audio_path"] == "" and body["audio_segments"] == []
    assert body["call"]["last_audio_path"] == ""
    duplicate = turn(api, initial)
    assert duplicate.status_code == 200 and duplicate.json() == body
    assert api.state.asr == api.state.chat == 1


def test_empty_turn_regression_no_llm_or_history(api):
    api.state.empty = True
    response = turn(api, start(api))
    assert response.status_code == 200, response.text
    body = response.json()
    assert body["phase"] == "empty_turn" and body["transcript"] == body["reply"] == ""
    assert api.state.asr == 1 and api.state.chat == 0
    assert all(not session["history"] for session in api.ledger.snapshot("u1")["sessions"].values())


def test_cross_user_and_unauthenticated_call_control_never_enter_asr(api):
    initial = start(api)
    path = f"/voice/realtime/state/{initial['call_id']}"
    assert api.client.get(path).status_code == 401
    assert api.client.get(path, headers=headers(token=TOKEN2)).status_code == 404
    response = api.client.post("/voice/realtime/end", headers=headers(token=TOKEN2), json={
        "call_id": initial["call_id"], "expected_generation": 0,
    })
    assert response.status_code == 404 and api.state.asr == 0


def test_interrupt_during_turn_rejects_late_result_and_accepts_new_generation(api):
    initial = start(api)
    api.state.hold = True
    with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=1) as pool:
        future = pool.submit(turn, api, initial)
        try:
            assert api.state.entered.wait(5)
            interrupted = api.client.post("/voice/realtime/interrupt", headers=headers("interrupt"), json={
                "call_id": initial["call_id"], "expected_generation": 1,
                "reason": "PRIVATE_REASON_CANARY",
            })
            assert interrupted.status_code == 200, interrupted.text
        finally:
            api.state.release.set()
        assert future.result(timeout=5).status_code == 409
    assert api.state.chat == 0 and not api.ledger.snapshot("u1")["objects"]
    assert "PRIVATE_REASON_CANARY" not in json.dumps(api.ledger.snapshot("u1"))
    api.state.hold = False
    next_turn = turn(api, interrupted.json(), "next-turn")
    assert next_turn.status_code == 200, next_turn.text
    stale = api.client.post("/voice/realtime/end", headers=headers("stale-end"), json={
        "call_id": initial["call_id"], "expected_generation": 1,
    })
    assert stale.status_code == 409


def test_end_idempotency_no_old_turn_replay_and_generation_required(api):
    initial = start(api)
    completed = turn(api, initial).json()
    payload = {"call_id": initial["call_id"], "expected_generation": completed["execution_generation"]}
    first = api.client.post("/voice/realtime/end", headers=headers("end"), json=payload)
    assert first.status_code == 200
    assert api.client.post("/voice/realtime/end", headers=headers("end"), json=payload).json() == first.json()
    assert turn(api, initial).status_code == 404
    assert api.state.asr == 1


def test_actual_http_timeout_does_not_restart_late_thread(api, monkeypatch):
    initial = start(api)
    original_begin = api.ledger.begin

    def short_admission(**kwargs):
        kwargs["deadline_seconds"] = 1
        return original_begin(**kwargs)

    monkeypatch.setattr(api.ledger, "begin", short_admission)
    api.state.hold = True
    try:
        response = turn(api, initial)
        assert response.status_code == 504, response.text
        assert api.state.entered.is_set() and not api.state.finished.is_set()
        records = list(api.ledger.snapshot("u1")["operations"].values())
        assert len(records) == 1 and records[0]["status"] == "unknown"
        assert turn(api, initial).status_code == 409
        assert api.state.asr == 1 and api.state.chat == 0
    finally:
        api.state.release.set()
        assert api.state.finished.wait(5)
    assert not api.ledger.snapshot("u1")["objects"]
```

解释：上述身份测试没有 monkeypatch `enforce_http_policy()`；Token 摘要映射走真实第五批验证。原文 canary 只用于测试，断言自由打断原因没有进入账本。第五批旧测试中无 generation 的控制请求须按新合同增加字段，不能把 422 改成忽略。这里不创建实际音频输出，音频权限覆盖由第 12 节和 26.1 的共同提交对照承担。

### 26.3 新增 `tests/integration/test_voice_execution_redis.py`

文件功能：真实 Redis、spawn 子进程持有实际资格并准备调用真实发布函数；父进程通过同一生产控制函数打断，再允许子进程迟到提交。没有传递进程内锁、frame、HMAC 或假 Redis。

```python
from __future__ import annotations

import multiprocessing
import os
import secrets

import pytest
from redis import Redis

from aiagent.state.execution_ledger import Capability, ExecutionLedger, ExecutionRejected, RedisAtomic, digest
from aiagent.state.voice_execution import control_call, get_call, start_call, voice_session


def _late_publish(url, prefix, values, ready, release, output):
    client = Redis.from_url(url, decode_responses=True, socket_connect_timeout=2, socket_timeout=2)
    ledger = ExecutionLedger(RedisAtomic(client), prefix)
    cap = Capability(**values)
    try:
        ledger.check(cap)
        ready.set()
        if not release.wait(15):
            raise RuntimeError("test_release_timeout")
        ledger.finish(cap, result={"output_id": "late", "body": {"ok": True}},
                      voice_result={"transcript": "late", "output_id": "late",
                                    "audio_url": "", "has_audio": False})
        output.put("unexpected_commit")
    except ExecutionRejected:
        output.put("rejected")
    except Exception as exc:
        output.put(type(exc).__name__)
    finally:
        client.close()


def test_real_redis_cross_process_interrupt_fences_late_voice_commit():
    from dataclasses import asdict

    url = os.environ.get("BATCH06_REDIS_URL")
    if not url:
        pytest.skip("dedicated BATCH06_REDIS_URL required")
    client = Redis.from_url(url, decode_responses=True, socket_connect_timeout=2, socket_timeout=2)
    prefix = "batch06-voice-" + secrets.token_hex(12)
    ledger = ExecutionLedger(RedisAtomic(client), prefix)
    ctx = multiprocessing.get_context("spawn")
    ready, release, output = ctx.Event(), ctx.Event(), ctx.Queue()
    process = None
    try:
        client.ping()
        call = start_call(ledger, user_id="synthetic-user", username="synthetic", idempotency_key="start")
        cap = ledger.begin(user_id=call.user_id, session_id=voice_session(call.user_id, call.call_id),
                           idempotency_key="turn", payload_digest=digest("turn"),
                           voice_call_id=call.call_id, expected_voice_generation=0).capability
        ledger.start(cap)
        process = ctx.Process(target=_late_publish, args=(url, prefix, asdict(cap), ready, release, output))
        process.start()
        assert ready.wait(15)
        control_call(ledger, user_id=call.user_id, call_id=call.call_id,
                     expected_generation=cap.generation, close=False)
        release.set()
        assert output.get(timeout=15) == "rejected"
        process.join(15)
        assert process.exitcode == 0
        assert get_call(ledger, call.user_id, call.call_id).phase == "interrupted"
        root = ledger.snapshot(call.user_id)
        assert "result" not in root["operations"][cap.operation_id]
        assert not root["objects"]
    finally:
        release.set()
        if process is not None and process.is_alive():
            process.terminate()
            process.join(5)
        client.delete(ledger.key("synthetic-user"))
        client.close()
```

**仅提供、未执行的本段验证指令：**

```powershell
.\.venv\Scripts\python.exe -m ruff check aiagent/state aiagent/perception/voice_call_store.py apps/api/execution_route.py apps/api/routes/voice_realtime.py tests/unit/test_voice_execution.py tests/api/test_execution_voice_contract.py tests/integration/test_voice_execution_redis.py
.\.venv\Scripts\python.exe -m pytest tests/unit/test_voice_execution.py tests/api/test_execution_voice_contract.py -q
# BATCH06_REDIS_URL 必须由你设置为专用测试 Redis，不可用生产库。
.\.venv\Scripts\python.exe -m pytest tests/integration/test_voice_execution_redis.py -q
```

旧 `tests/*voice*` 中依赖独立 `_calls/clear_for_tests/save/next_turn` 的仓储单测需要改成显式注入账本、原子准入与期望 generation，不恢复旧接口。真实 API smoke 增加先 start -> turn -> state -> 带当前 generation 的 interrupt/end，且在第七批前不再断言“有音频就 SPEAKING”。这是一项有意的合同修正，不是删断言来隐藏回归。

## 27. RAG 嵌入与本地 ASR：实际计算点预算

### 27.1 `aiagent/knowledge/vector_store.py` 的精确修改

文件功能仍是 RAG 的嵌入和 FAISS 适配，不替换你已写好的响应数量、索引、有限数值和维度校验。新增 imports：

```python
from aiagent.state.execution_context import call, current
```

`OpenAICompatibleEmbeddings.__init__()` 创建 `OpenAI(...)` 时增加 `max_retries=0, timeout=60.0` 两个参数。实际发送处仍使用下面的剩余预算覆盖默认 timeout，不能只改构造器就认为满足整轮超时。

`_embed_batch()` 第一行、现有 `cleaned = ...` 之前增加：

```python
        if (not texts or len(texts) > 64 or any(not isinstance(text, str) for text in texts)
                or sum(len(text.encode("utf-8")) for text in texts) > 64 * 1024):
            raise EmbeddingValidationError(
                "embedding_input_budget_exceeded", "Embedding batch input exceeds policy",
                validation_stage="input",
            )
```

原来的 `response = self.client.embeddings.with_raw_response.create(**kwargs)` 单行完整替换成：

```python
            response = call(
                "rag_embedding",
                lambda timeout: self.client.with_options(
                    timeout=timeout, max_retries=0,
                ).embeddings.with_raw_response.create(**kwargs),
                per_call_seconds=60.0,
            )
```

保留原 `except ValueError ...`、原始 JSON 解析及全部 response validation。方法最后 `return vectors` 之前增加：

```python
        frame = current(required=True)
        assert frame is not None
        frame.remaining()
```

在 `OpenAICompatibleEmbeddings` 之后、`LangChainVectorStore` 之前新增下方完整类，不另建 embeddings 工厂：

```python
class BudgetedLocalEmbeddings(Embeddings):
    def __init__(self, underlying: Embeddings) -> None:
        self.underlying = underlying

    @staticmethod
    def _checked(texts: list[str]) -> None:
        if (not texts or len(texts) > 64 or any(not isinstance(text, str) for text in texts)
                or sum(len(text.encode("utf-8")) for text in texts) > 64 * 1024):
            raise EmbeddingValidationError(
                "embedding_input_budget_exceeded", "Local embedding input exceeds policy",
                validation_stage="input",
            )

    def embed_documents(self, texts: list[str]) -> list[list[float]]:
        self._checked(texts)
        return call("rag_embedding", lambda timeout: self.underlying.embed_documents(texts),
                    per_call_seconds=60.0)

    def embed_query(self, text: str) -> list[float]:
        self._checked([text])
        return call("rag_embedding", lambda timeout: self.underlying.embed_query(text),
                    per_call_seconds=60.0)
```

在 `LangChainVectorStore._build_embeddings()` 的本地分支，把原 `return self._build_huggingface_embeddings(...)` 改为 `return BudgetedLocalEmbeddings(self._build_huggingface_embeddings(...))`，中间已有所有参数原样保留，即：

```python
        if self.embedding_provider in {"huggingface", "local"}:
            return BudgetedLocalEmbeddings(self._build_huggingface_embeddings(
                embedding_model_name=embedding_model_name,
                embedding_model_path=embedding_model_path,
                device=device,
                normalize_embeddings=normalize_embeddings,
                local_files_only=local_files_only,
            ))
```

**解释：** OpenAI-compatible 每个实际 batch 占一次 `rag_embedding`，查询上限 2 次。HuggingFace 的一次有界本地批推理也占一次计算槽，不伪称它是远程收费请求。`timeout` 无法强停本地 native 推理，只保证开始前和返回后资格检查；不能绕开最终提交。建库必须使用后续维护装配，不能以“是建库”为由遇到无 frame 时自动放行。当前 `SimpleReranker` 是本地确定性算分，没有远程模型外呼，不为它伪造 Provider usage。

### 27.2 完整替换 `integrations/asr/faster_whisper_client.py`

文件功能：本地模型只从已准备好的文件加载；一次转写从调用到消费惰性 segments 都在一个 `asr` 槽位内。删除原来记录完整 transcript 和输入路径的日志。`WhisperModel` 延迟导入，API 远程 ASR 部署不因 import 这个模块就加载本地推理包。

```python
from __future__ import annotations

import math
from pathlib import Path

from aiagent.state.execution_context import call, current
from aiagent.state.execution_ledger import ExecutionRejected


class UnavailableASRClient:
    def transcribe(self, audio_path: str) -> str:
        raise RuntimeError("asr_capability_unavailable")


class FasterWhisperClient:
    def __init__(self, model_size: str = "small", model_path: str = "",
                 device: str = "cpu", compute_type: str = "int8", language: str = "zh") -> None:
        path = Path(model_path) if model_path.strip() else None
        if path is None or not path.is_dir():
            raise ValueError("asr_local_model_directory_required")
        from faster_whisper import WhisperModel

        self.language = language
        self.model = WhisperModel(
            str(path.resolve()), device=device, compute_type=compute_type,
            local_files_only=True,
        )

    def transcribe(self, audio_path: str) -> str:
        path = Path(audio_path)
        if not path.is_file() or not 0 < path.stat().st_size <= 25 * 1024 * 1024:
            raise ValueError("asr_input_file_invalid")

        def decode(timeout: float) -> str:
            frame = current(required=True)
            assert frame is not None
            frame.remaining()
            segments, info = self.model.transcribe(str(path), language=self.language, vad_filter=True)
            try:
                duration = getattr(info, "duration", None)
                if (isinstance(duration, bool) or not isinstance(duration, (float, int))
                        or not math.isfinite(duration) or not 0 <= duration <= 60):
                    raise ExecutionRejected("asr_audio_duration_exceeded")
                parts: list[str] = []
                characters = 0
                for index, segment in enumerate(segments):
                    frame.remaining()
                    if index >= 1024 or not isinstance(segment.text, str):
                        raise ExecutionRejected("asr_segment_budget_exceeded")
                    text = segment.text.strip()
                    characters += len(text)
                    if characters > 16_384:
                        raise ExecutionRejected("asr_transcript_budget_exceeded")
                    parts.append(text)
                frame.remaining()
                return "".join(parts).strip()
            finally:
                close = getattr(segments, "close", None)
                if callable(close):
                    close()

        return call("asr", decode, per_call_seconds=60.0)
```

**兼容说明：** `model_size` 参数为调用签名兼容保留，但不再触发运行时下载；请设置已有 `ASR_MODEL_PATH` 为本地完整模型目录。native decoder 可能先解码文件才返回 duration，因此 60 秒限制不是媒体解析沙箱，也不能防止全部解码峰值；本批保留风险，不冒充 Rust 媒体隔离。共享取消只撤提交资格，不能杀掉 CTranslate2 工作线程。

`apps/core/bootstrap.py` 导入 `UnavailableASRClient`，在 ASR 初始化的既有 `except Exception as exc:` 内，将 `asr_client = MockASRClient()` **仅该处**替换为：

```python
        asr_client = UnavailableASRClient()
```

同一 `mark_degraded(...)` 文案改为 `"ASR unavailable; explicit mock mode was not enabled."`。配置明确选择 mock 的正常分支仍使用 `MockASRClient()`，不要全局替换。这是为防止真实 ASR 加载失败后返回合成转写却被 Voice 提交为真实用户发言。若当前 ASR 客户端类型注解要求枚举 union，在 `ASRListener.__init__` 的原 union 中追加 `UnavailableASRClient` 并同步 import；`transcribe_file()` 继续原样调用，不在其 catch 中回退 Mock。

### 27.3 新增 `tests/unit/test_rag_asr_execution_budget.py`

文件功能：真实 OpenAI SDK 的 MockTransport 请求计数、429 无自动重试、缺资格零调用、取消后无结果；本地 embedding 和 ASR 使用模型替身，不加载模型/音频解码器，不访问网络。

```python
from __future__ import annotations

import sys
from types import SimpleNamespace
from unittest.mock import Mock

import httpx
import pytest
from openai import OpenAI, RateLimitError

from aiagent.knowledge.vector_store import BudgetedLocalEmbeddings, OpenAICompatibleEmbeddings
from aiagent.state.execution_context import bind
from aiagent.state.execution_ledger import (
    ExecutionBudgetExceeded, ExecutionLedger, ExecutionRejected, LocalAtomic, digest,
)
from integrations.asr.faster_whisper_client import FasterWhisperClient, UnavailableASRClient


def running():
    ledger = ExecutionLedger(LocalAtomic())
    cap = ledger.begin(user_id="u1", session_id="compute", idempotency_key="one",
                       payload_digest=digest("compute")).capability
    ledger.start(cap)
    return ledger, cap


def embedding(handler):
    result = OpenAICompatibleEmbeddings(model="synthetic", api_key="synthetic", base_url="https://model.invalid/v1",
                                        dimensions=2)
    result.client.close()
    result.client = OpenAI(api_key="synthetic", base_url="https://model.invalid/v1", max_retries=0,
                           http_client=httpx.Client(transport=httpx.MockTransport(handler)))
    return result


def test_actual_rag_sdk_two_calls_then_budget_refusal():
    requests = []

    def handler(request):
        requests.append(request)
        return httpx.Response(200, json={"data": [{"index": 0, "embedding": [0.2, 0.4]}]})

    adapter = embedding(handler)
    ledger, cap = running()
    try:
        with pytest.raises(ExecutionRejected, match="context_required"):
            adapter.embed_query("no capability")
        assert not requests
        with bind(ledger, cap):
            assert adapter.embed_query("a") == [0.2, 0.4]
            assert adapter.embed_query("b") == [0.2, 0.4]
            with pytest.raises(ExecutionBudgetExceeded):
                adapter.embed_query("third")
        assert len(requests) == 2
        assert ledger.operation("u1", cap.operation_id)["calls"] == {"rag_embedding": 2}
    finally:
        adapter.client.close()


def test_rag_429_no_sdk_retry_and_failure_still_spends_one_call():
    requests = []

    def handler(request):
        requests.append(request)
        return httpx.Response(429, json={"error": {"message": "synthetic limited", "type": "rate_limit"}})

    adapter = embedding(handler)
    ledger, cap = running()
    try:
        with bind(ledger, cap), pytest.raises(RateLimitError):
            adapter.embed_query("a")
        assert len(requests) == 1
        assert ledger.operation("u1", cap.operation_id)["total_calls"] == 1
    finally:
        adapter.client.close()


def test_cancelled_rag_response_not_returned_and_local_embedding_has_same_guard():
    ledger, cap = running()

    def handler(request):
        ledger.cancel_operation("u1", cap.operation_id)
        return httpx.Response(200, json={"data": [{"index": 0, "embedding": [0.2, 0.4]}]})

    adapter = embedding(handler)
    try:
        with bind(ledger, cap), pytest.raises(ExecutionRejected):
            adapter.embed_query("cancel while sending")
    finally:
        adapter.client.close()
    local = BudgetedLocalEmbeddings(Mock())
    with pytest.raises(ExecutionRejected):
        local.embed_query("no capability")
    local.underlying.embed_query.assert_not_called()


def test_local_asr_lazy_segments_are_guarded_and_closed(monkeypatch, tmp_path):
    ledger, cap = running()
    closed = []

    def segments():
        try:
            yield SimpleNamespace(text="first")
            ledger.cancel_operation("u1", cap.operation_id)
            yield SimpleNamespace(text="late")
        finally:
            closed.append(True)

    model = SimpleNamespace(transcribe=lambda *a, **kw: (segments(), SimpleNamespace(duration=1.0)))
    factory = Mock(return_value=model)
    monkeypatch.setitem(sys.modules, "faster_whisper", SimpleNamespace(WhisperModel=factory))
    model_dir = tmp_path / "model"
    model_dir.mkdir()
    client = FasterWhisperClient(model_path=str(model_dir))
    assert factory.call_args.kwargs["local_files_only"] is True
    source = tmp_path / "synthetic.wav"
    source.write_bytes(b"synthetic")
    with bind(ledger, cap), pytest.raises(ExecutionRejected):
        client.transcribe(str(source))
    assert closed == [True]
    assert ledger.operation("u1", cap.operation_id)["calls"] == {"asr": 1}


def test_asr_normal_and_unavailable_contrast(monkeypatch, tmp_path):
    ledger, cap = running()
    model = SimpleNamespace(transcribe=lambda *a, **kw: (
        iter([SimpleNamespace(text="你好"), SimpleNamespace(text="世界")]), SimpleNamespace(duration=2.0),
    ))
    monkeypatch.setitem(sys.modules, "faster_whisper", SimpleNamespace(WhisperModel=lambda *a, **kw: model))
    model_dir = tmp_path / "model"
    model_dir.mkdir()
    source = tmp_path / "synthetic.wav"
    source.write_bytes(b"synthetic")
    client = FasterWhisperClient(model_path=str(model_dir))
    with bind(ledger, cap):
        assert client.transcribe(str(source)) == "你好世界"
    with pytest.raises(RuntimeError, match="capability_unavailable"):
        UnavailableASRClient().transcribe(str(source))
```

**验证指令仅提供，未执行：**

```powershell
.\.venv\Scripts\python.exe -m pytest tests/unit/test_rag_asr_execution_budget.py tests/unit/test_rag_embedding_response.py -q
```

既有 `test_rag_embedding_response.py` 中实际走 SDK 的测试需要显式使用第 13.5 节的 `provider_execution`，或在各自 fixture 中绑定独立 ledger；纯响应验证不应改断言。原始宽泛 catch 在 RAGRunner.run、RAGPipeline.inspect/retrieve 等降级点必须先调用已有的 `raise_if_execution_error(exc)`，预算/取消/共享存储异常不得变成“可选 RAG 失败后继续生成”。后续整链节会统一给出剩余入口装配，不能只应用本节后开放缺少维护资格的建库服务。

## 28. 独立评测：每个 case/实际回合一份预算，不向业务提供匿名旁路

### 28.1 新增 `aiagent/evaluation/execution_scope.py`

文件功能：组件评测拥有显式、短期、隔离的执行上下文；只在评测代码 import，普通 Provider 缺上下文仍拒绝。每次 scope 新建 LocalAtomic 是**评测隔离设计**，不是生产 Redis 断连 fallback，不用于真实 Runtime 的 Memory/资源发布。远端 API 的资格仍在服务端签发。

```python
from __future__ import annotations

from collections.abc import Generator
from contextlib import contextmanager
from typing import Any

from aiagent.state.execution_context import ExecutionFrame, bind, current
from aiagent.state.execution_ledger import ExecutionLedger, ExecutionRejected, LocalAtomic, digest


@contextmanager
def evaluation_scope(label: str, payload: Any) -> Generator[ExecutionFrame, None, None]:
    if current() is not None:
        raise ExecutionRejected("evaluation_scope_must_not_replace_active_execution")
    ledger = ExecutionLedger(LocalAtomic(), prefix="isolated-component-evaluation")
    cap = ledger.begin(
        user_id="synthetic-evaluation", session_id="component:" + digest(label),
        idempotency_key="single", payload_digest=digest(payload),
    ).capability
    ledger.start(cap)
    try:
        with bind(ledger, cap) as frame:
            yield frame
            if frame.staged_objects or frame.staged_memories or frame.voice_result is not None:
                raise ExecutionRejected("evaluation_component_cannot_publish_business_effects")
            ledger.finish(cap, result={"evaluation_executed": True})
    except BaseException:
        ledger.terminal(cap, "unknown")
        raise
```

`evaluation_executed=True` 只说明评测工作执行完，不等于 oracle/质量通过；质量仍以既有 Result/StageCheck 为准。总预算仍为 120 秒/24 次；不会给整个数据集一个长达数小时的 frame。多轮 Persona 的每个回合单独 scope，不能把 llm 槽位上限改成整个数据集大小。

### 28.2 `aiagent/knowledge/evaluation/runner.py`

导入 `evaluation_scope`。现有 `evaluate_query_case()` **仅改名**为 `_evaluate_query_case()`，保留现有所有指标计算；在它前面添加：

```python
def evaluate_query_case(pipeline: RAGPipeline, case: RAGEvalCase) -> RAGEvalCaseResult:
    with evaluation_scope("retrieval:" + case.case_id, case.model_dump(mode="json")):
        return _evaluate_query_case(pipeline, case)
```

同文件最上方新增：

```python
from aiagent.evaluation.execution_scope import evaluation_scope
```

`_evaluate_query_case()` 最后异常结果中的 `error=f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}"` 改为 `error=type(exc).__name__`，不要把 Provider 原文写入报告。现有粗召回和 pipeline.inspect 各可能执行一次嵌入，正好受到 2 次上限；若未来加第三次，应修实际重复检索或明确修改预算策略，不能在评测入口悄悄放宽。

### 28.3 `aiagent/evaluation/adapters.py`

导入 `evaluation_scope`。原 `rag_observation()` **仅改名**为 `_rag_observation()`，保留其全部现有 BM25/API 和回答归因逻辑；在它前面添加：

```python
def rag_observation(params: RAGInput, root: Path, user: str) -> Observation:
    with evaluation_scope("rag-component", params.model_dump(mode="json")):
        return _rag_observation(params, root, user)
```

原 `persona_observation()` 的 `for text in params.turns:` 内，只将实际调用改为以下形状，后面的 normalize/history/transcript/raw_answers 操作保持原样：

```python
    for index, text in enumerate(params.turns):
        with evaluation_scope(f"persona-turn:{index}", {"text": text, "history_size": len(history)}):
            raw = service.invoke_messages_strict(service.build_messages(
                system_prompt=persona.build_system_prompt(), user_text=text, history_messages=history,
            ))
        answer = persona.normalize_reply(raw) if params.apply_guard else raw
        history.extend([HumanMessage(content=text), AIMessage(content=answer)])
        transcript.append({"user": text, "answer": answer})
        raw_answers.append(raw)
```

不要在整个 `observe()` 外统一包 scope：真实 graph/runtime 有自己的 ledger 和 owner，HTTP API 也由服务器签发资格，给它们再造一个本地假主体会造成 ledger/checkpoint 作用域冲突。真实 graph 的准确接线见第 31 节，不能把此处组件 scope 宣称覆盖了完整 Runtime。

### 28.4 `aiagent/evaluation/scoring.py` 与 `semantic.py`

`scoring.py` 导入 `evaluation_scope`。`evaluate_one()` 中保留 `observation = observer(...)`，仅把下一行 `result = score(...)` 替换为：

```python
        with evaluation_scope("judge:" + key, {"case_id": case.case_id, "key": key}):
            result = score(case, observation, judge, key)
```

`execution_context.py` 第 3 节 `CALL_LIMITS` 新增 `"judge": 2`，允许一次初始判分和既有至多一次结构修复；它和 Judge 已有全数据集 request_budget 是两个同时成立的约束。

`semantic.py` 导入 `call`：

```python
from aiagent.state.execution_context import call
```

在 `Judge._request()` 内保留现有所有错误分类、`request_options`、模型与 token 设置，仅用以下完整块替换原 `with OpenAI(**kwargs) as client: return client.chat.completions.create(...)`：

```python
            def send(timeout):
                with OpenAI(**{**kwargs, "timeout": timeout, "max_retries": 0}) as client:
                    return client.chat.completions.create(
                        model=str(self.identity["model"]), temperature=0,
                        messages=messages, response_format={"type": "json_object"},
                        max_tokens=self.max_output_tokens, **request_options,
                    )

            return call("judge", send, per_call_seconds=60.0)
```

`score()` 原有 judge_error 结果继续保留，预算不足只能得到失败结果，不能继续修复或借用下一 case 的额度。直接调用 `Judge.evaluate()` 的真实 SDK contract 测试必须显式用 `evaluation_scope`；Mock Judge 无外呼的测试无需授权。

### 28.5 `aiagent/knowledge/evaluation/agent_quality.py`

导入同一个 `evaluation_scope`，在 `evaluate_provider()` 每次 iteration 的 `try` 内，把从 `system, user = build_case_prompt(...)` 到 `answer = service.invoke_messages_strict(messages)` 这段替换为：

```python
                with evaluation_scope("legacy-answer:" + key, case.model_dump(mode="json")) as frame:
                    system, user = build_case_prompt(case, dataset, pipeline, persona)
                    info["prompt_sha256"] = fingerprint({"system": system, "user": user})
                    messages = service.build_messages(system_prompt=system, user_text=user)
                    stage = "generation"
                    calls += 1
                    answer = service.invoke_messages_strict(messages)
                    record = frame.ledger.operation(frame.capability.user_id, frame.capability.operation_id)
                    info["execution_calls"] = dict(record["calls"])
```

既有空答检查与 except 不变；`result["transport_retries"]` 改为 `"SDK retries disabled; per-case ledger counts actual budgeted sends"`。`calls` 仍是逻辑调用计数，不能把它混同网络发送次数。项目当前两个评测入口都要接线，不能只更新旧 runner 后忽略你新写的 `aiagent/evaluation/`。

### 28.6 新增 `tests/unit/test_evaluation_execution_scope.py`

文件功能：验证隔离 case 之间不串预算、同一回合不能超额、缺资格仍拒绝、不能嵌套 scope 覆盖真实上下文，以及组件评测不能发布业务副作用。

```python
from __future__ import annotations

import pytest

from aiagent.evaluation.execution_scope import evaluation_scope
from aiagent.state.execution_context import call, current
from aiagent.state.execution_ledger import ExecutionBudgetExceeded, ExecutionRejected


def test_each_component_case_has_own_bounded_budget_and_no_context_leak():
    ledgers = []
    for index in range(3):
        assert current() is None
        with evaluation_scope(str(index), {"case": index}) as frame:
            ledgers.append(frame.ledger)
            assert call("llm", lambda timeout: "answer", per_call_seconds=5) == "answer"
            with pytest.raises(ExecutionBudgetExceeded):
                call("llm", lambda timeout: "unexpected", per_call_seconds=5)
    assert current() is None
    assert len({id(ledger) for ledger in ledgers}) == 3
    with pytest.raises(ExecutionRejected, match="context_required"):
        call("llm", lambda timeout: "unexpected", per_call_seconds=5)


def test_nested_scope_cannot_replace_current_qualification():
    with evaluation_scope("parent", {}) as frame:
        with pytest.raises(ExecutionRejected, match="must_not_replace"):
            with evaluation_scope("child", {}):
                pytest.fail("must reject before entry")
        assert current() is frame


def test_judge_repair_budget_is_two_actual_sends():
    with evaluation_scope("judge", {}):
        for _ in range(2):
            call("judge", lambda timeout: "synthetic verdict", per_call_seconds=5)
        with pytest.raises(ExecutionBudgetExceeded):
            call("judge", lambda timeout: "unexpected third", per_call_seconds=5)


def test_component_scope_rejects_business_resource_publication():
    with pytest.raises(ExecutionRejected, match="cannot_publish_business_effects"):
        with evaluation_scope("not-runtime", {}) as frame:
            frame.staged_objects["output:forbidden"] = {"kind": "output"}
    assert current() is None
```
