
## 29. 可重复的 HTTP 时延与调用预算基线入口

### 29.1 新增 `scripts/benchmark_execution_v12.py`

文件功能：向已经由你启动的专用测试服务发送有界聊天样本，读取本人 operation 的真实计数，记录环境/代码/配置/fixture 摘要、并发、延时分位、错误/降级率和阶段耗时。脚本默认拒绝运行，必须显式 `--execute`；本交付没有调用它。报告不保存问答或 Token，不将客户端首请求当成服务冷启动，不伪造首 token、服务端 CPU/RSS、Token 费用。

```python
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import hashlib
import ipaddress
import json
import math
import os
import platform
import re
import time
import uuid
from collections import Counter
from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor
from datetime import UTC, datetime
from pathlib import Path
from urllib.parse import urlsplit

import httpx

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
HEX64 = re.compile(r"[0-9a-f]{64}\Z")


def percentile(values: list[float], fraction: float) -> float | None:
    if not values:
        return None
    if not 0 < fraction <= 1:
        raise ValueError("invalid_percentile")
    ordered = sorted(values)
    return ordered[max(0, math.ceil(fraction * len(ordered)) - 1)]


def checked_origin(value: str, allow_remote: bool) -> str:
    parsed = urlsplit(value)
    if (parsed.scheme not in {"http", "https"} or not parsed.hostname
            or parsed.username or parsed.password or parsed.query or parsed.fragment
            or parsed.path not in {"", "/"}):
        raise ValueError("benchmark_origin_only_required")
    host = parsed.hostname.lower()
    local = host == "localhost"
    try:
        local = local or ipaddress.ip_address(host).is_loopback
    except ValueError:
        pass
    if not local and (not allow_remote or parsed.scheme != "https"):
        raise ValueError("remote_benchmark_requires_explicit_https")
    return value.rstrip("/")


def code_digest() -> str:
    result = hashlib.sha256()
    for package in ("aiagent", "apps", "cloud", "config"):
        for path in sorted((ROOT / package).rglob("*.py")):
            result.update(path.relative_to(ROOT).as_posix().encode("utf-8"))
            result.update(path.read_bytes())
    return result.hexdigest()


def bounded_json(client: httpx.Client, method: str, url: str, **kwargs):
    with client.stream(method, url, **kwargs) as response:
        content = bytearray()
        for chunk in response.iter_bytes(64 * 1024):
            if len(content) + len(chunk) > 1024 * 1024:
                raise ValueError("benchmark_response_budget_exceeded")
            content.extend(chunk)
        body = json.loads(content)
        if not isinstance(body, dict):
            raise ValueError("benchmark_object_response_required")
        return response.status_code, dict(response.headers), body


def summarize(rows):
    latencies = [row["elapsed_ms"] for row in rows]
    successes = [row for row in rows if row["success"]]
    calls = Counter()
    for row in rows:
        calls.update(row["calls"])
    return {
        "samples": len(rows), "successes": len(successes),
        "p50_ms": percentile(latencies, 0.50), "p95_ms": percentile(latencies, 0.95),
        "success_p95_ms": percentile([row["elapsed_ms"] for row in successes], 0.95),
        "error_rate": sum(not row["success"] for row in rows) / len(rows) if rows else None,
        "degraded_rate": sum(row["degraded"] for row in rows) / len(rows) if rows else None,
        "degradation_measurement": "metadata_reported_only_not_total_capability_availability",
        "states": dict(Counter(row["state"] for row in rows)), "stage_calls": dict(calls),
        "rows_without_call_evidence": sum(not row["call_evidence"] for row in rows),
    }


def main(argv=None) -> int:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    parser.add_argument("--execute", action="store_true")
    parser.add_argument("--origin", default="http://127.0.0.1:8000")
    parser.add_argument("--allow-remote", action="store_true")
    parser.add_argument("--user-id", required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--configuration-sha256", required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--fixture", type=Path, required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--output", type=Path, required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--requests", type=int, default=20)
    parser.add_argument("--concurrency", type=int, default=1)
    parser.add_argument("--warmup", type=int, default=2)
    args = parser.parse_args(argv)
    if not args.execute:
        parser.error("benchmark requires explicit --execute; it may invoke paid providers")
    origin = checked_origin(args.origin, args.allow_remote)
    if (not 1 <= args.requests <= 192 or not 0 <= args.warmup <= 8
            or not 1 <= args.concurrency <= 8 or not HEX64.fullmatch(args.configuration_sha256)
            or not args.user_id or len(args.user_id) > 128):
        parser.error("invalid bounded benchmark arguments")
    token = os.environ.get("BATCH06_BENCHMARK_TOKEN", "")
    if not re.fullmatch(r"[A-Za-z0-9_-]{43,256}", token):
        parser.error("set a dedicated BATCH06_BENCHMARK_TOKEN locally")
    if args.fixture.stat().st_size > 1024 * 1024:
        parser.error("fixture exceeds 1MiB")
    raw_fixture = args.fixture.read_bytes()
    prompts = json.loads(raw_fixture)
    if (not isinstance(prompts, list) or not 1 <= len(prompts) <= 256
            or any(not isinstance(text, str) or not text.strip() or len(text) > 4096 for text in prompts)):
        parser.error("fixture must be 1..256 nonblank synthetic prompt strings")
    if args.output.exists():
        parser.error("refusing to overwrite an existing evidence report")
    run_id = uuid.uuid4().hex
    headers = {"Authorization": f"Bearer {token}"}

    def work(index):
        row = {"index": index, "success": False, "degraded": False,
               "state": "unknown", "calls": {}, "call_evidence": False,
               "stage_latency_ms": {}, "status": 0, "error_type": ""}
        started = time.perf_counter()
        with httpx.Client(timeout=125, trust_env=False, follow_redirects=False) as client:
            try:
                status, response_headers, body = bounded_json(
                    client, "POST", origin + "/chat",
                    headers={**headers, "Idempotency-Key": f"bench-{run_id}-{index}"},
                    json={"user_id": args.user_id, "username": "Benchmark",
                          "session_id": f"bench-{run_id}-{index % args.concurrency}",
                          "text": prompts[index % len(prompts)]},
                )
                row["elapsed_ms"] = (time.perf_counter() - started) * 1000
                row["status"] = status
                row["state"] = response_headers.get("x-execution-state", "unknown")
                row["success"] = status == 200 and body.get("ok") is True and row["state"] == "completed"
                metadata = body.get("metadata", {})
                if isinstance(metadata, dict):
                    row["degraded"] = any(
                        str(value).lower() == "degraded"
                        or key.endswith("_status") and str(value).lower() == "failed"
                        or key.endswith("_degraded") and str(value).lower() in {"true", "1"}
                        for key, value in metadata.items()
                    )
                    for key, value in list(metadata.items())[:256]:
                        if not key.endswith("_latency_ms") or len(key) > 80:
                            continue
                        try:
                            number = float(value)
                        except (ValueError, TypeError):
                            continue
                        if math.isfinite(number) and number >= 0:
                            row["stage_latency_ms"][key] = number
                operation_id = response_headers.get("x-operation-id", "")
                if HEX64.fullmatch(operation_id):
                    evidence_status, _, evidence = bounded_json(
                        client, "GET", origin + f"/executions/{operation_id}", headers=headers,
                    )
                    counts = evidence.get("operation", {}).get("calls")
                    if (evidence_status == 200 and isinstance(counts, dict)
                            and len(counts) <= 32 and all(
                                isinstance(key, str) and len(key) <= 64 and type(value) is int and 0 <= value <= 24
                                for key, value in counts.items())):
                        row["calls"] = counts
                        row["call_evidence"] = True
            except Exception as exc:
                row["error_type"] = type(exc).__name__
        row.setdefault("elapsed_ms", (time.perf_counter() - started) * 1000)
        return row

    warmup = [work(index) for index in range(args.warmup)]
    # Exactly one sequential stream per session avoids accidental same-session contention.
    def stream(worker):
        return [work(args.warmup + index) for index in range(worker, args.requests, args.concurrency)]

    started = time.perf_counter()
    with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=args.concurrency) as pool:
        rows = [row for batch in pool.map(stream, range(args.concurrency)) for row in batch]
    wall_seconds = time.perf_counter() - started
    rows.sort(key=lambda row: row["index"])
    report = {
        "schema": "v12-execution-baseline-1", "created_at": datetime.now(UTC).isoformat(),
        "run_id": run_id, "python": platform.python_version(), "platform": platform.platform(),
        "source_sha256": code_digest(), "configuration_sha256": args.configuration_sha256,
        "fixture_sha256": hashlib.sha256(raw_fixture).hexdigest(), "concurrency": args.concurrency,
        "origin_sha256": hashlib.sha256(origin.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest(),
        "warmup": summarize(warmup), "steady": summarize(rows), "wall_seconds": wall_seconds,
        "throughput_requests_per_second": len(rows) / wall_seconds,
        "cold_start": "unavailable: service restart was not controlled",
        "server_cpu_rss": "unavailable: collect separately on the server",
        "queue_wait": "unavailable: no durable queue claim evidence in this probe",
        "provider_tokens_cost": "unavailable: no provider usage/price evidence collected",
        "first_token_latency": "unavailable: aggregated non-streaming API",
        "samples": rows,
    }
    args.output.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    with args.output.open("x", encoding="utf-8") as target:
        json.dump(report, target, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2, allow_nan=False)
    print(json.dumps({"output": str(args.output), "summary": report["steady"]}, ensure_ascii=False))
    return 0 if all(row["success"] and row["call_evidence"] for row in rows) else 1


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())
```

**解释：** 独立 worker 顺序使用独立 session，不在相同 session 上故意制造 409 来冒充吞吐。warmup 不计入稳态分位；样本的 elapsed 不包含随后的状态查询，但 wall_seconds 包含采证成本，吞吐字段应据此解读。指标缺失明确 unavailable，不写成 0。Token 只从本地环境读取，不进入参数、配置摘要或报告；配置 SHA 由操作者对已脱敏的固定配置快照计算，不建议对整个含密钥 `.env` 哈希。专用主体最多 200 个新操作，重复跑前需换专用主体或按后续清理流程处理配额，不盲删生产 key。

HTTP timeout 仍是客户端的网络阶段超时，不是 native 工作的硬终止；本脚本不能证明 Provider 被及时停止。它是第六批的**采集入口**，第九批才做服务器 CPU/RSS、真实故障和同环境热点优化对照，不预填提升百分比。

`degraded_rate` 仅表示响应 metadata 已声明的降级/阶段失败，不等于所有可选能力实际可用率；未标记的 Null RAG 等情况可能不在其中，报告固定标明 `metadata_reported_only_not_total_capability_availability`。前后对照必须另保存相同版本的 capability/readiness 快照，不能凭该值为 0 宣称完整能力健康。

### 29.2 新增 `tests/unit/test_execution_benchmark_contract.py`

文件功能：只测统计和目的地校验，不发任何 HTTP、不执行 benchmark main、不生成报告。

```python
from __future__ import annotations

import pytest

from scripts.benchmark_execution_v12 import checked_origin, percentile, summarize


def test_nearest_rank_percentiles_and_missing_data_are_not_zero():
    assert percentile([], 0.95) is None
    assert percentile([4, 1, 3, 2], 0.5) == 2
    assert percentile(list(range(1, 101)), 0.95) == 95
    assert summarize([])["error_rate"] is None


@pytest.mark.parametrize("origin", [
    "https://user:secret@example.com", "http://example.com", "https://example.com/path",
    "https://example.com?token=secret", "file:///private", "//example.com",
])
def test_unsafe_or_nonorigin_destinations_are_rejected(origin):
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        checked_origin(origin, allow_remote=False)


def test_loopback_and_explicit_https_remote_contrast():
    assert checked_origin("http://127.0.0.1:8000/", False) == "http://127.0.0.1:8000"
    assert checked_origin("https://example.com", True) == "https://example.com"
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        checked_origin("http://example.com", True)


def test_failures_degradation_and_call_evidence_are_separate():
    rows = [
        {"success": True, "elapsed_ms": 10, "degraded": False, "state": "completed",
         "calls": {"llm": 1}, "call_evidence": True},
        {"success": False, "elapsed_ms": 20, "degraded": True, "state": "unknown",
         "calls": {}, "call_evidence": False},
    ]
    summary = summarize(rows)
    assert summary["error_rate"] == summary["degraded_rate"] == 0.5
    assert summary["degradation_measurement"] == "metadata_reported_only_not_total_capability_availability"
    assert summary["rows_without_call_evidence"] == 1
    assert summary["stage_calls"] == {"llm": 1}
```
