## 30. 实际 MainRunner、官方 saver、Memory 与 Voice 的联合回归

### 30.1 新增 `tests/smoke/test_execution_joint_runtime.py`

文件功能：使用既有离线 Runtime fixture 构建真实 CoreRuntime/Dispatcher/MainRunner/LLMRunner 和官方本地 saver；只有 Mem0 物理层、Memory 策略的确定性提案和 ASR 文本采用替身。不是把主图换成一个 `lambda reply`，也不宣称覆盖真实 Provider 精度。正常用例与“图已经完成、提交前突然打断”的负例使用同一套主流程。

```python
from __future__ import annotations

import copy
from types import SimpleNamespace

import pytest
from langgraph.checkpoint.memory import InMemorySaver

from aiagent.memory.approved_write import approved_metadata, verify_approved
from aiagent.memory.memory_write_audit import MemoryWriteAuditLog
from aiagent.memory.published_memory import PublishedLongTermMemory
from aiagent.schemas.memory import MemoryCategory, MemoryImportance, MemoryWriteDecision
from aiagent.state.execution_context import bind, current
from aiagent.state.execution_ledger import ExecutionRejected, digest
from aiagent.state.voice_execution import control_call, get_call, start_call, voice_session
from apps.api.routes.voice_realtime import _run_turn
from apps.core.runtime_registry import get_runtime, reset_runtime


class PhysicalMemory:
    def __init__(self):
        self.rows = {}
        self.approved_texts = []

    def _stage_approved(self, payload, *, visibility_tag):
        verify_approved(payload)
        self.approved_texts.append(payload.text)
        physical_id = "joint-physical-" + str(len(self.approved_texts))
        self.rows[physical_id] = {
            "id": physical_id, "memory": payload.text,
            "user_id": payload.user_id, "agent_id": payload.agent_id,
            "metadata": {**approved_metadata(payload), "visibility_tag": visibility_tag},
        }
        return {"physical_id": physical_id}

    def _get_physical(self, physical_id):
        return copy.deepcopy(self.rows.get(physical_id))

    def _search_visible_physical(self, *, user_id, agent_id, query, tags, limit):
        return [dict(row, score=0.9) for row in self.rows.values()
                if row["user_id"] == user_id and row["agent_id"] == agent_id
                and row["metadata"]["visibility_tag"] in tags][:limit]


@pytest.fixture
def actual_runtime(offline_runtime_assets, monkeypatch, tmp_path):
    reset_runtime()
    runtime = get_runtime()
    ledger = runtime.dispatcher.execution_ledger
    physical = PhysicalMemory()
    facade = PublishedLongTermMemory(physical, ledger, available=True)
    runtime.long_term_memory = facade
    runtime.memory_runner.memory = facade
    runtime.memory_runner.async_write_enabled = False
    runtime.memory_runner.audit_log = MemoryWriteAuditLog(tmp_path / "joint-audit.jsonl")
    runtime.memory_runner.preference_store.set(user_id="joint-user", long_term_enabled=True)
    monkeypatch.setattr(runtime.memory_runner.policy_service, "decide_write", lambda **kwargs:
                        MemoryWriteDecision(should_store=True, category=MemoryCategory.PREFERENCE,
                                            importance=MemoryImportance.MEDIUM, confidence=0.99,
                                            facts=["用户喜欢无糖茶"], memory_hint="用户喜欢无糖茶"))
    monkeypatch.setattr(runtime.asr_listener, "asr_client", SimpleNamespace(
        transcribe=lambda path: "我喜欢无糖茶，请记住这个偏好",
    ))
    llm = runtime.agent_core.main_runner.llm_runner
    assert isinstance(llm.checkpointer, InMemorySaver), "this fixture must explicitly remain single-process local"
    assert runtime.memory_runner.memory.ledger is ledger is llm.execution_ledger
    try:
        yield SimpleNamespace(runtime=runtime, ledger=ledger, physical=physical,
                               facade=facade, llm=llm, tmp=tmp_path)
    finally:
        runtime.memory_runner.write_dispatcher.shutdown(wait=True)
        reset_runtime()


def accepted_voice(case):
    initial = start_call(case.ledger, user_id="joint-user", username="Joint", idempotency_key="start")
    cap = case.ledger.begin(user_id="joint-user", session_id=voice_session("joint-user", initial.call_id),
                            idempotency_key="turn", payload_digest=digest("synthetic audio"),
                            voice_call_id=initial.call_id, expected_voice_generation=0).capability
    case.ledger.start(cap)
    return initial, cap


def test_actual_runtime_voice_memory_checkpoint_share_one_successful_commit(actual_runtime, monkeypatch):
    case = actual_runtime
    monkeypatch.chdir(case.tmp)
    initial, cap = accepted_voice(case)
    with bind(case.ledger, cap) as frame:
        frame.persona_config = case.runtime.dispatcher.persona_manager.get_active_persona().config.model_dump(mode="json")
        body = _run_turn(case.runtime, initial.call_id, "joint-user", "Joint", b"synthetic", ".wav")
        assert body["reply"] and body["transcript"]
        assert frame.checkpoint.startswith("op12:")
        assert frame.staged_memories, "the real Memory prepare/guard path must have approved a fact"
        before = case.ledger.snapshot("joint-user")
        assert not before["memory"] and before["sessions"][cap.session_id]["checkpoint"] == ""
        assert not before["objects"]
        assert case.llm.graph.get_state({"configurable": {"thread_id": frame.checkpoint}}).values
        committed = case.ledger.finish(
            cap, result={"status_code": 200, "body": {"ok": True, **body}, "output_id": body["output_id"]},
            checkpoint=frame.checkpoint, history_pair=frame.history_pair,
            objects=frame.staged_objects, memories=frame.staged_memories, voice_result=frame.voice_result,
        )
    root = case.ledger.snapshot("joint-user")
    assert root["operations"][cap.operation_id]["status"] == "completed"
    assert root["sessions"][cap.session_id]["checkpoint"].startswith("op12:")
    assert len(root["memory"]) == 1 and len(root["sessions"][cap.session_id]["history"]) == 1
    assert case.ledger.visible_object("joint-user", "output", body["output_id"])
    assert committed["body"]["call"]["last_output_id"] == body["output_id"]
    assert get_call(case.ledger, "joint-user", initial.call_id).phase == "completed"
    assert case.physical.approved_texts == ["用户喜欢无糖茶"]
    assert case.facade.list_records("other-user") == []


def test_actual_completed_graph_loses_qualification_before_output_commit(actual_runtime, monkeypatch):
    case = actual_runtime
    monkeypatch.chdir(case.tmp)
    initial, cap = accepted_voice(case)
    graph = case.runtime.agent_core.main_runner.graph
    original_invoke = graph.invoke
    checkpoints = []

    def invoke_then_interrupt(*args, **kwargs):
        result = original_invoke(*args, **kwargs)
        frame = current(required=True)
        checkpoints.append(frame.checkpoint)
        assert frame.staged_memories
        control_call(case.ledger, user_id="joint-user", call_id=initial.call_id,
                     expected_generation=cap.generation, close=False)
        return result

    monkeypatch.setattr(graph, "invoke", invoke_then_interrupt)
    with bind(case.ledger, cap) as frame:
        frame.persona_config = case.runtime.dispatcher.persona_manager.get_active_persona().config.model_dump(mode="json")
        with pytest.raises(ExecutionRejected):
            _run_turn(case.runtime, initial.call_id, "joint-user", "Joint", b"synthetic", ".wav")
    root = case.ledger.snapshot("joint-user")
    assert checkpoints and case.llm.graph.get_state({"configurable": {"thread_id": checkpoints[0]}}).values
    assert case.physical.approved_texts == ["用户喜欢无糖茶"]
    assert not root["memory"] and not root["objects"]
    assert root["sessions"][cap.session_id]["checkpoint"] == ""
    assert root["sessions"][cap.session_id]["history"] == []
    assert root["operations"][cap.operation_id]["status"] == "cancelled"
    assert "result" not in root["operations"][cap.operation_id]
    assert get_call(case.ledger, "joint-user", initial.call_id).phase == "interrupted"
```

**解释：** 第二个用例故意不删除已写入的物理 checkpoint/Mem0 替身数据，而是证明它们没有当前可见引用。真实副作用无法跨系统回滚，声明“物理记录存在但不可见、待清理”比断言“取消后什么都没发生”更准确。测试使用官方 `InMemorySaver` 是显式离线开发合同，不是生产 Redis 的替代；真实 RedisSaver 另列门禁。Memory 同步路径参与本用例；后台竞争由第 21/22 节负责，不把同步成功当成线程池恢复证据。

本段指令仅提供，未执行：

```powershell
.\.venv\Scripts\python.exe -m pytest tests/smoke/test_execution_joint_runtime.py tests/smoke/test_mock_runtime_smoke.py -q
```

## 31. 真实 Graph 评测入口：使用 Runtime 自己的账本

### 31.1 `aiagent/evaluation/adapters.py`

完整替换 `graph_observation()`。这仍是逐节点流式观察主图，不改为假回复；refusal 分支不调用外部组件，runtime 分支必须显式允许隔离评测，使用独立 `eval-` 主体和随机会话。正常返回前提交，异常撤销；清理只撤本次会话，不删除整个人的长期记忆。此处暂时关闭本次 Runtime 的异步记忆写入，让评测结束时的引用状态确定；后台交错仍由专门测试覆盖。

```python
def graph_observation(params: GraphInput, root: Path, user: str) -> Observation:
    import hashlib
    import secrets
    from contextlib import ExitStack

    from aiagent.evaluation.http_support import asset, require_isolated
    from aiagent.graphs.main_graph import MainRunner
    from aiagent.persona.persona_models import PersonaConfig
    from aiagent.persona.persona_runtime import PersonaRuntime
    from aiagent.schemas.inputs import InputAttachment, InputEvent, InputSource
    from aiagent.state.execution_context import bind, current
    from aiagent.state.execution_ledger import digest as execution_digest

    if current() is not None:
        raise ValueError("graph_evaluation_must_not_nest_execution")
    runtime = None
    ledger = None
    cap = None
    with ExitStack() as stack:
        try:
            if params.driver == "refusal":
                dependencies = {name: Mock(name=name) for name in (
                    "state_runner", "planner_runner", "rag_runner", "llm_runner", "memory_runner", "vision_runner",
                )}
                runner = MainRunner(**dependencies)
                session_id = "eval-refusal:" + secrets.token_hex(16)
                turn_id = "evaluation"
                scope = "offline_refusal_graph"
                frame = None
            else:
                require_isolated()
                if not user.startswith("eval-") or len(user) > 128:
                    raise ValueError("graph_evaluation_requires_dedicated_eval_subject")
                from apps.core.bootstrap import build_runtime

                runtime = build_runtime()
                runtime.memory_runner.async_write_enabled = False
                runner = runtime.agent_core.main_runner
                ledger = runtime.dispatcher.execution_ledger
                session_id = "eval-graph:" + secrets.token_hex(16)
                cap = ledger.begin(
                    user_id=user, session_id=session_id, idempotency_key=secrets.token_hex(16),
                    payload_digest=execution_digest(params.model_dump(mode="json")),
                    persona_digest=execution_digest(params.persona), policy_version="v12-06-eval-graph",
                ).capability
                ledger.start(cap)
                frame = stack.enter_context(bind(ledger, cap))
                frame.persona_config = params.persona
                turn_id = f"{cap.operation_id}:{cap.generation}"
                scope = "isolated_graph_configured_providers"
                dependencies = {}
            attachments = []
            if params.image:
                path = asset(root, params.image)
                if path.stat().st_size > 20 * 1024 * 1024:
                    raise ValueError("graph_image_too_large")
                if hashlib.sha256(path.read_bytes()).hexdigest() != params.image_sha256:
                    raise ValueError("graph_image_hash_mismatch")
                attachments.append(InputAttachment(type="image", path=str(path)))
            event = InputEvent(source=InputSource.CHAT, user_id=user, session_id=session_id,
                               turn_id=turn_id, text=params.text, attachments=attachments)
            state = {"input_event": event,
                     "persona_runtime": PersonaRuntime(PersonaConfig.model_validate(params.persona)),
                     "history": [], "session_id": session_id, "turn_id": turn_id}
            events, metadata, merged = [], {}, {}
            packet = None
            for update in runner.graph.stream(state, stream_mode="updates"):
                if frame is not None:
                    frame.remaining()
                for node, patch in update.items():
                    if not isinstance(patch, dict):
                        continue
                    events.append(node)
                    merged.update(patch)
                    metadata.update(patch.get("metadata", {}))
                    if patch.get("response_packet") is not None:
                        packet = patch["response_packet"]
                        metadata.update(packet.metadata)
            if any(dependency.mock_calls for dependency in dependencies.values()):
                raise AssertionError("offline_refusal_entered_downstream_component")
            if packet is None:
                raise RuntimeError("graph_evaluation_response_packet_missing")
            if frame is not None:
                frame.remaining()
                ledger.finish(
                    cap, result={"status": "evaluation_executed"}, checkpoint=frame.checkpoint,
                    history_pair=(params.text, packet.reply_text),
                    objects=frame.staged_objects, memories=frame.staged_memories,
                )
                metadata["evaluation_operation_id"] = cap.operation_id
                metadata["evaluation_external_calls"] = str(ledger.operation(user, cap.operation_id)["calls"])
            evidence = {}
            rag = merged.get("rag_result")
            if rag is not None:
                context = "\n".join(getattr(rag, "context", []))
                if context:
                    evidence["rag_context"] = str(context)
            return Observation(
                scope=scope, answer=packet.reply_text, events=events, evidence=evidence,
                data={"metadata": metadata, "memory_prompt_context": merged.get("memory_prompt_context", ""),
                      "vision_context": merged.get("vision_context", "")},
            )
        except BaseException:
            if ledger is not None and cap is not None:
                try:
                    ledger.terminal(cap, "unknown")
                except Exception:
                    pass
            raise
        finally:
            if runtime is not None:
                try:
                    runtime.memory_runner.write_dispatcher.shutdown(wait=True)
                finally:
                    try:
                        if ledger is not None and cap is not None:
                            ledger.cancel_session(user, cap.session_id, close=True, clear=True)
                    finally:
                        client = getattr(runner.llm_runner.checkpointer, "_redis", None)
                        if client is not None:
                            client.close()
```

解释：`ExitStack` 在异常处理及清理之后才解除 frame，避免后台调用偷偷获得一个无资格空上下文。`evaluation_executed` 只说明流程执行，评分仍由第四批的 scorer 判定。关闭评测会话不会物理删除已经提交的隔离评测记忆，专用评测环境须按第九批生命周期清理；不要对真实用户运行此评测。

### 31.2 新增 `tests/smoke/test_execution_graph_evaluation.py`

文件功能：回归真实 Graph 评测入口，证明 runtime 分支不再出现 checkpoint 作用域不匹配；refusal 分支仍保留原有“不应进入下游”的对照。

```python
from __future__ import annotations

import pytest

from aiagent.evaluation.adapters import GraphInput, graph_observation
from aiagent.state.execution_context import current
from apps.core.runtime_registry import reset_runtime


def test_graph_runtime_evaluation_uses_actual_ledger(offline_runtime_assets, monkeypatch, tmp_path):
    import aiagent.evaluation.http_support as support
    from apps.core.bootstrap import build_runtime

    monkeypatch.setattr(support, "require_isolated", lambda: None)
    runtime = build_runtime()
    # Only inject the factory, not MainRunner, its graph, or its official saver.
    import apps.core.bootstrap as bootstrap
    monkeypatch.setattr(bootstrap, "build_runtime", lambda: runtime)
    persona = runtime.dispatcher.persona_manager.get_active_persona().config.model_dump(mode="json")
    try:
        result = graph_observation(GraphInput(persona=persona, text="你好", driver="runtime"),
                                   tmp_path, "eval-batch06-graph")
        assert result.answer and result.events
        operation_id = result.data["metadata"]["evaluation_operation_id"]
        root = runtime.dispatcher.execution_ledger.snapshot("eval-batch06-graph")
        # Cleanup revokes the session even though the graph executed successfully.
        record = root["operations"][operation_id]
        assert root["sessions"][record["session_id"]]["closed"] is True
        assert current() is None
    finally:
        reset_runtime()


def test_graph_evaluation_rejects_nondedicated_user(monkeypatch, tmp_path):
    import aiagent.evaluation.http_support as support
    monkeypatch.setattr(support, "require_isolated", lambda: None)
    # This check runs before constructing a runtime or contacting providers.
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="dedicated_eval_subject"):
        graph_observation(GraphInput(persona={}, text="hello", driver="runtime"), tmp_path, "real-user")
    assert current() is None
```

## 32. 索引维护边界：启动只读，有界离线构建，不让聊天触发全库重嵌入

本批不建立在线发布队列。生产 Runtime 只加载运维批准的不可变索引制品；构建使用显式命令、单独资格、最多 120 秒/2 次 rag_embedding 请求，并输出可审阅摘要。超出规模先失败，不循环新开资格把大任务伪装成很多聊天。第九批可复用现有 CloudTaskQueue 加入分块恢复及共享发布指针，此前旧在线重建入口明确 503，不能悄悄走无预算线程。

这是维护可用性的明确收紧，不是“自动重建已经完成”。已有线上重建需求必须审阅这个运行模式变化；不接受此限制时，不应部署第六批的一半。

### 32.1 `aiagent/knowledge/rag_pipeline.py` 精确修改

在 `__init__` 签名最后增加 `read_only: bool = False`，赋值区增加 `self.read_only = read_only`。默认 False 仅保留显式离线评测/构建兼容；Runtime 工厂总传 True。修改如下三个位置，保留其余检索、置信度、新鲜度算法。

`build_index()` 在任何状态更新之前插入：

```python
        if self.read_only and (force_rebuild or not self.docs_index_path.is_file() or not self.faiss_dir.is_dir()):
            raise RuntimeError("rag_read_only_artifact_required")
```

`rebuild_async()` 方法首行插入：

```python
        if self.read_only:
            raise RuntimeError("rag_online_rebuild_disabled")
```

`ensure_ready()` 最后原 `self.build_index(force_rebuild=True)` 前插入：

```python
        if self.read_only:
            raise RuntimeError("rag_read_only_artifact_missing")
```

同时在 `_warn_if_stale()` 中把 `if self.auto_rebuild_on_stale:` 改为 `if self.auto_rebuild_on_stale and not self.read_only:`。没有缓存时允许 RAG 明确降级 Null，不能偷偷下载模型或全库重建。原 `build_index` 的异常已重新抛出，不要再加一层吞掉资格错误的 fallback。`RAGRunner.run()` 的既有异常降级必须保留共享错误重抛，`MainRunner` 同样已有 helper，禁止删除。

### 32.2 新增 `aiagent/knowledge/runtime_factory.py`

文件功能：抽出 Bootstrap 已有构造参数，构建和加载共用一个受控工厂；不另建检索算法。制品目录只来自运维配置/CLI，不来自用户 HTTP。`artifact.json` 的 SHA-256 必须由部署配置固定，先验全部文件摘要再反序列化 FAISS。SHA-256 是完整性校验，不是签名，也不是对不可信 pickle 的安全转换；只有受信任构建机产物可以部署。

```python
from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import json
from pathlib import Path

from aiagent.knowledge.document_loader import DocumentLoader
from aiagent.knowledge.rag_pipeline import RAGPipeline
from aiagent.knowledge.reranker import SimpleReranker
from aiagent.knowledge.retriever import HybridRetriever
from aiagent.knowledge.vector_store import LangChainVectorStore
from config.env_secrets import resolve_named_secret

ARTIFACT_FILES = ("split_docs.json", "index-manifest.json", "faiss/index.faiss", "faiss/index.pkl")


def sha256_file(path: Path) -> str:
    if path.is_symlink() or not path.is_file() or path.stat().st_size > 64 * 1024 * 1024:
        raise ValueError("rag_artifact_file_invalid")
    with path.open("rb") as stream:
        return hashlib.file_digest(stream, "sha256").hexdigest()


def verify_artifact(directory: Path, expected_sha256: str) -> dict:
    if directory.is_symlink() or not directory.is_dir() or not expected_sha256:
        raise ValueError("rag_approved_artifact_required")
    manifest = directory / "artifact.json"
    if manifest.stat().st_size > 16 * 1024 or sha256_file(manifest) != expected_sha256:
        raise ValueError("rag_artifact_digest_mismatch")
    data = json.loads(manifest.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    if (not isinstance(data, dict) or set(data) != {"schema", "files"} or data["schema"] != 1
            or not isinstance(data["files"], dict) or set(data["files"]) != set(ARTIFACT_FILES)):
        raise ValueError("rag_artifact_manifest_invalid")
    for name, expected in data["files"].items():
        target = directory / name
        if not target.resolve().is_relative_to(directory.resolve()) or sha256_file(target) != expected:
            raise ValueError("rag_artifact_file_digest_mismatch")
    return data


def create_pipeline(config, *, artifact_dir: Path, knowledge_dir: Path, read_only: bool) -> RAGPipeline:
    provider = config.rag_embedding_provider.strip().lower()
    if provider in {"huggingface", "local"} and (
        not config.rag_embedding_local_files_only or not config.rag_embedding_model_path
    ):
        raise ValueError("rag_local_model_must_be_preprovisioned")
    api_key = resolve_named_secret(config.rag_embedding_api_key_env) if config.rag_embedding_api_key_env else None
    base_url = config.rag_embedding_base_url or (
        config.siliconflow_base_url if provider == "siliconflow" else None
    )
    return RAGPipeline(
        loader=DocumentLoader(),
        retriever=HybridRetriever(bm25_top_k=config.rag_bm25_top_k, vector_top_k=config.rag_vector_top_k),
        vector_store=LangChainVectorStore(
            embedding_model_name=config.rag_embedding_model_name,
            embedding_model_path=config.rag_embedding_model_path, device=config.rag_embedding_device,
            local_files_only=True, embedding_provider=provider, embedding_api_key=api_key,
            embedding_base_url=base_url, embedding_batch_size=min(config.rag_embedding_batch_size, 64),
            embedding_dimensions=config.rag_embedding_dimensions,
        ),
        reranker=SimpleReranker(), knowledge_dir=knowledge_dir,
        docs_index_path=artifact_dir / "split_docs.json", faiss_dir=artifact_dir / "faiss",
        manifest_path=artifact_dir / "index-manifest.json",
        chunk_size=config.rag_chunk_size, chunk_overlap=config.rag_chunk_overlap,
        final_top_k=config.rag_final_top_k, auto_rebuild_on_stale=False, read_only=read_only,
    )
```

`config/env_secrets.py` 当前 `resolve_named_secret(name: str)` 按变量名读取凭据，空名称在工厂中直接返回 None；不要把 Provider key 的值写进 artifact/config digest。必须把制品挂载为只读并由独立部署账号更新，进程内哈希检查不能防住拥有目录写权限的攻击者在检查后替换 pickle。四个文件覆盖实际被加载的 FAISS 和 split-docs；新增制品文件需同步白名单，不接受用户提供任意 pickle。

### 32.3 `config/settings.py`、`.env.example` 和 Bootstrap

`Settings` 在现有 RAG 字段附近新增：

```python
    rag_artifact_dir: str = Field(default="", alias="RAG_ARTIFACT_DIR")
    rag_artifact_sha256: str = Field(default="", alias="RAG_ARTIFACT_SHA256")
```

统一 `.env.example` 的 RAG 段增加以下行，真实 `.env` 的本机/服务器路径分别由部署填写，**其余 Redis/身份/CORS 仍共用之前的统一配置体系**：

```dotenv
# 运维批准的只读索引目录。空值意味着 RAG 不可用，不在启动期间重建。
RAG_ARTIFACT_DIR=
# 构建命令成功提交后输出的 artifact.json SHA-256；不是目录名或 Provider key。
RAG_ARTIFACT_SHA256=
RAG_AUTO_REBUILD_ON_STALE=false
```

Bootstrap 在原 RAG try 内，仅用下面代码替换从 `rag_pipeline = RAGPipeline(...)` 到 `rag_pipeline.build_index(...)`，保留既有 capability available/degraded 分支；删除因工厂提取而未再使用的 DocumentLoader/HybridRetriever/LangChainVectorStore/SimpleReranker/RAGPipeline 导入。

```python
        from pathlib import Path

        from aiagent.knowledge.runtime_factory import create_pipeline, verify_artifact
        from config.paths import KNOWLEDGE_PUBLIC_DIR

        artifact_dir = Path(settings.rag_artifact_dir).resolve() if settings.rag_artifact_dir else None
        if artifact_dir is None:
            raise RuntimeError("rag_approved_artifact_required")
        verify_artifact(artifact_dir, settings.rag_artifact_sha256)
        rag_pipeline = create_pipeline(
            settings, artifact_dir=artifact_dir, knowledge_dir=KNOWLEDGE_PUBLIC_DIR, read_only=True,
        )
        rag_pipeline.build_index(force_rebuild=False)
```

RAG except 不把异常原文放进 Null reason 或日志：原 `logger.exception(...)` 改为 `logger.warning("rag_initialization_unavailable error_type=%s", type(exc).__name__)`，`NullRAGPipeline(reason=str(exc))` 改为 `NullRAGPipeline(reason="rag_initialization_unavailable")`。capability 的 `error=exc` 改为 `error=RuntimeError("rag_initialization_unavailable")`，防止内含路径/凭据被健康接口回显。

### 32.4 关闭旧在线写入口，不让已有任务自动重放

`apps/api/routes/knowledge.py` 导入 `HTTPException`，保留 admin dependency；完整替换 `knowledge_rebuild()`：

```python
@router.post("/knowledge/rebuild")
async def knowledge_rebuild(req: KnowledgeRebuildRequest, _: None = Depends(require_cloud_admin)):
    raise HTTPException(status_code=503, detail={
        "code": "knowledge_online_rebuild_disabled",
        "automatic_retry_allowed": False,
    })
```

删除此文件不再使用的 `get_request_id`、`cloud_settings`、`CloudTaskQueue` 导入和 `task_queue = ...`。查询 routes 按第 6.1 节使用 ExecutionRoute。`CoreRuntime.rebuild_knowledge_index` 和 `rebuild_knowledge_index_async` 两方法也完整替换，防止直接调用绕过 HTTP：

```python
    def rebuild_knowledge_index(self, force_rebuild: bool = True) -> dict:
        raise RuntimeError("knowledge_online_rebuild_disabled")

    def rebuild_knowledge_index_async(self, force_rebuild: bool = True) -> dict:
        raise RuntimeError("knowledge_online_rebuild_disabled")
```

`apps/worker/main.py` 的 `_run_task()` 在读取 `task_type` 后、`await queue.start(...)` 前插入：

```python
    if task_type == "knowledge.rebuild":
        await queue.dead_letter(
            task_id, "knowledge_online_rebuild_disabled", reason="maintenance_requires_reviewed_offline_build",
        )
        return
```

同时把 `_handle_knowledge_rebuild()` 全文替换为下段，删除 `_run_task` 中不可达的 knowledge 分支并让 vision 分支成为 `if`。保留现有队列，不新建第二个任务系统，也不把被阻止的任务标记 done。

```python
async def _handle_knowledge_rebuild(payload: dict[str, Any]) -> dict:
    raise RuntimeError("knowledge_online_rebuild_disabled")
```

### 32.5 新增 `scripts/build_rag_artifact_v12.py`

文件功能：受信运维从现有知识目录构建新目录，绝不覆盖运行目录。构建资格默认使用配置中的 Redis；本地只能显式 local 单进程。限制 64 个文本文件、总 64 KiB、最多 64 个 chunk，每批输入最多 64 KiB；超过限制停止，不能未经审阅放大预算。PDF/JSON 的解析成本及任意符号链接不在本维护入口开放，仍可先在隔离环境生成受审阅文本。

```python
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path
from tempfile import TemporaryDirectory

from aiagent.knowledge.document_loader import DocumentLoader
from aiagent.knowledge.runtime_factory import ARTIFACT_FILES, create_pipeline, sha256_file
from aiagent.state.execution_context import bind
from aiagent.state.execution_ledger import ExecutionLedger, LocalAtomic, RedisAtomic, canonical, digest
from cloud.config import cloud_settings
from config.settings import settings


def configured_ledger() -> ExecutionLedger:
    if cloud_settings.execution_state_mode == "local":
        return ExecutionLedger(LocalAtomic(), prefix=cloud_settings.redis_prefix)
    if cloud_settings.execution_state_mode != "redis" or not cloud_settings.redis_url:
        raise ValueError("maintenance_shared_redis_required")
    from redis import Redis
    client = Redis.from_url(cloud_settings.redis_url, decode_responses=True,
                            socket_timeout=5, socket_connect_timeout=5)
    return ExecutionLedger(RedisAtomic(client), prefix=cloud_settings.redis_prefix)


class BoundedLoader(DocumentLoader):
    def split_documents(self, *args, **kwargs):
        result = super().split_documents(*args, **kwargs)
        if not result or len(result) > 64 or sum(len(row.page_content.encode("utf-8")) for row in result) > 64 * 1024:
            raise ValueError("maintenance_chunk_budget_exceeded")
        return result


def snapshot_sources(source: Path, destination: Path) -> dict[str, str]:
    if source.is_symlink() or not source.is_dir():
        raise ValueError("maintenance_source_invalid")
    files, total = {}, 0
    for path in sorted(source.rglob("*")):
        if path.is_symlink():
            raise ValueError("maintenance_symlink_rejected")
        if not path.is_file():
            continue
        if path.suffix.lower() not in {".txt", ".md", ".markdown"}:
            raise ValueError("maintenance_text_sources_only")
        if len(files) >= 64 or path.stat().st_size > 64 * 1024:
            raise ValueError("maintenance_source_budget_exceeded")
        raw = path.read_bytes()
        total += len(raw)
        if total > 64 * 1024:
            raise ValueError("maintenance_source_budget_exceeded")
        raw.decode("utf-8", errors="strict")
        relative = path.relative_to(source)
        target = destination / relative
        target.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        target.write_bytes(raw)
        files[relative.as_posix()] = sha256_file(target)
    if not files:
        raise ValueError("maintenance_sources_empty")
    return files


def build(source: Path, destination: Path, ledger: ExecutionLedger) -> dict:
    source, destination = source.resolve(), destination.resolve()
    if destination.exists() or destination.is_relative_to(source) or source.is_relative_to(destination):
        raise ValueError("maintenance_requires_new_separate_destination")
    destination.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    with TemporaryDirectory(prefix="rag-v12-", dir=destination.parent) as temporary:
        work = Path(temporary)
        inputs, outputs = work / "inputs", work / "artifact"
        inputs.mkdir()
        outputs.mkdir()
        sources = snapshot_sources(source, inputs)
        identity = {
            "sources": sources, "model": settings.rag_embedding_model_name,
            "provider": settings.rag_embedding_provider, "dims": settings.rag_embedding_dimensions,
            "chunk_size": settings.rag_chunk_size, "chunk_overlap": settings.rag_chunk_overlap,
        }
        admission = ledger.begin(
            user_id="maintenance-rag-v12", session_id="build", idempotency_key=digest(identity),
            payload_digest=digest(identity), policy_version="v12-06-maintenance",
        )
        cap = admission.capability
        if not admission.created:
            raise ValueError("maintenance_existing_operation_review_required")
        ledger.start(cap)
        moved = False
        try:
            with bind(ledger, cap) as frame:
                pipeline = create_pipeline(settings, artifact_dir=outputs, knowledge_dir=inputs, read_only=False)
                pipeline.loader = BoundedLoader()
                pipeline.build_index(force_rebuild=True)
                frame.remaining()
                data = {"schema": 1, "files": {name: sha256_file(outputs / name) for name in ARTIFACT_FILES}}
                (outputs / "artifact.json").write_text(canonical(data), encoding="utf-8")
                artifact_digest = sha256_file(outputs / "artifact.json")
                # A directory rename is not a distributed commit; only the ledger result authorizes review.
                os.rename(outputs, destination)
                moved = True
                ledger.finish(cap, result={"status": "artifact_built", "artifact_sha256": artifact_digest})
                return {"ok": True, "operation_id": cap.operation_id,
                        "artifact_sha256": artifact_digest, "activated": False}
        except BaseException:
            try:
                ledger.terminal(cap, "unknown")
            except Exception:
                pass
            # Keep any moved directory quarantined; never print its digest as an approved success.
            if moved:
                print(json.dumps({"ok": False, "code": "artifact_quarantined", "operation_id": cap.operation_id}))
            raise


def main() -> None:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    parser.add_argument("--source", type=Path, required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--destination", type=Path, required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--execute", action="store_true")
    args = parser.parse_args()
    if not args.execute:
        parser.error("explicit --execute required; this command can call a paid embedding provider")
    ledger = configured_ledger()
    try:
        print(json.dumps(build(args.source, args.destination, ledger), ensure_ascii=False))
    finally:
        if isinstance(ledger.atomic, RedisAtomic):
            ledger.atomic.redis.close()


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

**解释与限制：** 输出成功后运维审阅制品及 operation，再把目录和输出摘要同时写入同一版部署配置，API/Worker 一起滚动重启；不在线修改文件、不声称所有进程自动热更新。失败后可能留下隔离目录，这是不可见的运维产物，不是可使用索引。暂存源的绝对路径会进入索引新鲜度元数据；加载后可能报告 stale，属于诊断，不触发重建。需要对真实部署源路径做精确新鲜度比较时，第九批补独立 source manifest 映射，不擅改原来源引用算法。本命令只有运维可信路径，不对任意 HTTP 开放。

## 33. Mem0 的离线依赖预检：不在真实请求里安装模型

### 33.1 新增 `aiagent/memory/offline_dependencies.py`

文件功能：在创建物理 Memory 前拒绝未适配的 embedder 和缺失的词法模型。当前本机读到 `mem0ai=2.0.1`、`langgraph=1.1.10`、`langgraph-checkpoint-redis=0.5.2`，这是本次只读包元数据，不是建议升级或已通过兼容测试。当前未安装 spaCy/model，本批不会替你安装；未补依赖时 Memory 明确 unavailable，不能把 Null 当正常长期记忆。

```python
from __future__ import annotations

from importlib.metadata import PackageNotFoundError, version


def require_memory_dependencies(embedder_provider: str) -> None:
    if embedder_provider.strip().lower() not in {"openai", "siliconflow"}:
        raise RuntimeError("memory_embedder_budget_adapter_required")
    try:
        version("spacy")
        version("en-core-web-sm")
    except PackageNotFoundError:
        raise RuntimeError("memory_offline_nlp_assets_missing") from None
    import spacy

    if not spacy.util.is_package("en_core_web_sm"):
        raise RuntimeError("memory_offline_nlp_assets_invalid")
    # Only after the installed-package check may SDK loaders run. No download path is taken.
    from mem0.utils.spacy_models import get_nlp_full, get_nlp_lemma

    if get_nlp_full() is None or get_nlp_lemma() is None:
        raise RuntimeError("memory_offline_nlp_assets_unavailable")
```

Bootstrap 的 Memory try 内，在 `Mem0LongTermMemory(...)` **之前**增加：

```python
        from aiagent.memory.offline_dependencies import require_memory_dependencies

        require_memory_dependencies(settings.memory_embedder_provider)
```

`aiagent/memory/mem0_memory.py` 在 `import os` 之后、`from mem0 import Memory` 之前插入以下代码；把所有 Mem0 导入保留在这之后。当前 pyproject 已忽略 E402，无须给全项目新增忽略规则。

```python
# These are deployment policy, not user/request options; set before importing SDK telemetry.
os.environ["MEM0_TELEMETRY"] = "false"
os.environ["ANONYMIZED_TELEMETRY"] = "false"
```

统一 `.env.example` 同时列出 `MEM0_TELEMETRY=false`、`ANONYMIZED_TELEMETRY=false`、`HF_HUB_OFFLINE=1`、`TRANSFORMERS_OFFLINE=1`。API/Worker 的启动环境也设置这些值，确保其它模块先导入 SDK 时仍不启动遥测；不能只依靠 dotenv 在 import 之后设置。环境变量不是网络沙箱，生产另通过网络策略只放行业务 Provider/Redis/Qdrant，拒绝模型下载域名。

安装方案只提供、不执行：受信构建机预先获取并审阅匹配的 spaCy/model wheel，随后使用本项目既有 uv 锁定机制。示例为 spaCy 3.8 系列与 en_core_web_sm 3.8.0，不擅自改现有锁。离线模型 wheel 应先经过来源、摘要与许可检查，不使用对话中的未知下载链接。

```powershell
# 仅在已准备受信模型 wheel 后执行；路径改成实际的受信 wheel。
uv add "spacy>=3.8,<3.9" "F:/approved-wheels/en_core_web_sm-3.8.0-py3-none-any.whl"
uv sync --locked
# 容器也必须在构建期同步新的 pyproject/uv.lock 并安装模型；不得运行期 pip 安装。
```

如果锁文件记录本机绝对 wheel 路径，Linux 构建不能直接使用；应把经过批准的 wheel 放入仓库约定的制品源，或使用组织内可访问的固定 wheel URL，再锁定。不能把 F 盘路径原样放进 Dockerfile。本批不要求 API 启动拉取 CUDA 或 fastembed；未适配 provider 在创建 embedder 之前拒绝，避免“初始化后才发现不支持、此前已经下载模型”。

### 33.2 新增 `tests/unit/test_memory_offline_dependencies.py`

```python
from __future__ import annotations

from importlib.metadata import PackageNotFoundError

import pytest

from aiagent.memory import offline_dependencies as module


def test_unsupported_embedder_rejected_before_dependency_lookup(monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.setattr(module, "version", lambda name: pytest.fail("must reject before initialization"))
    with pytest.raises(RuntimeError, match="budget_adapter_required"):
        module.require_memory_dependencies("fastembed")


def test_missing_model_rejected_without_sdk_loader(monkeypatch):
    def missing(name):
        raise PackageNotFoundError(name)
    monkeypatch.setattr(module, "version", missing)
    with pytest.raises(RuntimeError, match="assets_missing"):
        module.require_memory_dependencies("openai")


def test_installed_assets_load_without_downloader(monkeypatch):
    import sys
    from types import SimpleNamespace

    monkeypatch.setattr(module, "version", lambda name: "test")
    fake_spacy = SimpleNamespace(util=SimpleNamespace(is_package=lambda name: name == "en_core_web_sm"))
    monkeypatch.setitem(sys.modules, "spacy", fake_spacy)
    monkeypatch.setitem(sys.modules, "mem0.utils.spacy_models", SimpleNamespace(
        get_nlp_full=lambda: object(), get_nlp_lemma=lambda: object(),
    ))
    module.require_memory_dependencies("siliconflow")
```

解释：第三个用例验证本项目预检分支，不证明真实 spaCy 模型质量。真实容器还需在网络出口受限的环境做初始化与首个请求探针，列入最终门禁；只做包存在检查不能代表词法模型能正常加载。

## 34. 旧 Memory 的受控重审，不认领旧向量

旧记录可能包含原始问答、未批准 metadata 或遗留实体边，本批默认不展示。安全迁移不是给全部旧记录补一个 tag；运维在停写、私有备份后，按主体导出并人工重审文本，只把批准后的新事实提交到当前 facade。旧物理记录仍作为隔离数据等待第九批清理，不修改它的 owner/正文来伪造历史审批。

`aiagent/memory/approved_write.py` 的第五批白名单 `_SOURCES` 精确替换为下行，仅增加受控导入来源；不放开任意来源，也不修改签名/正文校验：

```python
_SOURCES = {"chat_turn", "user_edit", "user_merge", "manual_review_import"}
```

### 34.1 新增 `aiagent/memory/reviewed_import.py`

文件功能：处理一份最多 32 条的人工重审清单，每条独立原子提交。稳定 `review_id` 与清单摘要绑定，原始记录摘要防止重审后源被改。需要用户先显式启用长期记忆；导入不会替用户启用。幂等缓存已被后续禁用/删除撤销时，返回 conflict 而非自动复活。

```python
from __future__ import annotations

import json
from pathlib import Path

from aiagent.memory.published_memory import text_digest
from aiagent.schemas.memory import MemoryCategory, MemoryImportance
from aiagent.state.execution_context import bind, current
from aiagent.state.execution_ledger import ExecutionRejected, checked_name, digest


def _pairs(items):
    result = {}
    for key, value in items:
        if key in result:
            raise ValueError("memory_review_duplicate_json_key")
        result[key] = value
    return result


def load_review(path: Path) -> dict:
    if path.is_symlink() or not path.is_file() or path.stat().st_size > 128 * 1024:
        raise ValueError("memory_review_file_invalid")
    data = json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8-sig"), object_pairs_hook=_pairs)
    if (not isinstance(data, dict) or set(data) != {"schema", "review_id", "user_id", "agent_id", "items"}
            or type(data["schema"]) is not int or data["schema"] != 1):
        raise ValueError("memory_review_schema_invalid")
    for key in ("review_id", "user_id", "agent_id"):
        checked_name(data[key])
    if not isinstance(data["items"], list) or not 1 <= len(data["items"]) <= 32:
        raise ValueError("memory_review_items_invalid")
    seen = set()
    for item in data["items"]:
        if not isinstance(item, dict) or set(item) != {"old_id", "old_text_sha256", "approved_text", "category", "importance"}:
            raise ValueError("memory_review_item_invalid")
        checked_name(item["old_id"])
        if item["old_id"] in seen:
            raise ValueError("memory_review_duplicate_source")
        seen.add(item["old_id"])
        if (not isinstance(item["old_text_sha256"], str) or len(item["old_text_sha256"]) != 64
                or any(c not in "0123456789abcdef" for c in item["old_text_sha256"])
                or not isinstance(item["approved_text"], str) or not 1 <= len(item["approved_text"].strip()) <= 2000):
            raise ValueError("memory_review_text_invalid")
        MemoryCategory(item["category"])
        MemoryImportance(item["importance"])
    return data


def import_review(facade, data: dict, *, execute: bool) -> dict:
    if current() is not None:
        raise ExecutionRejected("memory_review_must_be_control_entry")
    user, agent = data["user_id"], data["agent_id"]
    if not facade.available or not facade.ledger.memory_view(user, agent)["enabled"]:
        raise ExecutionRejected("memory_review_requires_available_enabled_memory")
    completed, reused = 0, 0
    for index, item in enumerate(data["items"]):
        raw = facade._physical._get_physical(item["old_id"])
        if not isinstance(raw, dict) or not isinstance(raw.get("memory"), str):
            raise ValueError("memory_review_source_missing")
        metadata = raw.get("metadata") or {}
        for key, expected in (("user_id", user), ("agent_id", agent)):
            candidates = [value for value in (raw.get(key), metadata.get(key)) if value is not None]
            if not candidates or any(value != expected for value in candidates):
                raise ValueError("memory_review_source_owner_mismatch")
        if text_digest(raw["memory"]) != item["old_text_sha256"]:
            raise ValueError("memory_review_source_changed")
        payload = facade._edit_payload(
            item["approved_text"].strip(), MemoryCategory(item["category"]),
            MemoryImportance(item["importance"]), "manual_review_import", user, agent,
        )
        if not execute:
            continue
        admission = facade.ledger.begin(
            user_id=user, session_id="memory-reviewed-import",
            idempotency_key="review:" + digest([data["review_id"], index]),
            payload_digest=digest([data, index]), policy_version="v12-06-memory-reviewed-import",
        )
        if not admission.created:
            if admission.status == "completed" and admission.result is not None:
                reused += 1
                continue
            raise ExecutionRejected("memory_review_existing_operation_requires_review")
        cap = admission.capability
        facade.ledger.start(cap)
        try:
            with bind(facade.ledger, cap) as frame:
                result = facade.add_approved(payload)
                if not result.get("ok") or result.get("status") != "staged":
                    raise RuntimeError("memory_review_not_staged")
                facade.ledger.finish(cap, result={"status": "review_imported"}, memories=frame.staged_memories)
                completed += 1
        except BaseException:
            try:
                facade.ledger.terminal(cap, "unknown")
            except Exception:
                pass
            raise
    return {"ok": True, "dry_run": not execute, "reviewed_count": len(data["items"]),
            "imported_count": completed, "reused_count": reused, "old_physical_deleted": False}
```

解释：dry-run 会读取原记录并重新过滤批准文本，但不调用 embedding、不创建操作；执行前暂停当前主体业务写入。一次清单不是跨 32 条记录的事务，中途出错，之前成功项保留，可用同清单幂等续跑。清单含敏感正文，仅存放私有目录，不提交 Git，不打印；metadata 仅来自本批批准适配器，绝不复制旧 user_name/reason/raw_messages。`_edit_payload` 保留现有敏感过滤，而不是因为运维导入就绕过。

### 34.2 新增 `scripts/import_reviewed_memory_v12.py`

```python
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import json
from pathlib import Path

from aiagent.memory.reviewed_import import import_review, load_review
from apps.core.bootstrap import build_runtime


def main() -> None:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    parser.add_argument("--review", type=Path, required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--execute", action="store_true")
    args = parser.parse_args()
    data = load_review(args.review)
    runtime = build_runtime()
    try:
        print(json.dumps(import_review(runtime.long_term_memory, data, execute=args.execute), ensure_ascii=False))
    finally:
        runtime.memory_runner.write_dispatcher.shutdown(wait=True)
        client = getattr(runtime.agent_core.main_runner.llm_runner.checkpointer, "_redis", None)
        if client is not None:
            client.close()


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

输入结构示例，`old_text_sha256` 填私有导出正文实际 UTF-8 SHA-256，不能直接执行占位值：

```json
{
  "schema": 1,
  "review_id": "review-2026-10-28-u1",
  "user_id": "u1",
  "agent_id": "yzl",
  "items": [{
    "old_id": "旧物理记录ID",
    "old_text_sha256": "填写64位实际摘要",
    "approved_text": "用户喜欢无糖茶",
    "category": "preference",
    "importance": "medium"
  }]
}
```

### 34.3 新增 `tests/unit/test_memory_reviewed_import.py`

```python
from __future__ import annotations

import copy
import json

import pytest

from aiagent.memory.approved_write import approved_metadata, verify_approved
from aiagent.memory.published_memory import PublishedLongTermMemory, text_digest
from aiagent.memory.reviewed_import import import_review, load_review
from aiagent.state.execution_ledger import ExecutionLedger, LocalAtomic


class Physical:
    def __init__(self):
        self.rows = {"old": {"id": "old", "memory": "用户喜欢茶；旧问答不应直接恢复",
                             "user_id": "u1", "agent_id": "yzl", "metadata": {}}}
        self.writes = 0

    def _get_physical(self, key):
        return copy.deepcopy(self.rows.get(key))

    def _stage_approved(self, payload, *, visibility_tag):
        verify_approved(payload)
        self.writes += 1
        key = "new-" + str(self.writes)
        self.rows[key] = {"id": key, "memory": payload.text, "user_id": payload.user_id,
                          "agent_id": payload.agent_id,
                          "metadata": {**approved_metadata(payload), "visibility_tag": visibility_tag}}
        return {"physical_id": key}


def fixture():
    ledger = ExecutionLedger(LocalAtomic())
    ledger.memory_control("u1", enabled=True)
    physical = Physical()
    facade = PublishedLongTermMemory(physical, ledger, available=True)
    data = {"schema": 1, "review_id": "r1", "user_id": "u1", "agent_id": "yzl", "items": [
        {"old_id": "old", "old_text_sha256": text_digest(physical.rows["old"]["memory"]),
         "approved_text": "用户喜欢无糖茶", "category": "preference", "importance": "medium"},
    ]}
    return ledger, physical, facade, data


def test_review_dry_run_then_import_and_replay():
    ledger, physical, facade, data = fixture()
    assert import_review(facade, data, execute=False)["dry_run"]
    assert physical.writes == 0 and not ledger.snapshot("u1")["operations"]
    assert import_review(facade, data, execute=True)["imported_count"] == 1
    assert import_review(facade, data, execute=True)["reused_count"] == 1
    assert physical.writes == 1
    rows = facade.list_records("u1")
    assert len(rows) == 1 and rows[0].memory == "用户喜欢无糖茶"
    assert "old" in physical.rows


@pytest.mark.parametrize("fault", ["owner", "digest", "disabled"])
def test_review_wrong_owner_changed_source_and_disabled_do_not_write(fault):
    ledger, physical, facade, data = fixture()
    if fault == "owner":
        physical.rows["old"]["user_id"] = "u2"
    elif fault == "digest":
        physical.rows["old"]["memory"] = "source changed"
    else:
        ledger.memory_control("u1", enabled=False)
    with pytest.raises((ValueError, RuntimeError)):
        import_review(facade, data, execute=True)
    assert physical.writes == 0


def test_review_input_bounds_and_duplicate_keys(tmp_path):
    _, _, _, data = fixture()
    path = tmp_path / "review.json"
    path.write_text(json.dumps(data, ensure_ascii=False), encoding="utf-8")
    assert load_review(path) == data
    path.write_text('{"schema":1,"schema":1}', encoding="utf-8")
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="duplicate_json_key"):
        load_review(path)
```

## 35. 真实 RedisSaver 联合门禁，不用普通 Redis 冒充 checkpoint 通过

### 35.1 新增 `tests/integration/test_execution_redis_saver_joint.py`

文件功能：复用第 30 节真实 Runtime 场景，但将官方 saver 与 ledger 都换成真实 Redis。要求专用测试 Redis **支持 RedisJSON/Search**，不同于核心 Lua 账本只需普通 Redis。无环境变量明确 skip；显式提供服务但不兼容则失败，不能再 skip 掩盖。

```python
from __future__ import annotations

import os
import secrets

import pytest
from langgraph.checkpoint.redis import RedisSaver
from redis import Redis

from aiagent.memory.shared_preferences import SharedMemoryPreferenceStore
from aiagent.state.execution_ledger import ExecutionLedger, RedisAtomic
from tests.smoke.test_execution_joint_runtime import (
    actual_runtime as actual_runtime,
    test_actual_completed_graph_loses_qualification_before_output_commit as check_cancelled,
    test_actual_runtime_voice_memory_checkpoint_share_one_successful_commit as check_success,
)


@pytest.fixture
def redis_runtime(actual_runtime):
    url = os.environ.get("BATCH06_SAVER_REDIS_URL", "")
    if not url:
        pytest.skip("dedicated RedisJSON/Search URL not configured")
    case = actual_runtime
    prefix = "batch06-saver-" + secrets.token_hex(12)
    client = Redis.from_url(url, decode_responses=True, socket_timeout=10, socket_connect_timeout=5)
    saver = RedisSaver(redis_url=url, checkpoint_prefix=prefix + ":checkpoint",
                       checkpoint_write_prefix=prefix + ":write")
    ledger = ExecutionLedger(RedisAtomic(client), prefix=prefix)
    original_saver = case.llm.checkpointer
    try:
        client.ping()
        saver.setup()
        case.llm.checkpointer = saver
        case.llm.graph = case.llm._build_graph()
        case.llm.execution_ledger = ledger
        case.ledger = ledger
        case.runtime.dispatcher.execution_ledger = ledger
        case.facade.ledger = ledger
        case.runtime.memory_runner.preference_store = SharedMemoryPreferenceStore(ledger)
        case.runtime.memory_runner.preference_store.set(user_id="joint-user", long_term_enabled=True)
        yield case
    finally:
        # Every index uses this random namespace; never FLUSHDB, scan-delete or delete production prefixes.
        try:
            for index in (saver.checkpoints_index, saver.checkpoint_writes_index):
                if index.exists():
                    index.delete(drop=True)
            for user in ("joint-user", "other-user"):
                client.delete(ledger.key(user))
        finally:
            saver._redis.close()
            client.close()
            case.llm.checkpointer = original_saver


def test_actual_success_with_official_redis_saver(redis_runtime, monkeypatch):
    check_success(redis_runtime, monkeypatch)


def test_actual_cancel_with_official_redis_saver(redis_runtime, monkeypatch):
    check_cancelled(redis_runtime, monkeypatch)
```

实施前定位 `LLMRunner._build_graph()`：当前源码它返回已经 `compile(checkpointer=self.checkpointer)` 的图，因此本例重新赋值 `graph`。不要再对返回值重复 `.compile()`。这份用例复用的是实际主图场景，不是把 saver 换成字典；Memory 物理层和 Provider 仍是可控替身，真实 Qdrant/真实模型必须单独提供证据。真实 Redis 双进程竞争另见第 5/22.2/26.3 节，不把单进程 saver 测试称为跨进程整链通过。

清理只针对此用例随机前缀索引及已知主体 key；支持 RedisJSON/Search 的专用实例即使测试中途失败也应保留日志。运行者须确认变量绝不指向生产实例。

### 35.2 新增 `tests/integration/test_memory_qdrant_visibility.py`

文件功能：验证本机锁定的 Mem0/Qdrant **真实适配器和真实服务**执行 MatchAny/tag/owner 过滤。仅 embedding 和词法处理采用确定性替身，不发送真实 Provider；不能把它报告为真实嵌入语义质量通过。当前部署的 Qdrant 若不兼容 Mem0 的 keyword/search 接口必须失败，不降级为空结果冒充隔离成功。

```python
from __future__ import annotations

import os
import secrets
from threading import RLock

import pytest
from qdrant_client import QdrantClient

from aiagent.memory.mem0_memory import Mem0LongTermMemory, VisibilityScopedMem0
from aiagent.memory.published_memory import PublishedLongTermMemory
from aiagent.schemas.memory import MemoryCategory, MemoryImportance
from aiagent.state.execution_context import bind, call
from aiagent.state.execution_ledger import ExecutionLedger, LocalAtomic, digest


def test_real_qdrant_hides_unpublished_and_foreign_records(monkeypatch, tmp_path):
    url = os.environ.get("BATCH06_QDRANT_URL", "")
    if not url:
        pytest.skip("dedicated BATCH06_QDRANT_URL not configured")
    import mem0.memory.main as sdk_module

    # This gate is about actual backend filtering, not English NLP quality or model downloads.
    monkeypatch.setattr(sdk_module, "lemmatize_for_bm25", lambda text: text)
    monkeypatch.setattr(sdk_module, "extract_entities", lambda text: [])
    name = "batch06_visibility_" + secrets.token_hex(12)
    client = QdrantClient(url=url, api_key=os.environ.get("BATCH06_QDRANT_API_KEY") or None,
                          timeout=10, prefer_grpc=False)
    sdk = None
    try:
        sdk = VisibilityScopedMem0.from_config({
            "version": "v1.1", "history_db_path": str(tmp_path / "history.sqlite"),
            "llm": {"provider": "openai", "config": {"model": "gpt-4o-mini", "api_key": "synthetic-unused"}},
            "embedder": {"provider": "openai", "config": {"model": "text-embedding-3-small",
                                                            "api_key": "synthetic-unused"}},
            "vector_store": {"provider": "qdrant", "config": {
                "collection_name": name, "embedding_model_dims": 2, "client": client,
            }},
        })

        class Embedding:
            def embed(self, text, memory_action=None):
                return call("embedding", lambda timeout: [1.0, 0.0], per_call_seconds=5)

        sdk.embedding_model = Embedding()
        physical = object.__new__(Mem0LongTermMemory)
        physical.memory, physical.enable_graph, physical._lock = sdk, False, RLock()
        ledger = ExecutionLedger(LocalAtomic())
        facade = PublishedLongTermMemory(physical, ledger, available=True)
        for user in ("u1", "u2"):
            ledger.memory_control(user, enabled=True)

        for user, key, publish in (("u1", "visible", True), ("u1", "hidden", False), ("u2", "foreign", True)):
            payload = facade._edit_payload("用户喜欢无糖茶", MemoryCategory.PREFERENCE,
                                           MemoryImportance.MEDIUM, "user_edit", user, "yzl")
            cap = ledger.begin(user_id=user, session_id=key, idempotency_key=key,
                                payload_digest=digest(key)).capability
            ledger.start(cap)
            with bind(ledger, cap) as frame:
                facade.add_approved(payload)
                if publish:
                    ledger.finish(cap, result={"ok": True}, memories=frame.staged_memories)
                else:
                    ledger.cancel_operation(user, cap.operation_id)
        # All three physically exist, while only one is visible to this subject.
        assert client.count(name, exact=True).count == 3
        cap = ledger.begin(user_id="u1", session_id="search", idempotency_key="search",
                            payload_digest=digest("query")).capability
        ledger.start(cap)
        with bind(ledger, cap):
            hits = facade.search("u1", "用户喜欢无糖茶", limit=10)
            assert len(hits) == 1 and hits[0].memory == "用户喜欢无糖茶"
            ledger.finish(cap, result={"ok": True})
        facade.delete_memory(hits[0].id, "u1")
        assert facade.list_records("u1") == []
        assert len(facade.list_records("u2")) == 1
        assert client.count(name, exact=True).count == 3
    finally:
        try:
            if client.collection_exists(name):
                client.delete_collection(name)
        finally:
            if sdk is not None:
                sdk.db.close()
            client.close()
```

专用服务仅允许此随机 collection 的创建/删除；若配置开启 Mem0 遥测，先按第 33 节在进程启动前关闭。测试结束显式调用当前 SDK 的 history `close()`，避免 Windows 临时目录被占用；本用例不触碰真实用户 collection，也不等于完成物理删除 runbook。

## 36. 维护边界与知识 HTTP 的单元、回归和对照测试

### 36.1 新增 `tests/unit/test_execution_rag_maintenance.py`

文件功能：正常有界构建和取消后的制品不发布使用同一入口；哈希不符在 FAISS 反序列化前阻断；旧 Worker 任务进入现有死信而非成功/自动重跑。测试不下载模型、不调用 Provider、不连接 Redis。

```python
from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio
from types import SimpleNamespace
from unittest.mock import AsyncMock

import pytest

from aiagent.knowledge.rag_pipeline import RAGPipeline
from aiagent.knowledge.runtime_factory import ARTIFACT_FILES, verify_artifact
from aiagent.state.execution_context import call, current
from aiagent.state.execution_ledger import ExecutionLedger, ExecutionRejected, LocalAtomic
from scripts import build_rag_artifact_v12 as module


@pytest.mark.parametrize("cancel", [False, True])
def test_artifact_build_success_vs_revoked_commit(monkeypatch, tmp_path, cancel):
    ledger = ExecutionLedger(LocalAtomic())
    source, destination = tmp_path / "source", tmp_path / "artifact"
    source.mkdir()
    (source / "facts.txt").write_text("合成知识", encoding="utf-8")

    def factory(config, *, artifact_dir, knowledge_dir, read_only):
        assert read_only is False and knowledge_dir != source
        class Pipeline:
            def build_index(self, force_rebuild):
                call("rag_embedding", lambda timeout: [[0.1, 0.2]], per_call_seconds=5)
                for name in ARTIFACT_FILES:
                    path = artifact_dir / name
                    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
                    path.write_text("synthetic fixture, never load as FAISS", encoding="utf-8")
                if cancel:
                    frame = current(required=True)
                    ledger.cancel_operation(frame.capability.user_id, frame.capability.operation_id)
        return Pipeline()

    monkeypatch.setattr(module, "create_pipeline", factory)
    if cancel:
        with pytest.raises(ExecutionRejected):
            module.build(source, destination, ledger)
        assert not destination.exists()
    else:
        result = module.build(source, destination, ledger)
        assert result["activated"] is False
        assert verify_artifact(destination, result["artifact_sha256"])["schema"] == 1
        (destination / "faiss" / "index.pkl").write_bytes(b"tampered")
        with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="file_digest_mismatch"):
            verify_artifact(destination, result["artifact_sha256"])
        with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="new_separate_destination"):
            module.build(source, destination, ledger)
    record = next(iter(ledger.snapshot("maintenance-rag-v12")["operations"].values()))
    assert record["calls"] == {"rag_embedding": 1}
    assert record["status"] == ("cancelled" if cancel else "completed")


def test_readonly_pipeline_never_builds_on_cache_miss(tmp_path):
    pipeline = object.__new__(RAGPipeline)
    pipeline.read_only = True
    pipeline.docs_index_path = tmp_path / "missing.json"
    pipeline.faiss_dir = tmp_path / "missing-faiss"
    pipeline.documents = []
    with pytest.raises(RuntimeError, match="read_only_artifact_required"):
        pipeline.build_index(force_rebuild=False)
    with pytest.raises(RuntimeError, match="read_only_artifact_missing"):
        pipeline.ensure_ready()
    with pytest.raises(RuntimeError, match="online_rebuild_disabled"):
        pipeline.rebuild_async()


def test_maintenance_input_budget_no_silent_truncation(tmp_path):
    source, target = tmp_path / "source", tmp_path / "snapshot"
    source.mkdir()
    target.mkdir()
    (source / "large.txt").write_bytes(b"x" * (64 * 1024 + 1))
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="source_budget_exceeded"):
        module.snapshot_sources(source, target)


def test_legacy_worker_knowledge_task_deadletter_without_start_or_retry(monkeypatch):
    from apps.worker import main as worker

    queue = SimpleNamespace(dead_letter=AsyncMock(), start=AsyncMock(), finish=AsyncMock(),
                            fail_or_retry=AsyncMock())
    monkeypatch.setattr(worker, "queue", queue)
    asyncio.run(worker._run_task({"id": "legacy-task", "type": "knowledge.rebuild"}, "worker"))
    queue.dead_letter.assert_awaited_once_with(
        "legacy-task", "knowledge_online_rebuild_disabled", reason="maintenance_requires_reviewed_offline_build",
    )
    queue.start.assert_not_awaited()
    queue.finish.assert_not_awaited()
    queue.fail_or_retry.assert_not_awaited()
```

### 36.2 新增 `tests/api/test_execution_knowledge_contract.py`

文件功能：直接验证实际 knowledge 路由和 ExecutionRoute，正常检索计数且重复不再次调用；在线重建 503 且不触及 Runtime。此测试将身份层替换为固定测试主体，仅测此路由接线，真实 Bearer/越权仍由第五批和第 26 节验证，不混称全权限测试。

```python
from __future__ import annotations

from types import SimpleNamespace
from unittest.mock import Mock

import pytest
from fastapi import FastAPI
from fastapi.testclient import TestClient

from aiagent.state.execution_context import call
from aiagent.state.execution_ledger import ExecutionLedger, LocalAtomic
from apps.api import execution_route
from apps.api.routes import knowledge
from cloud.admin_auth import require_cloud_admin


@pytest.fixture
def api(monkeypatch):
    ledger = ExecutionLedger(LocalAtomic())
    calls = []

    async def identity(request):
        request.state.principal = SimpleNamespace(user_id="knowledge-test")

    def inspect(query, top_k):
        calls.append(query)
        call("rag_embedding", lambda timeout: [[0.1, 0.2]], per_call_seconds=5)
        return {"query": query, "should_inject": False, "chunks": []}

    config = SimpleNamespace(model_dump=lambda **kwargs: {})
    runtime = SimpleNamespace(
        dispatcher=SimpleNamespace(execution_ledger=ledger,
            persona_manager=SimpleNamespace(get_active_persona=lambda: SimpleNamespace(config=config))),
        inspect_knowledge=inspect,
    )
    monkeypatch.setattr(execution_route, "enforce_http_policy", identity)
    monkeypatch.setattr(execution_route, "get_runtime", lambda: runtime)
    monkeypatch.setattr(knowledge, "get_runtime", lambda: runtime)
    app = FastAPI()
    app.dependency_overrides[require_cloud_admin] = lambda: None
    app.include_router(knowledge.router)
    with TestClient(app) as client:
        yield client, ledger, calls


def test_knowledge_search_budget_and_same_payload_replay(api):
    client, ledger, calls = api
    headers = {"Idempotency-Key": "knowledge-query"}
    first = client.post("/knowledge/search", json={"query": "合成问题"}, headers=headers)
    assert first.status_code == 200 and first.json()["ok"] is True
    replay = client.post("/knowledge/search", json={"query": "合成问题"}, headers=headers)
    assert replay.status_code == 200 and replay.json() == first.json()
    assert calls == ["合成问题"]
    record = ledger.operation("knowledge-test", first.headers["x-operation-id"])
    assert record["calls"] == {"rag_embedding": 1} and record["status"] == "completed"
    conflict = client.post("/knowledge/search", json={"query": "载荷已变"}, headers=headers)
    assert conflict.status_code == 409 and len(calls) == 1


def test_online_rebuild_is_explicitly_unavailable(api, monkeypatch):
    client, _, _ = api
    get_runtime = Mock(side_effect=AssertionError("must not construct runtime or enqueue"))
    monkeypatch.setattr(knowledge, "get_runtime", get_runtime)
    response = client.post("/knowledge/rebuild", json={"force_rebuild": True, "async_rebuild": True})
    assert response.status_code == 503
    assert response.json()["detail"]["code"] == "knowledge_online_rebuild_disabled"
    get_runtime.assert_not_called()
```

## 37. 统一实施顺序、验收与回滚

### 37.1 实施顺序

1. 先核对第四/第五批实际实施情况与未提交代码，尤其是 `approved_write.py`、可信主体、私有资源、共享异常重抛。不要仅因存在文档就跳过依赖验收。
2. 停止当前实例写流量及旧 Memory 线程池/旧 knowledge Worker，记录代码、配置、依赖锁、Redis/Qdrant/制品的私有备份版本。禁止新旧实例同时读写同一账本 namespace。
3. 按第 2—8 节实施账本、执行上下文、Route、Dispatcher、官方 saver 分代；按第 9—15 节实施私有输出和实际发送点预算。按第 25/26 节替换 Voice，不能保留旧无 CAS 的 save API。
4. 按第 16—22 节实施唯一 Memory facade、共享偏好、暂存和撤销；第 27—28/31 节同步迁移组件/真实图评测入口。直接调用 LLM/RAG/ASR 的旧单测需显式 bind 测试资格，不准生产加“无 frame 放行”。
5. 按第 32/33 节同步只读索引、受控构建、配置、离线模型；有真实在线重建需求的部署必须先接受维护模式变化。云端和本机只改变连接地址/路径，不改变 fail-closed、认证和 CORS 原则。
6. 先 dry-run 共享偏好迁移，审阅后 execute。必要的旧记忆按第 34 节人工重审；未重审记录保持隔离，不以备份恢复直接认领。
7. 用户执行下列分层测试并保存真实结果；真实 Redis、RedisSaver、Qdrant、Provider、容器失败必须保留，未配置而 skip 不计入通过。
8. 独立环境采前后基线，确认没有把拒绝/取消变多当作时延优化。完成证据审阅后再逐步放量；第七批开始输出消费/主动性协议，不反向抢改本批。

### 37.2 账本、TTL 与容量

| 对象 | 存储与可见性 | 本批边界 |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 每主体执行 root | `{prefix}:v12:execution:{主体SHA256}`，单 key CAS | schema=1，带随机 epoch；不设置整 key TTL，防止失效标记丢失后旧资格复活 |
| operation/result | root 内，24h 幂等保留字段 | 字段到期不是自动物理删除；本批最多 256 个，满额拒绝，第九批有界回收 |
| session/history | root 内，单会话最多 6 对历史 | 最多 128 会话，clear/Memory 撤销清可见指针；不是清所有用户进程状态 |
| Voice call | 同一 root 的 calls | 最多 64 个；Redis 时间判断过期，轮询不续期；本批不清已过期槽位 |
| checkpoint | 官方 saver 的 op12 物理命名空间 | 最终 CAS 才发布当前指针；旧物理数据可能仍在，第九批负责清理 |
| Memory | Qdrant 物理 tag + root 可见引用 | 默认禁用，最多 1000 引用/128 暂存/2048 清理登记；禁用不等于删除 |
| 输出/音频 | root 中 owner 绑定的可见描述符 + 私有文件 | 生成不等于播放；过期/撤销后无下载资格，已下载数据不可撤回 |
| 索引制品 | 受信构建机生成、固定摘要、只读挂载 | 手动审阅发布，不热覆盖；在线 rebuild 明确关闭 |

第九批完成容量回收前，本批适合有限流量验证而非无限期高流量生产。**达到阈值必须明确报错，不能删整 key、忽略解码错误、换 prefix 或切内存来恢复服务。** Redis 启用持久化/备份，避免任意淘汰执行账本；恢复旧快照会恢复旧状态，必须先停流量、审阅撤销/tombstone 和迁移，不能宣称 Redis 持久化天然满足删除恢复安全。

配置文档手动同步位置：`docs/config-reference.md` 的 Memory/RAG/执行状态说明，加入下表内容；本次不直接修改该文件。原 `.env.example` 的 `MEMORY_LONG_TERM_DEFAULT_ENABLED`、`MEMORY_PREFERENCES_PATH` 注释同步标为“旧配置迁移兼容，不再决定在线主体偏好”，不删除旧私有文件或丢失迁移来源。

| 配置 | 第六批语义 |
| --- | --- |
| `EXECUTION_STATE_MODE=local` | 仅单进程开发；运行在本机并不强制 local，可在本机使用 Redis |
| `EXECUTION_STATE_MODE=redis` + `REDIS_URL` | API/Worker/维护命令连接同一共享状态；空 URL/断连拒绝，不切内存 |
| `REDIS_PREFIX` | 同版实例一致；更换不能当成清理/重试方案；迁移 namespace 需停写和审阅 |
| Voice provider/fail-open | provider 跟随 ledger；`fail_open=false`，旧 lock TTL 不再承担 fencing 保证 |
| `RAG_ARTIFACT_DIR` + `RAG_ARTIFACT_SHA256` | 运维批准的目录及 artifact.json 摘要，同时变更、只读挂载、重启生效 |
| `RAG_AUTO_REBUILD_ON_STALE` | Runtime 中强制不自动重建；不能用 true 绕过维护模式 |
| `MEMORY_LONG_TERM_DEFAULT_ENABLED` | 不再使新主体自动启用；主体默认 false，以共享偏好为真源 |
| `MEMORY_PREFERENCES_PATH` | 仅旧文件迁移来源，不是 API/Worker 的在线状态 |
| `MEM0_TELEMETRY` / `ANONYMIZED_TELEMETRY` | 启动环境 false，必须早于 SDK import |
| `HF_HUB_OFFLINE` / `TRANSFORMERS_OFFLINE` | 启动环境 1，预备本地模型；不能替代网络出口控制 |
| `ASR_MODEL_PATH` | 本地 ASR 必须预备模型目录；缺失真实能力失败，不隐式改 Mock |
| 120 秒/24 调用及阶段配额 | 第 3 节固定服务端策略，不接受用户 body 覆盖；不是测量结果 |

`.env` 不新增本地/云端两套互相覆盖的配置。CORS 沿用现有明确 origin 白名单，并按第 11.2 节放行/暴露幂等与执行关联 Header；CORS 不是主体认证，不能用 `*` 加凭据解决测试问题。

### 37.3 最终验证指令，仅提供，未执行

以下指令以 Windows 项目根目录为基准。先按照本文手动创建所有代码/测试，再执行；不直接复制到尚未实施的业务目录。已配置专用环境变量的真实集成测试可能实际写入并清理自己的随机 namespace。

```powershell
# 1. 依赖与静态检查。缺 Ruff 时由 uv 同步项目既有开发依赖，不全局 pip 随意安装。
uv sync --locked --extra dev
.\.venv\Scripts\python.exe -m ruff check aiagent apps cloud config scripts tests

# 2. 本批单元/组件对照
.\.venv\Scripts\python.exe -m pytest tests/unit/test_execution_ledger.py tests/unit/test_execution_output_commit.py tests/unit/test_budgeted_http.py tests/unit/test_llm_execution_budget.py tests/unit/test_published_memory.py tests/unit/test_memory_physical_contract.py tests/unit/test_memory_embedding_budget.py tests/unit/test_shared_memory_preferences.py tests/unit/test_voice_execution.py tests/unit/test_rag_asr_execution_budget.py tests/unit/test_evaluation_execution_scope.py tests/unit/test_execution_benchmark_contract.py tests/unit/test_memory_offline_dependencies.py tests/unit/test_memory_reviewed_import.py tests/unit/test_execution_rag_maintenance.py -q

.\.venv\Scripts\python.exe -m pytest tests/unit/test_execution_runtime_registry.py -q

# 3. 实际 HTTP contract、回归和真实主图离线 smoke
.\.venv\Scripts\python.exe -m pytest tests/api/test_execution_resources_contract.py tests/api/test_execution_admission_contract.py tests/api/test_execution_memory_contract.py tests/api/test_execution_voice_contract.py tests/api/test_execution_knowledge_contract.py tests/smoke/test_execution_joint_runtime.py tests/smoke/test_execution_graph_evaluation.py -q

# 4. 原有基础回归。可能暴露旧测试缺执行资格，逐项迁移，不使用 production fallback 适配测试。
.\.venv\Scripts\python.exe -m pytest tests/unit/test_llm_thread_deletion.py tests/unit/test_llm_thread_session_scope.py tests/unit/test_execution_state_config.py tests/unit/test_rag_embedding_response.py tests/unit/test_memory_intake.py tests/unit/test_memory_deduper.py tests/unit/test_evaluation_contracts.py tests/unit/test_evaluation_contrast.py tests/smoke/test_mock_runtime_smoke.py -q

# 5. 专用普通 Redis：BATCH06_REDIS_URL 必须在本地填写，不在聊天或 Git 中存真实凭据。
# BATCH06_SAVER_REDIS_URL 必须另指向支持 RedisJSON/Search 的专用实例。
.\.venv\Scripts\python.exe -m pytest tests/integration/test_execution_ledger_redis.py tests/integration/test_memory_visibility_redis.py tests/integration/test_voice_execution_redis.py tests/integration/test_execution_redis_saver_joint.py -q -rs
# 真实 Qdrant：专用 BATCH06_QDRANT_URL，凭据仅本地 BATCH06_QDRANT_API_KEY。
.\.venv\Scripts\python.exe -m pytest tests/integration/test_memory_qdrant_visibility.py -q -rs

# 6. 全后端回归。不要只保留本批新测试的绿色结果。
.\.venv\Scripts\python.exe -m pytest tests/unit tests/api tests/smoke -q
```

第 14 节实际文件名需与命令保持一致：它交付的是 `tests/api/test_execution_admission_contract.py`；若用户已手写为其它名字，只改命令/定位，不重复创建同一套测试。第 5 节的环境变量/子进程继承规则以该节代码为准，不因后续用例写了别的变量就误用生产地址。

运维命令只提供模板，不执行；先准备批准依赖、合成/审阅数据和私有目录，不把 `<...>` 当真实路径：

```powershell
# 索引：使用全新输出目录，成功只表示构建提交，不自动激活。
.\.venv\Scripts\python.exe -m scripts.build_rag_artifact_v12 --source F:/approved-knowledge --destination F:/approved-artifacts/rag-v12-06 --execute

# 偏好迁移：第 19.3 节给出脚本完整 CLI，先按该节 dry-run，再审核 execute。

.\.venv\Scripts\python.exe -m scripts.import_reviewed_memory_v12 --review F:/private-migration/review-u1.json
.\.venv\Scripts\python.exe -m scripts.import_reviewed_memory_v12 --review F:/private-migration/review-u1.json --execute

# 性能：第 29 节脚本需要显式配置摘要和专用 token，见该节完整参数。
```
